**Email-1**  
To: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Subject: JD-SHN-09 四半期レビュー 会場変更と事前資料のお願い  
Date: December 08, 2009, 10:20  
References:  
Body:  
アドゥバ＝ライス様

平素より大変お世話になっております。東雲電機株式会社 知的財産部の海田でございます。

来年2月に予定しております共同開発の四半期レビューにつきまして、社内調整の結果、下記のとおり変更させていただきたく存じます。

日程：2010年2月17日（水）10時00分から17時00分まで
会場：当社大阪本社 12階 第3会議室（当初ご案内の茨木事業所から変更）
出席予定：当社6名（知的財産部2名、音響技術部4名）

会場変更の理由は、茨木の試聴室が同じ週に空調設備の改修工事に入るためでございます。試聴を伴う議題は次回に繰り延べさせていただき、今回は資料ベースでの討議とさせていただければ幸いです。

事前資料につきまして、恐れ入りますが1月29日（金）までにご送付いただけますでしょうか。当社側は社内翻訳の手配がございますため、二週間ほどの余裕を頂戴できますと助かります。前回の予備打合せでは前日夜に資料が届き、当日の議論が資料の読み合わせに終始してしまいました。

宿泊につきましては新大阪駅前のホテルに5室を仮押さえしております。ご不要の場合は1月15日までにお知らせください。

なお、当日の議事録は従前どおり当社が起案し、両社でご確認いただく形とさせていただきます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-2**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Hanyoung Precision - JDA draft 6 - Annex A as it stands after the Ansan session  
Date: December 09, 2009, 16:20  
References:  
Body:  
Roswell,

Draft 6 came back from Hanyoung Precision Co., Ltd. last night. The commercial terms are yours; I am flagging the two clauses that will outlive all of us.

Annex A is now a single page listing four work packages, and all four of them are mechanical: H-1 chassis and magnet-assembly integration, H-2 transducer characterization, H-3 thermal test fixture design, H-4 production-line acceptance criteria. There is no signal-processing package and Hanyoung did not ask for one. Annex A is the definition of the field of the joint development and nothing else. It is not a list of patents and it is not a list of products.

Article 5 is the ownership clause and it is drafted against Annex A. Article 5.1: inventions arising within the Annex A field are jointly owned in equal shares. Article 5.2: inventions arising outside that field remain the sole property of the party whose personnel made them, whether or not the subject was raised at a program meeting. That is the same architecture as two other agreements in the portfolio, so please do not let the business treat the Annex A boundary as a formality when they are choosing which engineers sit in which review.

The term ends March 31, 2014. Ask them to say so in Article 3 rather than by reference to the work plan.

One consequence for licensing. Korean Patent Act Article 99 mirrors the Japanese rule: a co-owner may work the invention itself, but a license to a third party needs the consent of every co-owner. If anything commercially important lands inside Annex A we will be asking Ansan for permission for the next twenty years.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-3**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Executed - Hanyoung Precision joint development agreement - program code JD-HYP-10 - read Annex A before you docket anything  
Date: January 15, 2010, 09:05  
References: Email-2  
Body:  
All,

The joint development agreement with Hanyoung Precision Co., Ltd. of Ansan was executed yesterday, January 14. English governs; there is a Korean translation for their internal use only. Program code JD-HYP-10 has been assigned.

The final Annex A is the version dated January 8 and it carries the four work packages unchanged from draft 6. Ingrid has put a PDF of Annex A in the agreement matter. Nothing else is Annex A. If someone hands you a slide deck of program topics, that is not Annex A.

Why this matters operationally. Under Article 5.1 an invention arising within the Annex A field is jointly owned in equal shares and must be filed in both names; Korean Patent Act Article 44 requires a jointly owned right to be applied for jointly, and a defective filing is attackable later. Under Article 5.2 an invention arising outside that field is the sole property of the party whose personnel made it. So the first question on every disclosure that comes out of Ansan is not "was this discussed with Hanyoung," it is "does it fall inside one of H-1 to H-4."

Put JD-HYP-10 on the agreement matter and on cost bookings. Do not put it on an invention matter unless you have actually made the Annex A determination and written it down. A three-letter code on a matter is a fact about accounting and it will be read fifteen years from now as if it were a fact about title.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-4**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Provisional 61/311,904 filed - MAL-2010-0392 (San Mateo series) - adaptive microphone-array beamforming  
Date: March 10, 2010, 09:05  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

The provisional went in yesterday afternoon, March 9. Application number 61/311,904. The filing receipt is in the matter file. Whitcomb Reyes prepared it from the disclosure form and the two lab memos, and it runs to 34 pages with nine figures, which is more than we usually put into a provisional and which I think is right here.

Matter details as opened in PALLAS:

Docket: MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series
Title: Adaptive microphone-array beamforming for conference endpoints
Inventors: Hyacinth Delacroix, Bjørn Umbertsen, both San Mateo, Acoustic Systems group
Priority date: March 9, 2010
Convention and PCT deadline: March 9, 2011

Two fields I want confirmed before I close the record.

Ownership is sole, Marsden only. There is no joint development program behind this one and no program code to attach. The work sits entirely inside the endpoint hardware group's own roadmap and both inventors are our employees. I have left the program code field empty rather than putting a placeholder in it, because a placeholder in that field has a way of turning into a fact.

Second, the inventor declarations are signed but Umbertsen's assignment still needs recording. I will send it to him with the PCT instructions rather than chase him twice.

The acoustics group has asked whether we want a second provisional in the fall covering the microphone geometry variants. My view is that we should, and that it should sit on this matter rather than open a new one, but that is your call and not mine.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-5**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Verrocchio mutual NDA - Annex A permitted recipients - two additions requested  
Date: June 17, 2010, 11:40  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

The mutual non-disclosure agreement with Verrocchio Semiconductors, Inc. was executed on June 2. Three-year term, five-year survival on confidentiality, California law.

The point I need a decision on is Annex A. This NDA is a named-recipient NDA, not a need-to-know NDA. Annex A lists eleven individuals by name and title, five of ours and six of theirs, and Section 4.2 says disclosure to anyone not listed in Annex A is a breach even if that person is an employee of the receiving party. There is no "affiliates" language and no automatic substitution when someone changes role.

Systems engineering wants to bring two more people into the technical exchange starting the week of July 12. Under Section 4.3 an addition to Annex A requires a signed amendment countersigned by both parties, which Verrocchio's counsel says they can turn around in about ten days. That means I need the names now, not on July 9.

I would also like your view on whether we ask them to convert Annex A into a role-based list at the first amendment. Named lists are clean for audit and miserable in practice, and we have already had one person read a Verrocchio document while not on the list, which I have logged.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-6**  
To: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Subject: 適合性試験ベクタ 第2次配布のご案内（JD-SHN-09）  
Date: October 06, 2010, 15:40  
References:  
Body:  
アドゥバ＝ライス様

東雲電機の海田でございます。いつもお世話になっております。

適合性試験ベクタの第2次配布を本日実施いたしましたので、ご連絡申し上げます。

配布点数：24シーケンス（前回18シーケンスに6本を追加）
形式：24ビット／48kHz および 96kHz の両系列
容量：合計 41.2 GB
受渡方法：当社SFTPサーバ。アカウント情報は本メールとは別便でお送りいたします。
公開期限：2010年12月15日

追加分6本は、いずれも前回の定例会で御社側からご指摘のございました低ビットレート域での挙動を確認するためのものでございます。うち2本は当社の無響室で新規に収録したものでして、収録条件を記した仕様書を同梱しております。

ハッシュ一覧（SHA-256）も同梱しておりますので、展開後に必ずご照合をお願いいたします。前回の配布時に転送途中で1本が破損しており、そのまま二週間気づかれないままとなっておりましたので、念のため申し添えます。

利用条件につきまして、書面で改めて申し上げます。本ベクタは共同開発の作業目的に限りご使用いただけるものでございまして、第三者への再配布、および共同開発以外の製品評価への転用はご遠慮願います。この点は契約書とは別に、両社の実務として従前より確認してまいったところでございます。

ご不明の点がございましたら当方までご連絡ください。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-7**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: IDF-2010-1141 (speaker protection / thermal modeling) - clearance against the Hanyoung Annex A  
Date: November 16, 2010, 09:20  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

IDF-2010-1141 came in from Léopold Sanschagrin and Meret Aalbersberg on November 5. Title on the form is "Speaker protection using thermal modeling." The substance is a voice-coil temperature estimator running in the DSP that pulls back the excursion limiter before the coil reaches its thermal limit, using a two-pole thermal model identified at production test.

The clearance question is JD-HYP-10. Both inventors spent Q2 and Q3 in Ansan on the Hanyoung Precision program, and the estimator was demonstrated on a Hanyoung transducer at the June 24 review. That is enough factual overlap that I do not want to file in our sole name on my own judgment.

My reading is that we are outside Annex A. Annex A of the January 14, 2010 agreement lists four work packages, H-1 through H-4, and all four are mechanical: chassis and magnet-assembly integration, transducer characterization, thermal test fixture design, and production-line acceptance criteria. There is no signal-processing package. Article 5.2 leaves an invention arising outside the Annex A scope with the party whose personnel made it, and Article 9 treats background technology shown at reviews as disclosed for information rather than contributed to the program.

I would like a written confirmation from Hanyoung before we spend anything on drafting. Bang-byeonrisa at Hanul can put the request in the right register. Do you agree?

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-8**  
To: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
From: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
Cc: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: JD-HYP-10 관련 발명(IDF-2010-1141)의 권리 귀속 확인 요청의 건  
Date: December 09, 2010, 15:40  
References:  
Body:  
한영정밀 주식회사
지식재산부장 김도윤 부장님

안녕하십니까. 한울국제특허법률사무소 변리사 방지훈입니다. 마스든 오디오 래버러토리즈(Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.)를 대리하여 연락드립니다.

다름이 아니오라 2010년 1월 14일자로 양사가 체결한 공동개발계약(프로그램 코드 JD-HYP-10)과 관련하여 한 가지 확인을 요청드리고자 합니다.

의뢰인 사내 발명신고 IDF-2010-1141, 발명의 명칭 "열 모델링을 이용한 스피커 보호"는 보이스코일의 온도를 DSP에서 추정하여 변위 제한기의 이득을 사전에 낮추는 신호처리 기술입니다. 발명자는 마스든 종업원 2명(Léopold Sanschagrin, Meret Aalbersberg)이며, 2010년 6월 24일 정례 검토회에서 귀사 트랜스듀서를 사용하여 시연된 바 있습니다.

의뢰인의 검토로는 본건 발명이 공동개발계약 별지 A에 열거된 작업 패키지 H-1 내지 H-4(기구 통합, 트랜스듀서 특성 측정, 열시험 지그, 양산 합격기준)의 어느 것에도 해당하지 아니하며, 계약 제5조 제2항에 따라 공동 소유의 대상이 아니라고 판단하고 있습니다. 검토회에서의 시연은 제9조에 따른 배경기술 정보의 제공에 해당한다고 봅니다.

의뢰인은 본건에 관하여 단독 명의로 특허출원을 준비하고 있으며, 출원 전에 귀사의 서면 확인을 받아 두고자 합니다. 확인서 초안을 별도로 송부드리겠사오니 검토하여 주시기 바랍니다. 회신은 2011년 2월 말까지 주시면 일정에 무리가 없겠습니다.

바쁘신 중에 번거로우시겠지만 잘 부탁드립니다.

방지훈
변리사
한울국제특허법률사무소

**Email-9**  
To: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
From: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Subject: RE: JD-HYP-10 관련 발명(IDF-2010-1141)의 권리 귀속 확인 요청의 건  
Date: February 17, 2011, 10:05  
References: Email-8  
Body:  
방지훈 변리사님

한영정밀 지식재산팀 오세라입니다. 회신이 늦어져 송구합니다.

보내주신 확인서 초안은 지식재산팀 검토를 마치고 현재 법무팀에 회람 중입니다. 진행 상황을 말씀드리면 다음과 같습니다.

첫째, 별지 A 해당 여부에 관하여는 개발본부 김성환 수석의 의견을 받았으며, 본건 신호처리 기술이 H-1 내지 H-4 어디에도 포함되지 아니한다는 점에는 이견이 없습니다.

둘째, 2010년 6월 24일 검토회 건에 대하여도 당사 참석자 확인을 마쳤습니다. 당시 시연은 귀 의뢰인이 준비한 소프트웨어를 당사 시료에 적용한 것으로, 당사 인력의 기술적 기여는 시료 제공과 측정 지원에 그쳤다는 것이 참석자들의 일치된 기억입니다.

셋째, 다만 법무팀에서 확인서의 효력 범위에 관하여 문안 수정 의견을 낼 가능성이 있습니다. 당사는 계약 기간 중 발생하는 사안에 대하여만 사전 확인을 부여하는 것을 원칙으로 하고 있어, 그 취지가 반영될 수 있습니다.

본건은 3월 하순 지식재산위원회에 상정할 예정이며, 위원회 의결 후 4월 중 회신드릴 수 있을 것으로 봅니다. 당초 말씀하신 2월 말 일정에 맞추지 못하여 죄송합니다.

오세라
지식재산팀 대리
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-10**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 確認書のご送付（貴社整理番号 MAL-2009-0271「ダイアログ強調のためのメタデータ伝送」）  
Date: February 22, 2011, 11:15  
References:  
Body:  
都倉先生

いつもお世話になっております。東雲電機の海田でございます。

昨年12月にご依頼のございました確認書につきまして、本日付で社内決裁が下りましたので、原本を本日発送いたしました。到着まで二日ほど頂戴するかと存じます。以下に本文を写しておきます。

　　確認書
　　当社は、マースデン・オーディオ・ラボラトリーズ社の整理番号 MAL-2009-0271、発明の名称「ダイアログ強調のためのメタデータ伝送」に係る発明およびこれに基づく日本国出願について、日本国内において、共同開発契約に基づく共有持分その他一切の権利を主張しないことを確認いたします。
　　2011年2月22日
　　東雲電機株式会社 知的財産部長 海田 隆志

一点、ご説明を申し上げます。ご送付いただいた案文には地域の限定がございませんでしたが、社内で検討いたしました結果、「日本国内において」の文言を加えさせていただきました。理由は二つでございます。

第一に、本件の日本国出願につきましては、当社技術者の関与が形式的なものにとどまることを当方でも確認できました。しかしながら欧州および米国の対応出願につきましては、当社のミュンヘン駐在員が2009年秋の技術検討会に加わっており、その関与の程度について事実確認がまだ済んでおりません。

第二に、確認できていない範囲まで含めて一括して権利を放棄したと読まれかねない文言に署名することは差し控えたい、というのが当社法務部の意見でございます。

したがいまして、日本国外の対応出願に関する取扱いにつきましては、別途ご協議をお願い申し上げます。当社としては協議に応じる用意がございますし、事実確認の結果次第では同様の書面をお出しすることも十分あり得ると考えております。

本確認書はあくまで上記整理番号の案件の日本国出願に限ったものでございますので、その点は誤解のないようお願い申し上げます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-11**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: PCT/US2011/027655 filed - MAL-2010-0392 - beamforming for conference endpoints  
Date: March 09, 2011, 08:50  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

Filed yesterday at RO/US, one day inside the convention year. Application number PCT/US2011/027655, international filing date March 8, 2011, claiming priority from 61/311,904 of March 9, 2010.

ISA/EP elected. Whitcomb Reyes recommended EP over US on the strength of what we have been getting back on array processing, and I agree with them. The last three US searches on this technology came back with nothing in them but our own publications.

Claims as filed: 3 independent, 22 dependent. Delacroix wanted the excursion-limiting fallback in as a fourth independent claim; Adrienne talked him out of it on unity grounds and it stays as dependent claim 14 for now. He is not happy and he will raise it again at national phase, so please be ready for that conversation rather than surprised by it.

Key dates now in PALLAS:

- Article 19 amendment window, 16 months from priority, July 9, 2011
- Chapter II demand if we want one, January 9, 2012
- 30-month national phase, September 9, 2012
- 31-month national phase, October 9, 2012

No program code on the matter. Ownership stays sole, Marsden only, and I would rather the field stay visibly empty than carry something that gets read as meaning something in five years' time.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-12**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 - the Shinonome confirmation is limited to Japan on its face  
Date: March 17, 2011, 16:25  
References: Email-10  
Body:  
Roswell,

The confirmation letter from Shinonome Electric on MAL-2009-0271 arrived by post on Friday and it is not the letter we asked for.

We asked for an unqualified confirmation. What Kaida signed says the company will not assert rights 日本国内において, in Japan. His covering note is explicit that this was a deliberate edit made on his legal department's advice, that their Munich secondee was in the 2009 technical discussions, and that anything outside Japan is for separate negotiation.

So the position is: the Japanese member of that family is clean, and everything else in it is exactly as unresolved as it was in January. That includes the EP application, which is the one you care about, because the German license discussion you have running assumes we can grant alone.

Two instructions, and I would like them followed literally.

First, nobody is to describe this letter internally as a release, a waiver or a clearance. It is a Japan-only non-assertion, on one family, signed by one counterparty.

Second, it goes on MAL-2009-0271 and on nothing else. I have watched how these things travel. In eighteen months somebody will be told that "Shinonome signed something" and will apply it to whatever matter happens to be in front of them at the time, and the person telling them will be honestly repeating what they were told.

Kaida offered to negotiate the rest and I would rather take him up on it now, while the file is warm and while the people who were in the room are still in their jobs, than in 2016. Tell me if licensing wants me to open it.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-13**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 共同発明の届出について（符号器側ラウドネス測定・別紙A A-2）  
Date: March 24, 2011, 14:35  
References:  
Body:  
淀川国際特許事務所
弁理士 都倉 志乃 先生

いつも大変お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部の海田でございます。

JD-SHN-09 共同開発プログラムにおいて完成いたしました発明につきまして、両社の発明届出書がそろいましたのでご連絡申し上げます。

発明の名称は「チャネル間ゲーティングを用いた符号器側ラウドネス測定」でございます。長時間ラウドネスの積算に際し、チャネル間の相関に基づいて無音区間及び暗騒音区間を除外する処理を用いる点に特徴がございます。

発明者は、貴顧客側が Vidar Holmqvist 氏、当社側が第二音響開発部の楢崎 智也及び三雲 佳奈の計三名でございます。三雲は昨年10月の合同ワークショップにおいてゲーティング閾値の適応化を提案しており、当該提案は請求項3以下に対応するものと理解しております。

本発明は、共同開発契約 別紙A の作業パッケージ A-2「符号器側ラウドネス測定」の対象範囲にそのまま含まれます。したがいまして契約第5条第1項により両社の共有となり、特許法第38条の共同出願として、両社連名で出願する必要がございます。この点について当社内に異論はございません。

つきましては、優先権主張の基礎となる国内出願を4月中旬までに両社連名で行いたく存じます。持分は各二分の一、出願費用は折半、代理人は貴事務所にお願いする、という前提で貴顧客のご了解をいただけますでしょうか。

何卒よろしくお願い申し上げます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部 部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-14**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - joint filing with Shinonome Electric, docketed  
Date: April 20, 2011, 08:45  
References: Email-13  
Body:  
Roswell, Dermot,

Confirming what we agreed on the call. MAL-2011-0455, "Encoder-side loudness measurement with cross-channel gating," is a joint invention under JD-SHN-09 and we are filing it jointly.

This one is not a close question. The subject matter sits inside work package A-2 of Annex A word for word, and two of the three named inventors are Shinonome Electric employees. Kaida-san raised it himself before we did. Article 5.1 of the November 16, 2009 agreement makes inventions arising within the Annex A scope jointly owned in equal shares, and Article 38 of the Japanese Patent Act requires an application for a jointly owned right to be filed by all co-owners together. Filing in our sole name would not merely be wrong, it would be a ground of invalidity under Article 123(1)(ii) with no time limit on it.

Yodogawa filed the priority application, 特願2011-093177, on April 19 in both names. Equal shares, costs split fifty-fifty, Tokura-sensei acts for both applicants with a conflict waiver from each side.

Dermot, please have the docket opened with Ownership Basis "Joint - Shinonome Electric, Annex A A-2, JDA Art. 5.1," program code JD-SHN-09. Also flag it so that nobody grants a license under this family without a written consent from Shinonome. Article 73(3) says a co-owner cannot license without the consent of the others, and Article 7 of the JDA says the same thing in contract terms.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-15**  
To: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
From: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: IDF-2010-1141 관련 권리 귀속 확인서 송부의 건 (JD-HYP-10)  
Date: May 18, 2011, 16:40  
References: Email-9  
Body:  
한울국제특허법률사무소
변리사 방지훈 님

한영정밀 주식회사 지식재산부장 김도윤입니다. 오래 기다리시게 하여 송구합니다.

2010년 12월 9일자로 요청하신 건에 관하여, 당사 지식재산위원회(2011년 3월 24일 개최) 의결 및 법무팀 검토를 거쳐 확인서를 발행하였습니다. 원본은 금일자로 발송하였으며, 대표이사 직인 날인본입니다.

확인서 제1항의 문언은 아래와 같습니다.

「본건 발명은 2010년 1월 14일자 공동개발계약 별지 A에 정하는 대상 범위에 포함되지 아니하며, 귀사의 단독 소유임을 확인합니다. 당사는 본건 발명 및 이에 기초한 국내외 특허를 받을 권리에 대하여 어떠한 지분도 주장하지 아니하며, 제3자에 대한 실시허락도 행하지 아니합니다. 본 확인은 공동개발계약의 유효기간 동안에 한한다.」

제2항에는 본건 발명의 발명자가 귀 의뢰인 종업원인 Léopold Sanschagrin 및 Meret Aalbersberg 2인이며 당사 종업원은 발명자에 포함되지 아니한다는 점을 기재하였습니다.

제3항에는 2010년 6월 24일 정례 검토회에서의 본 기술 시연이 공동개발계약 제9조에 따른 배경기술 정보의 제공으로서 행하여진 것이며 공동개발의 성과물 제출이 아니라는 점을 명기하였습니다.

대상은 발명의 명칭 "열 모델링을 이용한 스피커 보호", 귀 의뢰인 정리번호 MAL-2011-0304, 사내 발명신고 IDF-2010-1141입니다.

귀 의뢰인이 단독 명의로 출원하시는 데 대하여 당사로서 이의가 없습니다.

김도윤
지식재산부장
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-16**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>, Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 - Hanyoung Precision confirmation of sole ownership received  
Date: May 24, 2011, 11:12  
References: Email-15  
Body:  
Ms. Bricknell,

Hanyoung Precision's written confirmation on MAL-2011-0304 arrived by courier this morning, dated May 18, 2011, signed by Kim Do-yun, General Manager of the Intellectual Property Department, under the company seal. The original is in our Seoul file and a scan has gone to the docket mailbox.

The operative paragraph confirms that the invention is not within the scope of Annex A of the January 14, 2010 joint development agreement and is Marsden's sole property; that Hanyoung asserts no share in the invention or in the right to obtain patents on it in Korea or elsewhere; and that Hanyoung will not license it to third parties. A second paragraph records that the two named inventors are Marsden employees and that no Hanyoung employee is an inventor. A third paragraph records that the June 24, 2010 demonstration was background technology shared under Article 9 and not a program deliverable.

That is everything Ms. Solheim-Baptista asked for in December and rather more than I expected them to give. Hanyoung's IP committee took it on March 24 and legal cleared it afterward, which is why it ran two months past the date I gave you.

You are clear to file in Marsden's sole name. Please let me know the intended priority route so that I can diarize the Korean national phase.

Ji-hoon Bang
Patent Attorney
Hanul International Patent & Law Office

**Email-17**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
Subject: RE: MAL-2011-0304 - Hanyoung Precision confirmation of sole ownership received  
Date: June 15, 2011, 13:28  
References: Email-16  
Body:  
Dermot,

Approved. File MAL-2011-0304 in Marsden's sole name.

Route: US provisional on or before June 17, then a PCT at twelve months claiming priority, then KR, EP, JP and CN at national phase. Sanschagrin and Aalbersberg are the only inventors and both assignments are already on file under their employment agreements.

For the docket record, Ownership Status is Sole. Put the following in the Ownership Basis note, in these words, so that the reason travels with the matter:

Sole - Hanyoung Precision confirmation 2011-05-18 (KO), Seoul corr. file, scan in docket mailbox.

The program code stays JD-HYP-10 because that is where the engineering time was booked and Roswell's team allocates against it. I want to be clear with everyone that the program code is a cost-allocation field and says nothing about who owns the matter. We have had that confusion on two other programs this year.

One housekeeping point. The confirmation is in Korean and the file copy is the Korean original. Mr. Bang's letter of May 24 is the only English account of what it says, so keep the two together in the correspondence folder. If anyone in San Mateo needs the letter itself, Seoul will have to read it to them.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-18**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0329 - sample rate conversion with fractional delay compensation - PCT on file  
Date: June 15, 2011, 13:35  
References:  
Body:  
Ingrid,

PCT/US2011/040288 was filed yesterday, June 14, claiming priority from 61/355,721 of June 17, 2010. Sample rate conversion with fractional delay compensation. Sole inventor, Ansel Kirkbride-Nwosu.

Two things for the docket.

The claim set narrowed a long way from the provisional. We dropped the polyphase bank claims entirely after the pre-filing search turned up a 2004 Japanese publication that reads on them almost word for word. What survives is the error-feedback loop on the fractional delay estimate, which the search did not touch. Kirkbride-Nwosu has told me twice that he thinks we have given away the useful part. I have told him twice that the alternative was a patent we could not enforce. I expect a third conversation.

The other thing is the number. This matter is 0329. Not 0392. I have had two people this quarter open the wrong file because they read the digits in the order they expected rather than the order they were written, and one of those two was me. Until we have a docketing system that will let us alias a matter to a title string, please put the title in the subject line every single time, as I have done here.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-19**  
To: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Reinhild Achterberg <r.achterberg@kestenholz-wt.de>  
Subject: Liefervertrag KWT/Marsden Europe - Annex A und Schutzrechte an Änderungskonstruktionen  
Date: September 14, 2011, 08:55  
References:  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Hollerith,

im Anschluss an das Telefonat vom Montag fasse ich unseren Standpunkt schriftlich zusammen.

Erstens zum Annex A des Liefervertrags vom 4. April 2009. Annex A führt derzeit vierzehn Teilenummern mit Preisstaffel auf. Die Positionen KWT-4412-A und KWT-4412-B laufen zum 31. Dezember 2011 aus und werden durch die Position KWT-4418-A ersetzt. Wir bitten um eine Änderungsvereinbarung, die Annex A mit Wirkung zum 1. Januar 2012 neu fasst. Die Preisstaffel bleibt unverändert; die Mindestabnahme sinkt von 40.000 auf 25.000 Stück je Kalenderjahr.

Zweitens zu den Schutzrechten, weshalb ich Sie und nicht den Einkauf anschreibe. § 9 des Liefervertrags bestimmt, dass Änderungskonstruktionen an den in Annex A aufgeführten Teilen, die von uns allein entwickelt werden, unser alleiniges Eigentum bleiben und Marsden lediglich ein einfaches, nicht übertragbares Nutzungsrecht für die Dauer der Belieferung erhält. Gemeinsam entwickelte Änderungen stehen beiden Parteien zu gleichen Teilen zu.

Die geänderte Zentrierspinne der Position KWT-4418-A stammt vollständig aus unserer Entwicklung in Villingen. Wir haben hierzu am 22. August 2011 eine deutsche Gebrauchsmusteranmeldung eingereicht, Aktenzeichen 20 2011 050 118.4. Bitte bestätigen Sie uns, dass Marsden hieraus keine über § 9 hinausgehenden Rechte herleitet und dass die Aufnahme der Position in Annex A daran nichts ändert.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen
Reinhild Achterberg
Leiterin Vertrieb
Kestenholz Wandlertechnik GmbH

**Email-20**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: WO 2011/112480 A1 published - the search report is better than it looks  
Date: September 20, 2011, 10:10  
References: Email-11  
Body:  
Eleanor,

International publication came out on September 15 as WO 2011/112480 A1, with the search report and written opinion.

The ISA cited five documents. One X against claim 1 only, a Kesselring Audiotechnik application from 2008 on beam steering for a wall-mounted conference unit. Two Y in combination against claims 1 and 3, both the same applicant. Two A.

Adrienne's read, and mine, is that the X reference is a steering document and not an adaptation document. It changes the beam pattern in response to a user control. It does not change it in response to the measured coherence of the array. Claim 1 as filed does not say that clearly enough, which is exactly why we got the X, and the Article 34 amendment can fix it in one sentence.

The written opinion is negative on novelty for claim 1 and positive on inventive step from claim 4 down, which is where the substance sits anyway.

Do you want a Chapter II demand? The deadline is January 9. My recommendation is yes, purely to put a clean preliminary report in front of examiners in four offices for the price of one filing. Dermot thinks it is money we do not need to spend and that we will end up making the same argument in each office regardless of what the IPEA says. We have each been wrong about this before, so I am putting both views to you rather than one.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-21**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 共同出願の取扱いについて（費用分担及び国際出願の準備）  
Date: October 12, 2011, 09:50  
References: Email-13  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

いつもお世話になっております。淀川国際特許事務所の都倉でございます。

標記の件、両社のご了解事項を書面にまとめましたので、ご確認をお願い申し上げます。

一、出願人は、東雲電機株式会社及び Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. の両名、持分は各二分の一といたします。

二、基礎出願は特願2011-093177（2011年4月19日出願）でございます。

三、国際出願は、受理官庁を日本国特許庁として2012年4月中旬までに行います。優先期間の満了は2012年4月19日でございますので、事務処理の余裕をみて4月上旬の提出を予定しております。

四、費用は出願、中間手続、登録及び年金のいずれも折半とし、当事務所より両社に二分の一ずつご請求申し上げます。

五、指定国のうち実際に国内移行する国は、国際調査報告及び見解書の内容を踏まえ、優先日から二十四月の時点で両社協議のうえ決定いたします。現時点の想定は日本国、米国、欧州特許庁、韓国及び中国でございます。

六、共有に係る特許を受ける権利につき、いずれの当事者も他方の書面による同意なくして第三者への実施許諾を行わないものといたします。特許法第73条第3項及び共同開発契約第7条の趣旨によるものでございます。

七、当職は両社の共同代理人として手続を行います。利益相反に関する同意書を両社よりご提出いただいております。

以上について、ご異存がなければ本メールへのご返信をもって確認とさせていただきます。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-22**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Coriolis audio codec program - Annex A refresh due January 31 - two claims coming out  
Date: November 08, 2011, 15:25  
References:  
Body:  
Roswell,

The annual Annex A refresh for the Coriolis Audio Codec licensing program opens next week and closes January 31. Annex A of the program license agreement is the list of essential claims; it is claim-level, not patent-level, which is why it takes three weeks of engineering time every year and why I keep asking for the time in advance.

Marsden currently has fourteen claims in Annex A across six families. The proposed changes are:

Remove claim 7 of US 7,982,614. The 2011 revision of the specification moved the gain-smoothing step into an informative annex, so the claim is no longer read on by a compliant implementation.

Remove claim 1 of JP 特許第4721335号 for the same reason, and keep claims 4 and 5.

Add claims 1 and 12 of EP 1 892 703 B1, which the program's independent essentiality assessor has already reviewed favorably.

Net effect on our share of the royalty pool is a rise of roughly 0.4 points, because the pool weights by family count in each region rather than by claim count, and the EP addition brings a region where we were thin.

Vantour, as program administrator, will circulate the consolidated Annex A on February 15. Anything not in Annex A on that date earns nothing for the year, whatever it says on the certificate.

Wendell Aduba-Reiss
Director, Standards
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-23**  
To: Almut Pfannenstiel <pfannenstiel@pfannenstiel-ip.de>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0932 (Münchener Serie) – Lautsprecher-Auslenkungsbegrenzung – Aktenzeichen bitte stets mit Seriennennung  
Date: November 09, 2011, 09:45  
References:  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Dr. Pfannenstiel,

vielen Dank für die Eingangsbestätigung zur europäischen Anmeldung vom 29. September 2011. Bevor wir in die Sachprüfung gehen, eine Bitte zur Aktenführung, die uns hier in München schon mehrfach Ärger bereitet hat.

Marsden führt derzeit drei voneinander unabhängige Aktenzeichenreihen: San Mateo, Yokohama und München. Jede Reihe beginnt im Januar wieder bei 0001. Das Aktenzeichen MAL-2010-0932 gibt es deshalb zweimal, einmal bei uns für die vorliegende Sache "Lautsprecher-Auslenkungsbegrenzung" und einmal in San Mateo für eine ganz andere Erfindung aus dem Bereich der Raumkorrektur. Im September ist eine Kostennote auf die falsche Kostenstelle gelaufen, weil in der Rechnung nur die nackte Nummer stand, und die Rückbuchung hat mich zwei Vormittage gekostet.

Bitte schreiben Sie daher in jeder Korrespondenz und auf jeder Rechnung "MAL-2010-0932 (Münchener Serie)". Umgekehrt gilt: erhalten Sie von uns eine Nummer ohne Serienangabe, fragen Sie bitte nach, statt zu raten.

Zur Sache selbst. Die Anmeldung beansprucht die Priorität der DE 10 2010 041 887.2 vom 30. September 2010. Alleiniger Erfinder ist Herr Konradin Ahlborn-Vesely, hier in München angestellt. Die Diensterfindung wurde ordnungsgemäß gemeldet und unbeschränkt in Anspruch genommen; die Vergütungsvereinbarung liegt vor.

Zu notieren wäre ferner, dass die Jahresgebühr für das dritte Jahr für die europäische Anmeldung am 30. September 2013 fällig wird, also unabhängig davon, ob bis dahin erteilt ist. Ich habe das hier vermerkt und bitte Sie, es ebenfalls zu vermerken.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen

Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-24**  
To: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
From: 陈慕青 <chen.muqing@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 《联合开发协议》(JD-CHW-12) 已于3月15日双方用印完毕  
Date: March 19, 2012, 10:20  
References:  
Body:  
傅老师，您好。

《辰威微电子有限公司与 Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. 联合开发协议》（项目编号 JD-CHW-12）已于2012年3月15日在上海完成双方用印，一式四份，中英文各两份。协议第十八条约定，中英文本不一致时以中文本为准，烦请您在归档及后续办理专利事务时以中文本为据。

协议附件A列明六个工作包（A-1 至 A-6），依次为低码率传输层、比特分配策略、片上集成验证、参考解码器实现、一致性测试向量、系统级功耗评估。项目实施期限自2012年4月1日起至2015年3月31日止。

请您特别留意第五条的约定，全文如下：

「第五条（知识产权归属）　在本合作项目实施过程中产生的一切发明创造、技术秘密及其他知识产权，无论是否属于附件A所列工作包的范围，均由双方共同所有，各占百分之五十的份额。任何一方不得单独以自己名义就前述发明创造申请专利。」

这一条与我司以往对外签署的同类协议不同，是本次谈判中我方坚持保留的条款，对方法务在第三轮谈判后接受。请您在为本项目办理任何专利申请事务时以此为准，如遇任何一方单方提出申请的情形，请及时告知我。

项目例会定于每月第二个周三上午，地点在张江。烦请代为转达 Ostenfeld 先生。

陈慕青
知识产权总监
辰威微电子有限公司

**Email-25**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Chenwei Microsystems JDA executed - JD-CHW-12 - Annex A does NOT bound ownership on this one  
Date: March 20, 2012, 10:10  
References:  
Body:  
All,

The joint development agreement with 辰威微电子有限公司 (Chenwei Microsystems Co., Ltd.), Shanghai, was executed in Shanghai on March 15. Program code JD-CHW-12. Article 18 makes the Chinese text the governing text; the English in our file is a courtesy translation prepared by their side. Treat it as a reading aid and nothing more.

Annex A lists six work packages: A-1 low-bitrate transport layer, A-2 bit allocation strategy, A-3 on-chip integration validation, A-4 reference decoder implementation, A-5 conformance test vectors, A-6 system-level power evaluation.

Now read Article 5, and read it twice, because it is not the clause you are used to. The translation reads:

"Article 5 (Ownership of Intellectual Property). All inventions, technical know-how and other intellectual property arising in the course of the implementation of this program, whether or not falling within the scope of the work packages listed in Annex A, shall be jointly owned by the two Parties in equal shares of fifty percent (50%) each. Neither Party shall file a patent application in its own name alone in respect of the foregoing."

Whether or not. Annex A on this agreement defines the work to be done and the cost split. It does not define what is jointly owned. I lost that point in the January session and I want everyone to know I lost it rather than discover it in 2019.

There is no Annex A carve-out of the kind we have on the Shinonome and Hanyoung agreements and there is no sub-clause preserving anything back to either side. What Chinese law gives a co-owner by default is what each of us will have: self-working, and a unilateral non-exclusive license with the royalties shared. Anything exclusive needs both signatures.

Two operational consequences. First, a disclosure that comes out of this program is presumptively joint; the question is whether it arose "in the course of the Program," not whether it sits in a work package. Second, for any invention made in China we need the confidentiality examination before filing abroad. Do not let an engineer in Shanghai send a draft to San Mateo and treat that as a US-first filing.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-26**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 国際出願完了のご報告（PCT/JP2012/059118）  
Date: April 03, 2012, 15:05  
References: Email-21  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

お世話になっております。都倉でございます。

標記国際出願を本日、日本国特許庁を受理官庁として提出いたしましたのでご報告申し上げます。

・国際出願番号 PCT/JP2012/059118
・国際出願日 2012年4月3日
・優先権主張 特願2011-093177（2011年4月19日）
・出願人 東雲電機株式会社、Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.（各二分の一）
・発明の名称 チャネル間ゲーティングを用いた符号器側ラウドネス測定
・発明者 楢崎 智也、三雲 佳奈、Vidar Holmqvist
・使用言語 日本語
・請求項数 18（独立項3）

願書の出願人欄は両社連名で記載しております。国際段階における手続は当職が両社を代理して行いますが、優先権証明書の請求及び PCT第19条補正の要否につきましては、国際調査報告の受領後にあらためてご相談申し上げます。国際調査報告は本年7月頃の発行が見込まれます。

国際公開は優先日から十八月経過後、2012年10月下旬の予定でございます。

なお、国内移行の判断期限は、日本国、米国及び中国が優先日から三十月（2013年10月19日）、欧州特許庁及び韓国が三十一月（2013年11月19日）でございます。期限管理表を別途整備のうえ、本年内にお送りいたします。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-27**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 貴社整理番号 MAL-2012-0114 に関する確認書ご依頼の件（ご回答）  
Date: May 15, 2012, 14:05  
References:  
Body:  
都倉先生

東雲電機の海田でございます。4月26日付でご依頼いただきました確認書の件につきまして、社内で検討いたしました結果をご回答申し上げます。

結論から申し上げますと、本件につきましては確認書を差し上げることができません。

ご依頼の対象は、貴社整理番号 MAL-2012-0114、発明の名称「共通メタデータ伝送のためのビットストリーム構文拡張」と承知しております。当社の理解では、本発明は共同開発契約の別紙Aに掲げる作業項目のうち、共通メタデータ伝送に係るビットストリーム構文の項目そのものに該当いたします。契約第5条第1項により、別紙Aの範囲内で生じた発明は両社の共有と定められております。

事実関係につきましても、当社技術者の関与は形式的なものではないと考えております。2011年11月の定例レビューの議事録には、当社の宮部が提案いたしました拡張フィールドの構成が記載されております。貴社の発明届出書に記載された構成は、当方の記録と照らす限り、これと実質的に同一でございます。当該議事録は両社でご確認いただいたものでございます。

したがいまして、当社といたしましては、本件は共有に係る発明として、特許法第38条により両社共同で出願すべき案件であると考えます。共同出願とする場合の費用分担および手続の窓口につきましては、当社から具体案をお示しすることも可能でございます。

昨年、別件で確認書を差し上げた経緯がございますので、本件も同様にとお考えであったかもしれません。しかしながら当社では、確認書のご依頼をいただくたびに、案件ごとに事実関係を確認したうえで判断させていただいております。一律の取扱いはいたしておりません。何卒ご理解を賜りますようお願い申し上げます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-28**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Re: file copy request - MAL-2010-0392 - you have pulled the wrong 0392  
Date: June 12, 2012, 11:50  
References:  
Body:  
Tomás,

Before I copy anything: the file you have asked for and the file you want are two different matters.

Your note says you need "the 0392 prosecution history for the Yokohama licensing review." The matter you cited by number, MAL-2010-0392, is the San Mateo series matter. It is adaptive microphone-array beamforming for conference endpoints, Delacroix and Umbertsen, provisional in March 2010, PCT published last September. It has nothing to do with anything anybody in Yokohama is licensing, and I am fairly confident it is not what you are after.

There is a second matter carrying the same bare number in the Yokohama series. Different invention, different inventors, different responsible attorney, different office. I am not the paralegal on it, and I am not going to pull a file out of another office's series on a request that does not say which series it is.

This is the third time this year. Until January the three offices numbered independently and every series restarted at 0001 each January, so in 2010 alone I know of collisions at 0158, 0392 and 0932, and I would not bet against there being others. The harmonization in January re-prefixed the Yokohama series going forward. San Mateo numbers were left exactly as they were, which is why this matter still answers to the bare number and why a substring search will keep finding it forever.

If you tell me what the licensing review actually needs, I can probably work out which file you want faster than you can.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-29**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Shinonome royalty statement Q1 2012 - two product codes missing  
Date: June 13, 2012, 13:35  
References:  
Body:  
Roswell,

The Q1 statement from 東雲セミコンダクター株式会社 (Shinonome Semiconductor Co., Ltd., Kumamoto) came in on May 15, ten days inside the forty-five day window, so no late issue. This is our licensee under the patent license agreement effective July 1, 2011, not a development partner, and I say that because the statement was filed to the wrong folder again.

Reported volume is 3,914,220 units at USD 0.043, giving USD 168,311.46. Payment cleared June 8.

Two product codes are missing. Their own November product bulletin lists SDX-3F and SDX-3H as shipping since February, and both carry the licensed decoder block. Neither appears in the statement. Either the codes were rolled into SDX-3E for reporting, which the agreement does not permit because Section 6.2 requires reporting by product designation, or they are being treated as out of scope.

I have asked their licensing group for a line-by-line reconciliation by July 6. If the answer is that SDX-3F is a derivative and therefore reported under the parent, we should say now that we do not accept that reading, in writing, so that it does not become a course of dealing. We have an audit right once every three years under Section 9 and we have never used it.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-30**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2012-0114 の件（7月4日お打合せの確認および共同出願のご提案）  
Date: July 10, 2012, 09:30  
References: Email-27  
Body:  
都倉先生

先週4日はお時間を頂戴し、ありがとうございました。当社の見解に変更はございませんので、書面にて改めて申し上げます。

一、本件発明は別紙Aの範囲内であり、契約第5条第1項により両社の共有に属するものと考えております。この点は当社法務部および技術部門の双方で確認済みでございます。

二、貴社よりご指摘のございました「当社技術者の寄与は課題の提示にとどまる」という点につきましては、当方は見解を異にいたします。2011年11月の議事録に加えまして、同年12月2日に当社の宮部から貴社エンジニア宛に送付いたしました構文案（版数0.7）が当社に残っております。当該構文案の第4節と貴社出願の請求項1とを比較いたしますと、寄与が課題提示にとどまるとは当方としては申し上げられません。当該構文案は必要であれば写しをご提供いたします。

三、したがいまして、当社としては特許法第38条に基づく共同出願をご提案申し上げます。持分は各二分の一、出願費用は折半、手続の窓口は貴社ご指定の代理人に一本化していただいて差し支えございません。日本国外への出願につきましても同様の取扱いを希望いたします。

優先期間の関係で本年11月には方針を確定する必要があるものと承知しております。当社としては争いを望むものではございませんので、8月中にもう一度お時間を頂戴できればと存じます。

なお念のため申し添えます。当社は、求められた確認書に一律に署名するという方針をとってはおりません。案件ごとに社内の記録と技術者への聞き取りを行ったうえで、差し支えのないものについてのみお出ししております。本件はそれに当たらない、というのが今回の判断でございます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-31**  
To: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: National phase instructions - PCT/US2011/027655 - MAL-2010-0392 - US, EP, JP, CN  
Date: September 06, 2012, 15:20  
References: Email-11  
Body:  
Adrienne,

Instructions for national phase on the beamforming case. Eleanor signed off on the country list this morning.

Enter: United States, Europe, Japan, China. Do not enter Korea or India. The endpoint business has no Korean manufacture and the India numbers do not carry the cost.

Deadlines as we hold them:

- US, JP, CN: 30 months from priority, September 9, 2012. That is a Sunday, so the working deadline is Monday September 10, and I would like all three in this week regardless.
- EP: 31 months, October 9, 2012.
- China can be taken to 32 months on payment of the surcharge. We should not need it and I do not want to pay it.

For Japan, please instruct Tokura-sensei to file the entry now and the Japanese translation on her own schedule within the statutory window. The request for examination is due three years from the international filing date, so March 8, 2014. I have docketed the date itself and a six-month warning.

Use the amended claims from the Article 34 response as the basis everywhere except China, where we file as originally published and amend on entry into substantive examination.

Ownership on all four is Marsden alone. No co-applicant anywhere, nothing to record beyond the two inventor assignments already on file in the US, and no program code on the matter.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-32**  
To: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
From: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Subject: JD-HYP-10 3분기 정례 검토회 일정 및 시료 반송 건  
Date: September 12, 2012, 14:50  
References:  
Body:  
방지훈 변리사님

한영정밀 오세라입니다. 공동개발 프로그램 관련 사무 세 건 안내드립니다.

첫째, 3분기 정례 검토회는 10월 18일 목요일 오후 2시, 당사 안산사업장 제3연구동 회의실에서 개최합니다. 의뢰인 측 참석 예정자 명단을 9월 28일까지 회신하여 주시면 출입 등록을 진행하겠습니다. 이번 회차 의제는 H-3 열시험 지그의 3차 개정안과 H-4 양산 합격기준의 잠정 수치입니다.

둘째, 2분기에 의뢰인 측에 대여한 트랜스듀서 시료 12개(관리번호 HYP-TS-2012-041 내지 052) 가운데 8개가 아직 반송되지 않았습니다. 계약 부속서 C의 시료 관리 조항에 따라 대여 후 6개월 이내에 반송하거나 폐기 확인서를 제출하도록 되어 있습니다. 10월 검토회 전까지 처리 부탁드립니다.

셋째, 공동개발계약의 유효기간이 2014년 3월 31일로 되어 있습니다. 연장 여부는 계약 제3조에 따라 만료 6개월 전까지 서면으로 협의하도록 되어 있으므로, 2013년 하반기에 양사 실무 협의를 시작하는 것으로 일정에 반영해 두었습니다.

이상입니다. 감사합니다.

오세라
지식재산팀 대리
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-33**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Coastal Piedmont sponsored research agreement executed - Annex A research plan and the Article 7 problem  
Date: October 11, 2012, 07:50  
References:  
Body:  
Dermot,

The sponsored research agreement with Coastal Piedmont University was executed October 4. Two years, USD 640,000, principal investigator in the electrical engineering department, one postdoc and two graduate students.

Annex A is the research plan and it is three tasks: Task 1 psychoacoustic masking measurements on a listener panel, Task 2 room impulse response database collection, Task 3 an open reference implementation of the measurement tooling.

Article 7 is where our exposure sits and it is not negotiable at this university; they use the same clause with everyone.

7.1 Inventions made solely by University personnel in performance of the Annex A research plan are the sole property of the University. Marsden gets a first option, exercisable within six months of disclosure, to an exclusive license on commercially reasonable terms to be negotiated.

7.2 Inventions made jointly by University and Marsden personnel in performance of the Annex A research plan are jointly owned, with each party free to practice without accounting.

7.3 Anything made outside the Annex A research plan is untouched by the agreement.

The practical instruction is the same one I give on every one of these. Keep our separately funded work off the Annex A tasks and keep our engineers out of the lab notebook. Every hour a Marsden engineer spends inside Task 2 converts a University-owned invention we might license into a jointly owned invention we will have to co-manage forever.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-34**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2012-0257 - international search report - X citation is WO 2012/024513 (Norrköping Akustik)  
Date: October 24, 2012, 10:35  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

The search report on MAL-2012-0257, room response equalization from sparse measurement points, came back yesterday and it is worse than I had hoped.

The X reference is WO 2012/024513 A1, Norrköping Akustik AB, published February 23, 2012, priority August 2010. Cited against claims 1, 2, 5 and 11. I have read it twice. Their Figure 6 is our Figure 4 with the axes swapped, and their paragraph [0071] describes the sparse sampling grid in terms I would have been happy to write myself.

The differences I can find are these. They interpolate on a regular grid; we interpolate on a Delaunay triangulation of the measured points, which matters as soon as the room stops being rectangular. And they require a calibrated reference microphone, where we do not, and claim 5 says so. Claim 5 says it as a negative limitation, which no examiner has ever liked.

Options, ranked as I would rank them. Amend claim 1 to require the triangulation and accept a narrower patent. Argue that the reference teaches away from irregular grids at [0083] and hold the broad claim, which I would put at about one chance in four. Or drop this one and put the money into the follow-on disclosure that Delacroix filed last month, which is arguably the better invention anyway.

I would like twenty minutes with you before the Article 19 window closes on November 23.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-35**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>, Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: New disclosure MAL-2012-0688 - object metadata compression for low-bitrate transport  
Date: November 13, 2012, 15:45  
References:  
Body:  
Eleanor,

Invention disclosure IDF-2012-114 came in on November 8 from Solveig Marchetti-Rao and Cassius Delahaye-Brandt, both in the codec group here. I have docketed it as MAL-2012-0688, title "Object metadata compression for low-bitrate transport."

The substance: a shared exponent is computed across a group of object position vectors and each object carries only a mantissa differential against it, with the group size adapted to the observed spatial coherence of the scene. The bit budget for twenty-four objects drops from roughly 48 kbps to roughly 9 kbps at the reported error floor. Solveig's numbers are in the disclosure and Wendell has seen them.

One thing you should decide before I instruct anyone. The program code field on the disclosure form reads JD-CHW-12. The engineers charged their hours to the Chenwei program because that is where the budget was, and the reduction to practice happened on the Chenwei reference platform in Shanghai. My reading of Annex A is that none of the six work packages is about metadata compression — they are transport layer, bit allocation, silicon integration, reference decoder, conformance vectors and power. So I do not think the program agreement reaches this.

I would still like a view from you on whether we ask Chenwei to confirm that in writing before we file. My instinct is that it costs us one letter and buys us certainty, but it is your call and Halvard may have a view given where the second-phase talks are.

Proposed timetable: US provisional by mid-January, PCT twelve months later.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-36**  
To: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0392 - bitstream error concealment for object metadata - PCT filed, and please write the year  
Date: November 14, 2012, 16:40  
References:  
Body:  
Docket,

PCT/US2012/063114 was filed on November 2 on MAL-2011-0392, bitstream error concealment for object metadata. Priority 61/558,406 of November 10, 2011. Inventors Sunniva Belmonte-Achterberg and Hyacinth Delacroix. San Mateo series, sole Marsden ownership, no program code.

I am writing to the docket address rather than to individuals because I want this in the record and not in somebody's inbox.

We now have, live and pending at the same time, a 2010 matter numbered 0392 and a 2011 matter numbered 0392. I have already seen one internal note this year that referred to "the 0392 case" as though that were a complete identification of anything. It has not been a complete identification since January 2011 and it is about to get worse, because the 2011 matter will be in national phase in eighteen months and both will be generating correspondence at the same time.

The rule from today is that the four-digit number is never written on its own. Full docket number including the year, and the series where the matter predates harmonization. If a report template does not have room for it, the template is wrong and I will authorize the effort to fix it.

Dermot, I am aware that you have been doing this correctly for a year and that this note reads as though it is aimed at you. It is not.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-37**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2012-0187 - end-of-line acoustic test sequencing - this one is inside Annex A work package H-4  
Date: November 28, 2012, 17:22  
References: Email-3  
Body:  
Eleanor,

I have finished the Annex A determination on MAL-2012-0187, "Automated sequencing of end-of-line acoustic tests using a shared excitation signal." My conclusion is that it falls inside JD-HYP-10 Annex A work package H-4 and is therefore jointly owned with Hanyoung Precision in equal shares under Article 5.1. It is not the sole property of either party.

The reasoning, briefly. H-4 is "production-line acceptance criteria," which is exactly the subject matter. The work was done on Hanyoung's line in Ansan during scheduled program time, using their fixture. The two contributors are one of ours and one of theirs, and on my reading of the claim I would draft, both contributed to the characterizing feature — the shared excitation signal that lets the sequence collapse from four measurements to two came from their engineer in the September 12 review, and the timing scheme that makes it work came from ours.

That means Korean Patent Act Article 44 requires a joint application, and I would file jointly in KR and US and skip everything else. It also means Article 99 applies: we cannot license this to a third party without Hanyoung's consent, and we should assume they will want a fee.

I would rather record the determination now and live with a joint filing than file alone and be told in 2020 that we should not have. Please confirm and I will open the joint filing instruction.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-38**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: RE: New disclosure MAL-2012-0688 - we file alone and we are not writing to Chenwei about it  
Date: January 22, 2013, 09:35  
References: Email-35  
Body:  
Dermot,

Answering the question you left open in November. No, we are not asking Chenwei to confirm anything, and I want the reasons on the file so that nobody reopens this every time the program comes up for renewal.

First, Annex A is six work packages and every one of them is transport layer or silicon. Metadata compression is not in any of them and the drafting of Annex A is tight — the parties negotiated it item by item, which is why it took three rounds. The scope question answers itself on the face of the document.

Second, and this is the part that matters commercially, asking for a letter tells them there is something here worth having an opinion about. Today they have no view because nobody has invited one. If we write, they will form a view, and they will form it with their licensing people in the room. Halvard is three weeks from closing the second-phase extension and I am not putting a live ownership question in front of Chen Muqing while that is on the table.

Third, the program code is a cost code. It records where hours were booked. It is not a statement about who owns an invention and we should stop treating it as one.

File the provisional in Marsden's name alone. Record on the file that a confirmation was considered and declined, and why. I have not gone back through the agreement text for this and I do not think I need to; the work package list is the point.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-39**  
To: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Open matter MAL-2013-0771 - vibration-isolating suspension for compact voice-coil transducers - Nagano supplier, JP first filing  
Date: February 14, 2013, 09:45  
References:  
Body:  
Docket,

Please open MAL-2013-0771 in PALLAS, responsible attorney me, title "Vibration-isolating suspension for compact voice-coil transducers." Yokohama mechanical design group, two named inventors, both ours. Target a Japanese first filing in August 2013; this is a mechanical case with no US commercial urgency and the manufacture is entirely in Japan.

Background you need for the file. The transducer subassembly is machined by 東雲精密工業株式会社 (Shinonome Seimitsu Kogyo Co., Ltd.) of Nagano under a development consignment basic agreement dated September 3, 2012. They are a precision machining house, roughly 200 people, family owned. They are not related to Shinonome Electric in Osaka and they are not part of that group; the name is written with the same characters and that is all. Please do not link this matter to any existing Shinonome party record. Open a new one.

The agreement matters for ownership. Article 6.1 says inventions made solely by one party's personnel belong to that party. Article 6.2 says inventions made jointly are jointly owned, with shares to be fixed by discussion according to contribution. Our design group's position is that the suspension geometry was conceived internally in July 2012 before the drawings went to Nagano, so this is an Article 6.1 case and we file alone.

I have asked the group for the notebook pages that support the July date. Ownership Status stays Sole in the record until I have them.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-40**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0392 - Japanese national phase - translation on file, examination request timing  
Date: February 20, 2013, 09:15  
References: Email-31  
Body:  
Tokura-sensei,

Thank you for the year-end status report. Reconciling our record against yours: the Japanese national phase on this family was entered on September 10, 2012, the translation of the description and claims went in the same day, and the application number is 特願2012-556301. Our sheet had two of those digits the wrong way round and I have corrected it today.

On the request for examination. The statutory date is March 8, 2014, three years from the international filing date. You have recommended waiting until the last quarter of that period so that we can take account of the US first office action, which we expect in the summer. I understand the logic and I am not going to overrule you on Japanese practice.

Eleanor's instruction, however, is to request examination in September 2013, and the reason has nothing to do with prosecution strategy. The endpoint product using this technology ships in Japan in the second half of 2014 and she wants the file moving before there is a product on a shelf rather than after. If that costs us one wasted amendment, she is content to pay for one wasted amendment.

Could you confirm you can do that, and give me an estimate for the official fee at the claim count currently on file, which is 25.

One thing for your own file. This application has no co-applicant and no co-owner in any jurisdiction, and there is no joint development program behind it. If anything ever reaches you suggesting otherwise, it will be about a different matter of ours, and I would be grateful if you told me the same day rather than acting on it.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-41**  
To: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 韩立衡 <han.liheng@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Cc: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>, 陈慕青 <chen.muqing@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Subject: JD-CHW-12 二月度技术进展及元数据部分的分工确认  
Date: March 06, 2013, 14:10  
References:  
Body:  
Aduba-Reiss 先生，您好。

二月度进展汇报如下，请您审阅。

工作包 A-1，传输层封装已完成第三版，与贵司1月28日提供的参考流对齐无误。工作包 A-3，片上集成在我司 CW-8120 评估板上跑通，24对象场景下解码侧峰值占用为 41 MIPS，低于协议附件A所列 55 MIPS 的目标。工作包 A-5，一致性测试向量已生成 118 条，其中 26 条覆盖元数据边界条件。

有一件事想请您明确。去年10月上海集成周期间，我方周立群、邵旻两位同事提出的分组共享指数方案，目前已被写入 A-1 的封装格式草案第 6.4 节，并由邵旻完成了第一版定点实现（提交号 r2871）。该方案对码率的贡献很大，24对象场景由 48 kbps 降至 9 kbps 左右，主要就是这一条带来的。

我想确认的是，这部分成果在项目交付物中如何归类，以及双方是否需要就此提交专利申请。按照协议第五条，项目期间产生的发明创造由双方共同所有，我理解无论其是否落在附件A的工作包范围内。陈总监也提到过这一点。

若贵司已有相关申请安排，烦请告知，我方好在技术文档中作相应标注。

顺颂时祺。

韩立衡
研发副总裁
辰威微电子有限公司

**Email-42**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 日本国内移行、出願審査請求及び早期審査事情説明書について  
Date: April 11, 2013, 10:28  
References: Email-26  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

お世話になっております。都倉でございます。

標記国際出願の日本国内への移行手続を本年4月9日付で完了いたしましたのでご報告申し上げます。国際出願の言語が日本語でございますので翻訳文の提出は不要でございます。国内出願番号は特願2013-510827、出願人は両社連名のままでございます。

あわせて、両社のご指示に従い、同日付で出願審査請求を行いました。出願審査請求の法定期限は国際出願日から三年、すなわち2015年4月3日でございますが、事業日程を踏まえ前倒しで請求しております。

さらに、早期審査に関する事情説明書を提出いたしました。理由は実施関連出願（両社が本発明を実装した符号化装置を既に販売していること）及び外国関連出願（欧州特許庁及び米国に対応出願があること）の二点でございます。特許庁の運用によれば、事情説明書の提出から二月ないし三月で最初の通知が発せられる例が多うございます。

拒絶理由通知が発せられました場合、本件は日本法人が出願人に含まれ、かつ国内代理人が選任されておりますので、応答期間は六十日でございます。外国出願人のみの場合の三月とは異なりますのでご留意ください。

中間対応の方針につきましては、通知の受領後、両社同席のうえご協議申し上げたく存じます。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-43**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Re: MAL-2012-0257 - amendment over WO 2012/024513 - take the narrow claim  
Date: April 17, 2013, 13:05  
References: Email-34  
Body:  
Dermot,

I have now read the Norrköping publication properly rather than skimming it, and I have pulled their US national phase, which entered in February and has not been taken up.

I do not think the teaching-away argument survives contact with an examiner. Paragraph [0083] says a regular grid is "preferred for computational reasons." Preferred is not taught away from. There is a real prospect an examiner reads that sentence as an express invitation to use an irregular grid wherever computation happens to be cheap, which on any device built after about 2015 it will be.

Take the triangulation limitation up into claim 1. You lose the equalizer vendors who only deal with rectangular rooms, which is most of them, and you keep the automotive and cinema cases, which is where the licensing conversation actually is.

Two other points.

Watch their US file. If they amend toward the irregular grid we have a priority contest in substance if not in form, and I would rather see it coming than read about it.

Their priority document is a Swedish national application and I have not seen it. Order a certified copy. If the priority is not supported for the sparse-grid disclosure, their effective date moves to August 2011, which is after your filing date, and the whole problem goes away. I would not build a strategy on that, but I would spend three hundred dollars finding out.

Adrienne Whitcomb
Whitcomb Reyes LLP

**Email-44**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: RE: claim scope on MAL-2012-0688 - where the shared-exponent idea actually came from  
Date: May 14, 2013, 11:25  
References: Email-41  
Body:  
Dermot,

You asked me to sanity-check claim 1 before the PCT goes out. The claim is fine as a description of what the codec does. Claim 1 is the shared exponent across a group of object position vectors with per-object mantissa differentials, and the dependents cover the group-size adaptation and the error resilience. That is the invention as I understand it and the words match the code.

I need to be straight with you about provenance, because you should hear it from me now rather than from someone else later.

The shared exponent did not come from San Mateo. At the Shanghai integration week, the week of October 15, 2012, we put the bit budget on the whiteboard on the Tuesday — 48 kbps of metadata against a transport layer that could carry about ten. Han Liheng's people came back Wednesday morning with the grouped shared-exponent scheme drawn out. Shao Min wrote the first fixed-point implementation that week and it is in their commit history as r2871. Solveig took it from there and did the group-size adaptation and the error resilience, and that is the part I would call inventive over what was on the whiteboard.

I am not telling you their engineers are inventors on the claim we have drafted. I am telling you that if anyone ever asks who thought of the shared exponent, the honest answer is that it came off a whiteboard in Zhangjiang with four Chenwei engineers in the room, and there is a photograph of that whiteboard in the integration week minutes that Chenwei circulated to both sides.

Tell me if that changes anything about how we file. Han also wrote to me in March asking how this gets handled and I have not answered him.

Wendell Aduba-Reiss
Director, Standards
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-45**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Cathal Pellworm <c.pellworm@pellworm-sarno.com>  
Cc: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Renewals services agreement - Annex A jurisdiction schedule - proposed 2013 revision  
Date: May 22, 2013, 14:15  
References:  
Body:  
Dear Ingrid,

Attached in the portal is the proposed revision of Annex A to the renewals services agreement, for signature by June 28.

Annex A is the jurisdiction schedule. It currently lists forty-one jurisdictions in three service categories: Category 1 full instruction and payment, Category 2 payment on your written instruction only, Category 3 monitoring and reminder without payment authority. Our fee table is keyed to the category, so Annex A is the only document that determines what we are authorized to do and what we invoice.

Proposed changes for 2013: add Brazil and Indonesia in Category 2; move India from Category 2 to Category 1 at your request following last year's near miss; remove Ukraine, where you have no live case.

Two reminders that come up every year when clients read the schedule. We charge nothing against a jurisdiction in Annex A where no fee falls due, so the pending Japanese, Korean and Chinese applications on the list generate no annuity line at all — those offices take nothing until registration. The European Patent Office is the opposite case and clients are regularly surprised: renewal fees fall due on the pending application from the third year, and Annex A therefore carries EP twice, once for applications and once for the national validations after grant.

Please confirm the two additions with your cost center reference so we can bill correctly from July 1.

Kind regards,
Cathal Pellworm
Client Services Director
Pellworm & Sarno Renewals Ltd

**Email-46**  
To: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Almut Pfannenstiel <pfannenstiel@pfannenstiel-ip.de>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0932 (Münchener Serie) – Mitteilung nach Regel 71(3) EPÜ – Frist 23. September 2013  
Date: May 23, 2013, 11:25  
References: Email-23  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Hollerith,

die Prüfungsabteilung hat am 21. Mai 2013 die Mitteilung nach Regel 71(3) EPÜ erlassen. Die Viermonatsfrist endet damit am 23. September 2013. Die Frist ist nicht verlängerbar. Eine Weiterbehandlung wäre zwar möglich, ist aber teuer und peinlich, weshalb ich sie nicht einplane.

Innerhalb der Frist ist zu erledigen:

1. Entrichtung der Gebühr für die Erteilung und Veröffentlichung.
2. Einreichung der Übersetzungen der Ansprüche in die beiden anderen Amtssprachen, also ins Englische und ins Französische. Das Verfahren wird in deutscher Sprache geführt.
3. Erklärung des Einverständnisses mit der Fassung des Druckexemplars.

Zum Druckexemplar. Die Prüfungsabteilung hat in Anspruch 1 "im Wesentlichen linear" durch "linear" ersetzt und in Anspruch 7 einen Rückbezug auf Anspruch 3 in einen Rückbezug auf Anspruch 2 geändert. Die zweite Änderung ist eine offensichtliche Berichtigung. Die erste halte ich für nachteilig, weil sie den Schutzbereich ohne Not verengt.

Wollen Sie die erste Änderung nicht hinnehmen, müssten wir eine Änderung beantragen. Das bedeutet eine zweite Mitteilung nach Regel 71(3) und etwa fünf Monate Verzögerung, und die Prüfungsabteilung hat in diesem Verfahren bisher wenig Neigung gezeigt, sich umstimmen zu lassen.

Meine Empfehlung lautet: hinnehmen. Der Schutzbereich, auf den es wirtschaftlich ankommt, liegt ohnehin in den abhängigen Ansprüchen 9 bis 14, und diese sind unverändert geblieben.

Bitte beachten Sie ferner, dass die Jahresgebühr für das dritte Jahr am 30. September 2013 fällig wird und unabhängig vom Stand des Erteilungsverfahrens zu entrichten ist, solange der Hinweis auf die Erteilung nicht vorher im Patentblatt veröffentlicht wurde.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen

Dr. Almut Pfannenstiel
Patentanwältin
Pfannenstiel Patentanwaltskanzlei, München

**Email-47**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: US 13/456,102 (MAL-2011-0827) - inventorship correction under 37 CFR 1.48  
Date: July 09, 2013, 08:55  
References:  
Body:  
Ingrid,

We need to correct inventorship on 13/456,102 before the examiner issues a notice of allowance, and I would rather do it now than deal with it after issue.

The background is that the March amendment narrowed claim 1 to the adaptive smoothing embodiment. That embodiment came from Rafferty Osei-Bonsu, who is not a named inventor because at filing the claims were directed to the block-switching embodiment only. Once we narrowed, he became an inventor of what is claimed. Under 35 U.S.C. 116 inventorship follows the claims as they stand, so the correction is required, not optional.

The mechanics changed with the AIA rules that took effect on September 16, 2012, and the old practice notes in our template are wrong. What is needed now is a request under 37 CFR 1.48(a), a corrected application data sheet identifying the complete inventive entity with the added inventor marked, an oath or declaration from Mr. Osei-Bonsu, and the processing fee of $130 under 1.17(i). There is no longer any requirement to state that the error arose without deceptive intent, so please delete that paragraph from the template rather than adapting it.

Please also flag the foreign counterparts. The EP designation of inventor needs a corrected Rule 19 designation with a statement of derivation. The JP case is unaffected because the applicant is the company and the inventor designation there is not claim-dependent, but tell Tsuzuki-sensei anyway so the two files agree.

Target filing date July 19.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-48**  
To: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Divisional filed - MAL-2010-0392D - endpoint apparatus claims out of the beamforming case  
Date: July 18, 2013, 14:50  
References: Email-31  
Body:  
Adrienne,

Thank you for filing the divisional yesterday. Recorded here as follows.

Parent: US 13/583,900, national phase of PCT/US2011/027655, docket MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series.
Divisional: filed July 17, 2013, claims directed to the endpoint apparatus rather than to the method.
Docket: MAL-2010-0392D.

The letter suffix is the docket group’s convention and I have used it because it is the convention, but I want to register that I dislike it. A trailing letter is invisible in a spreadsheet column formatted as text and truncated, it survives copy and paste badly, and it reads like a document-type marker rather than part of an identifier. We already have four-digit numbers colliding across three series. Adding a character that people will read as noise is not an improvement on that.

So, for the avoidance of doubt and for whoever reads this file in ten years: MAL-2010-0392D is the divisional of the San Mateo beamforming family and nothing else. It is not a suffixed reference to a matter in another series, it is not a document type, and the D does not stand for anything except divisional.

A terminal disclaimer will be needed if the examiner raises obviousness-type double patenting over the parent, which I expect he will. Please do not file one preemptively.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-49**  
To: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 陈慕青 <chen.muqing@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Cc: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 关于 JD-CHW-12 项目成果的专利申请安排  
Date: September 11, 2013, 16:40  
References:  
Body:  
Ostenfeld 先生，您好。

项目实施已满一年半，我司内部在做中期知识产权盘点，有一事想请贵司协助。

按照《联合开发协议》第五条，本项目实施过程中产生的一切发明创造，无论是否属于附件A所列工作包的范围，均由双方共同所有，任何一方不得单独以自己名义申请专利。这一条在签约时双方均无异议。

我司这一年半内未就本项目成果单独提交任何专利申请，相关技术交底书均已按第七条报备至贵司项目经理。但我司迄今未收到贵司方面的任何对应报备，也未在公开数据库中检索到以双方共同名义提交的申请。

因此想请贵司提供两项材料：一、自2012年4月1日起，贵司就本项目成果已提交或计划提交的专利申请清单，含名称、申请日与申请人；二、若贵司认为某项成果不属于本协议范围，请说明理由，以便双方尽早厘清，避免日后争议。

我方研发副总裁韩立衡先生今年3月曾就分组共享指数方案的归属向贵司 Aduba-Reiss 先生询问，至今未获回复。这一项在我方内部被列为本项目最重要的技术贡献之一，因此我不得不再次提出。

烦请于9月30日前回复。若需当面沟通，我可安排在10月的项目例会上一并讨论。

顺颂商祺。

陈慕青
知识产权总监
辰威微电子有限公司

**Email-50**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 - no, the 2011 confirmation does not let us license the EP member alone  
Date: September 12, 2013, 10:40  
References: Email-10, Email-12  
Body:  
Roswell,

Short answer to Tuesday's question: no.

The confirmation Kaida signed on February 22, 2011 is limited to Japan by its own terms. I said so in March 2011 and the position has not changed, because nothing has happened since that would change it. The German prospect you are talking to would be taking a license under the EP member, and the EP member is not covered by that letter.

I appreciate that this is inconvenient and that two and a half years have gone by. It is still the same letter and it still says the same thing.

What we can do is go back to Shinonome Electric and ask for the extension Kaida himself offered to discuss. His covering note in 2011 expressly said the position outside Japan was for separate negotiation, so we are not opening a fight, we are taking up an invitation that has been sitting on the file since before anybody moved jobs. He is still in post. My guess is that they will want a number and that the number will be uncomfortable rather than absurd.

What we cannot do is sign a license on the theory that a Japan-limited non-assertion is close enough to a global one. If that came out in a dispute we would lose the license and the relationship in the same week, and I would deserve it.

Tomás, please make sure nothing goes in front of the prospect describing our rights in that family as unencumbered outside Japan.

And once more for the file, because I know exactly how these things get quoted back at people three years later: this concerns MAL-2009-0271. One family, one letter, one country. It says nothing whatever about any other matter in the portfolio, whoever the counterparty is.

Eleanor Bricknell
Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-51**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0117 (Munich series) / WO 2012/025431 - US restriction requirement, election due December 6  
Date: November 06, 2013, 15:55  
References:  
Body:  
Ingrid,

The US examiner on our Munich-originated case has issued a restriction requirement, and I would like San Mateo's view before I elect, because the answer is commercial rather than legal.

Family: MAL-2011-0117, Munich series. Adaptive port compensation for vented enclosures. Published as WO 2012/025431 A1 on March 1, 2012. Priority DE 10 2010 039 442.6 of August 20, 2010.

The examiner has split the claims three ways: the compensation method, the enclosure incorporating the compensation circuit, and the tuning procedure carried out at manufacture. One month to elect, so December 6.

My inclination is to elect the enclosure claims.
