The method claims are the ones we drafted first and the ones Ahlborn-Vesely cares about, but the enclosure is what a competitor ships and it is what a US court finds easiest to look at. The tuning procedure is worth having and we can chase it in a divisional if the enclosure claims come out cheaply.

Please also note the publication number carefully. WO 2012/025431 is ours. Twice this year I have been sent search results in which it had been confused with a differently ordered number in the same range belonging to somebody else entirely, and on the second occasion the confusion had already reached a slide that went to the business before anybody checked. I would rather be tedious about this now than explain it again in a meeting.

Beatrix Hollerith
European Patent Attorney
Marsden Europe GmbH, Munich

**Email-52**  
To: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
From: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
Cc: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 국내단계 진입 예정 통지 (2011년 5월 18일자 확인서 관련)  
Date: November 07, 2013, 09:35  
References: Email-15  
Body:  
한영정밀 주식회사
지식재산부장 김도윤 부장님

한울국제특허법률사무소 방지훈입니다. 늘 협조에 감사드립니다.

2011년 5월 18일자로 귀사가 발행하여 주신 확인서와 관련하여, 후속 절차를 사전에 알려 드립니다.

의뢰인 마스든은 본건 발명에 대하여 2011년 6월 17일 미국 가출원 61/498,320을, 2012년 6월 15일 국제출원 PCT/US2012/042911을 각각 단독 명의로 하였습니다. 국제공개는 2012년 12월 20일 WO 2012/174511 A1로 이루어졌습니다.

한국 국내단계 진입 기한은 우선일로부터 31개월인 2014년 1월 17일이며, 12월 중순에 번역문과 함께 진입할 예정입니다. 출원인은 확인서 제1항에서 확인하여 주신 바에 따라 마스든 단독 명의입니다. 심사청구는 국내단계 진입과 동시에 할 예정입니다.

또한 유럽특허청, 일본, 중국에 대하여도 동일하게 단독 명의로 진입합니다.

본건은 귀사의 확인서로 권리 귀속이 정리된 사안으로 처리하고 있으며, 별도로 회신하실 사항은 없습니다. 다만 향후 심사 과정에서 귀사 발행 자료가 인용되는 등의 사정이 생길 경우 지체 없이 알려 드리겠습니다.

감사합니다.

방지훈
변리사
한울국제특허법률사무소

**Email-53**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 拒絶理由通知書の受領と応答方針のご相談  
Date: November 26, 2013, 16:03  
References: Email-42  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

お世話になっております。都倉でございます。

標記出願（特願2013-510827）につき、本年11月21日付の拒絶理由通知書を受領いたしましたのでご報告申し上げます。応答期限は2014年1月20日でございます。

拒絶理由は二点でございます。

第一に、請求項1及び2について特許法第29条第2項（進歩性）でございます。引用文献1（特開2008-283305号公報）に長時間ラウドネスの積算処理が、引用文献2（米国特許出願公開第2009/0116664号明細書）にチャネル間相関に基づく音声区間検出がそれぞれ開示されており、両者の組合せは当業者にとって容易であるとの認定でございます。

第二に、請求項7ないし9について同法第36条第6項第2号（明確性）でございます。「暗騒音区間」の判定基準が特定されていないとのご指摘です。

第一点につきましては、引用文献2の区間検出は音声認識の前処理を目的とするものであり、ラウドネス積算からの除外という課題も効果も示されておりません。加えて、本願の適応閾値（請求項3）は引用文献のいずれにも記載がなく、これを請求項1に繰り上げる補正により進歩性を主張し得ると考えます。三雲様のご提案に係る部分でございますので、実験データのご提供をお願いできれば幸いでございます。

第二点は、明細書段落0048の定義を請求項に取り込むことで解消できると考えます。

手続補正書及び意見書の案を12月13日までにお送りいたします。両社のご確認をいただいたうえで、年明け早々に提出したく存じます。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-54**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
Cc: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 关于 PCT/US2014/010882 的中国保密审查问题（专利法第二十条）  
Date: January 14, 2014, 09:50  
References:  
Body:  
Achinstein 先生，您好。

贵司通知我所，上述国际申请已于2014年1月10日以美国专利商标局为受理局提交，优先权日为2013年1月15日（US 61/752,610），社内整理号 MAL-2012-0688。我所已建档。

在办理后续中国国家阶段之前，有一个前置问题需要贵司书面确认。

《中华人民共和国专利法》第二十条规定，任何单位或者个人将在中国完成的发明或者实用新型向外国申请专利的，应当事先报经国务院专利行政部门进行保密审查。违反该条规定向外国申请专利的，在中国申请专利的，不授予专利权。请注意，该条的适用取决于发明的实质性内容在何地完成，与申请人的国籍或者住所无关，也与是否使用了中国境内的设施无关。

我所之所以提出此点，是因为本申请说明书第 [0044] 至 [0051] 段所描述的分组共享指数编码方案，在贵司提供的技术交底材料中标注了"上海集成周"字样，且相关定点实现的提交记录来自辰威微电子的代码库。

因此，烦请贵司明确答复：本发明的实质性内容是否在中国境内完成？

若答复为否，请以书面形式说明发明人的工作地点与构思地点，我所存档即可。若答复为是，或者存在疑问，则应当立即补办保密审查请求（可就已提交的国际申请提出），并暂缓中国国家阶段的部分准备工作。

请于1月24日前回复为盼。

傅锦程
专利代理师
恒宇知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-55**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 배은지 <e.bae@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Subject: JD-HYP-10 공동개발계약 종료 준비 - 별지 A 작업패키지 완료 현황  
Date: February 06, 2014, 10:35  
References: Email-3  
Body:  
박세훈 매니저님,

한영정밀 지식재산팀 배은지입니다.

공동개발계약(JD-HYP-10)의 계약기간이 2014년 3월 31일자로 만료됩니다. 종료 절차를 준비하기 위하여 별지 A에 규정된 네 개 작업패키지의 완료 현황을 아래와 같이 정리하였으니 확인 부탁드립니다.

H-1 섀시 및 마그넷 어셈블리 통합: 2013년 6월 완료, 최종 보고서 제출 완료.
H-2 트랜스듀서 특성 측정: 2013년 11월 완료, 측정 설비는 계약 제11조에 따라 안산 공장에 잔류.
H-3 열시험 지그 설계: 미완료. 2월 28일까지 마무리 예정이며, 귀사 요코하마 측 시료 회신이 2월 14일까지 필요합니다.
H-4 양산 합격기준: 완료. 다만 관련 발명 한 건의 공동출원 절차가 진행 중입니다.

각 작업 패키지의 문서 및 시험보고서는 3월 14일 최종본을 제출할 예정입니다.

계약 제5조 및 제12조에 따라 종료 시점까지 양사가 산출물 목록을 서면으로 확정하여야 합니다. 별지 A에 기재되지 않은 항목은 목록에 포함시키지 않는 것이 원칙이므로, 지난해 별도로 진행한 방열 구조 검토는 제외하였습니다. 이견이 있으시면 알려주십시오.

회신은 2월 14일까지 부탁드립니다. 종료 확인서 초안은 3월 초에 송부하겠습니다.

감사합니다.

배은지
지식재산팀 차장
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-56**  
To: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0329 - final rejection under 103 - RCE, appeal, or let it go  
Date: March 12, 2014, 09:55  
References: Email-18  
Body:  
Dermot,

Final rejection mailed March 10 on the sample rate conversion case, US 13/704,215. All claims rejected under 103 over Yamagishi in view of Broussard-Iyengar.

The examiner has not moved at all on the error-feedback loop. His position after the interview is that Broussard-Iyengar paragraph [0044] discloses feedback on a delay estimate, that the only difference is where the summing node sits, and that relocating a summing node is a design choice. I disagreed with him for forty minutes. He was unfailingly polite and did not move an inch.

Three options.

RCE with the claim 9 limitation brought up into claim 1. Claim 9 requires the loop gain to be scheduled on the estimate variance, which is genuinely in neither reference and which I think gets allowed on the next action. It is also narrow enough that I want to hear from your side whether it is worth having.

Appeal. Two years, call it thirty thousand dollars, and I would put us at slightly better than even on the design-choice point, because the rejection does not articulate any reason a person of skill would have relocated the node.

Abandon. The technology is four years old now. If the product group is not shipping it, this is money spent on a certificate to hang on a wall.

I am obliged to say I have no view on the third option, because I do not know your business. Let me know by April 10 so we are not paying extension fees on top.

Adrienne Whitcomb
Whitcomb Reyes LLP

**Email-57**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 設定登録のご報告（特許第5498271号）  
Date: March 17, 2014, 11:40  
References: Email-53  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

お世話になっております。都倉でございます。

標記出願につきまして、2014年3月14日付で設定登録がなされましたのでご報告申し上げます。

・特許番号 特許第5498271号
・登録日 2014年3月14日
・特許権者 東雲電機株式会社、Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.（各二分の一）
・請求項数 15（補正により3項減）
・特許査定 2014年2月18日付

第一年分から第三年分までの特許料は、特許査定の謄本送達の日から三十日以内という期限に従い、2014年3月6日に一括して納付済みでございます。第四年分以降は毎年の納付となり、次回期限は2017年3月14日でございます。年金管理は従前どおり当事務所にて行い、費用は両社折半でご請求申し上げます。

登録原簿には共有者二名が記載されております。以後の取扱いにつき、念のため確認事項を申し添えます。

一、各共有者は、他の共有者の同意を得ることなく自ら本特許発明を実施することができます（特許法第73条第2項）。

二、他人に通常実施権を許諾するには、他の共有者全員の同意が必要でございます（同条第3項）。同意を欠く許諾は効力を生じません。

三、持分の譲渡及び質権の設定にも他の共有者全員の同意が必要でございます（同条第1項）。

対応外国出願（欧州特許庁、米国、韓国、中国）の状況は別途ご報告申し上げます。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-58**  
To: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 김도윤 <dy.kim@hanyoung-p.co.kr>, Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: JD-HYP-10 공동개발계약 기간 만료 및 종료 사무 안내  
Date: March 28, 2014, 16:20  
References: Email-32  
Body:  
방지훈 변리사님, 마스든 담당자님

한영정밀 오세라입니다.

2010년 1월 14일자 공동개발계약(JD-HYP-10)은 계약 제3조에 따라 2014년 3월 31일자로 유효기간이 만료됩니다. 양사 실무 협의 결과 연장은 하지 아니하기로 하였으므로, 종료에 따른 사무를 아래와 같이 정리하여 안내드립니다.

일. 최종 성과 보고서는 4월 18일까지 양사 공동 명의로 작성하여 각사 1부씩 보관합니다. 초안은 당사에서 준비하겠습니다.

이. 별지 A 작업 패키지 H-1 내지 H-4의 산출물 목록과 그 귀속은 최종 보고서 부록 1에 확정합니다. 현재까지 공동 소유로 정리된 산출물은 시험 지그 도면 3건과 합격기준 문서 1건입니다.

삼. 대여 시료 및 시험 장비는 4월 30일까지 상호 반송하거나 폐기 확인서를 교환합니다.

사. 계약 제12조 비밀유지 의무는 종료 후 5년간, 즉 2019년 3월 31일까지 존속합니다. 제13조 경업 제한은 종료와 동시에 소멸합니다.

오. 기간 중 발생한 비용 정산은 2분기 중 완료합니다.

기타 종료와 관련하여 확인이 필요한 사항이 있으시면 4월 11일까지 회신하여 주시기 바랍니다.

그동안 협조하여 주셔서 감사드립니다.

오세라
지식재산팀 차장
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-59**  
To: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 谭建华 <tan.jianhua@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Cc: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
Subject: JD-CHW-12：关于在附件A中增设第七工作包的建议  
Date: June 11, 2014, 10:55  
References: Email-25  
Body:  
Ostenfeld 先生，您好。

关于上月季度评审会上提出的功耗测量事项，我方正式建议在《联合开发协议》JD-CHW-12 的附件A中增设第七工作包。

拟增设内容为 A-7“跨工艺节点功耗对比测试”，作为附件A 第 A-6 项系统级功耗评估的补充，范围限于：统一的测量夹具规范、四类典型工作负载的定义、以及在 28nm 与 40nm 两个工艺节点上的对比测试流程。工期自 2014 年 9 月起至 2015 年 3 月止，即在本项目实施期限届满之前完成，费用按第八条各承担二分之一。

有两点请贵司一并确认。

第一，附件A的修订仅涉及工作范围与费用分摊，不改变第五条。按第五条，项目实施过程中产生的发明无论是否属于附件A所列范围，均由双方共有，各占二分之一份额。我方法务部希望在修订备忘录中重述这一点，以免日后被理解为附件A的新增内容才是共有的。

第二，A-7 的测试工作在上海完成。若由此产生发明并拟向外国申请专利，须先依《专利法》第二十条办理保密审查，请贵司美国同事不要直接以邮件方式将技术方案转往圣马特奥后再行申请。

请于 6 月 30 日前书面确认，以便我方安排预算。下次季度评审定于 7 月 16 日在上海举行。

此致
敬礼

谭建华
知识产权部部长
辰威微电子有限公司

**Email-60**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0392 - Japanese office action issued June 10 - response due September 10  
Date: June 18, 2014, 16:10  
References: Email-40  
Body:  
Dear Ingrid,

A notification of reasons for refusal was issued on Japanese application 2012-556301 on June 10, 2014. It reached this office on June 13. The response period for a foreign applicant is three months, so September 10, 2014, extendable by a further three months on request and payment of the extension fee.

The examiner raises two grounds.

Article 29(2), lack of inventive step, against claims 1 to 6 and 18, over JP-A-2008-193674 in the name of Konishiroku Onkyo combined with the same Kesselring application that your international search report cited. The reasoning is short and, if I may say so, not very careful. He asserts that combining the two is a matter of ordinary design without identifying any motivation to combine in either document.

Article 36(6)(ii), clarity, against claim 11. The term rendered in our translation as "substantially coherent" has no definition anywhere in the specification. This objection is fair and I would simply delete the word.

My proposal is a written opinion attacking the motivation to combine, filed together with an amendment bringing the coherence-threshold feature of claim 7 up into claim 1 and deleting the offending word from claim 11. I would not use the whole three-month period. I would like to file in mid-August so that the extension remains available if the examiner comes back quickly, which this examiner does.

An estimate follows separately from our accounts section.

One correction for your records. There is no annuity due on this application and none has ever been due. Annuities on a Japanese case begin only after the decision to grant, when the first three years are paid as a lump sum. I mention it because the docket sheet your predecessor sent me in 2013 carried a placeholder annuity entry against this application, and it is still there.

Yours sincerely,

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-61**  
To: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
From: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Watch notice - SHINONOME, Serial No. 86/342,117, class 9 - our view is no action  
Date: July 24, 2014, 16:50  
References:  
Body:  
Adrienne,

Our watch service flagged US application Serial No. 86/342,117 for the word mark SHINONOME in class 9, filed June 27, 2014 by Shinonome America, Inc. of Irvine, California. The thirty-day extension window after publication would run into early autumn, so I want our position recorded now rather than in September.

Shinonome America is the US sales subsidiary of the Osaka group and, as far as our records go, it holds the group's US trademark portfolio and nothing else. It is not a party to any Marsden agreement, it does not appear in any patent matter, and the last correspondence we have with it is a 2011 booth-sharing arrangement at a trade show.

The identified goods are loudspeaker drivers, amplifier modules and semiconductor components. None of our registered marks is close on sight, sound or meaning, and we do not sell into their channel. My recommendation is no opposition, no letter, no watch escalation.

The one thing I would like from you is a short memo I can put in the file saying why we did not act, because a trademark decision not to act tends to be re-litigated internally three years later by someone who has found the watch notice and not the reasoning. Please keep it to a page.

Please also make sure this does not get opened as a patent matter. It has happened before with this name.

Priyasha Nandakumar
IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-62**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Issued - US 8,824,693 B2 - MAL-2010-0392 (San Mateo series) - adaptive microphone-array beamforming  
Date: September 03, 2014, 08:35  
References: Email-31  
Body:  
All,

US 8,824,693 B2 issued yesterday, September 2, 2014, on the beamforming case. Twenty-three claims, three of them independent. Patent term adjustment 212 days.

Docket MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series. Inventors Hyacinth Delacroix and Bjørn Umbertsen. Assignee of record Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. Ownership status in PALLAS reads Sole and there is no program code on the matter. That is correct, and I have now confirmed it three separate times this year because people keep asking me, so please take this message as the answer.

Maintenance fees: 3.5 years, March 2, 2018; 7.5 years, September 2, 2021; 11.5 years, September 2, 2025. All three docketed with six-month and three-month warnings.

Foreign status for anyone who needs it. EP in examination and, according to Beatrix, close. JP under response to a first office action, with our reply going in next month. CN awaiting first action. The US divisional MAL-2010-0392D is in its first office action cycle.

Congratulations to Hyacinth and Bjørn, who have asked me for a status update roughly every six weeks since March 2010 and who may now stop.

Ingrid Solheim-Baptista
Patent Paralegal
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-63**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Shinonome - amendment No. 2 executed - SDX-5 family added, rate step-down above 12M units  
Date: November 19, 2014, 11:15  
References: Email-29  
Body:  
Roswell,

Amendment No. 2 to the patent license agreement with 東雲セミコンダクター株式会社 was executed on November 14 and takes effect January 1, 2015.

What it does:

Adds SDX-5A, SDX-5B and SDX-5C to the licensed products schedule, which closes the 2012 reporting argument on derivatives — the schedule now says expressly that a product designation appears in the statement if the die contains the licensed decoder block, whatever it is marketed as.

Introduces a rate step: USD 0.043 per unit up to 12,000,000 units in a calendar year, USD 0.037 above that. Their 2014 run rate is about 9.4 million, so the step is a 2016 problem on current volumes.

Tightens the audit right from once every three years to once every two, and gives us the cost if the audit finds under-reporting above three percent.

Leaves the term alone: December 31, 2019, with automatic three-year renewal unless either side gives notice six months out.

I did not get the territory clarification I wanted and I am recording that. Section 1.14 still defines Territory as "Japan and such other countries as the parties may agree," which does not describe what actually happens to their chips. Munich has views. I will come back to it at the next amendment rather than reopen a signed deal.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-64**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: EP 2 545 719 - MAL-2010-0392 - Rule 71(3) received, four-month clock to June 15  
Date: February 11, 2015, 14:45  
References: Email-31  
Body:  
Ingrid,

The Examining Division issued the Rule 71(3) communication on EP 2 545 719 yesterday, February 10. Four months, non-extendable, which after the ten-day rule puts us at June 22, 2015 in practice. Within that period we pay the fee for grant and publishing and file the claim translations into German and French. The proceedings are in English.

The text proposed for grant is acceptable. The Division has deleted the reference numerals from claim 1, which is cosmetic, and corrected an obvious typographical error in claim 16. I propose we approve without requesting amendment.

Two forward-looking points.

Grant will publish roughly four months after we pay, so late June. The opposition period runs nine months from the mention of grant in the Bulletin, which puts the opposition deadline in the second half of March 2016. I would like that diarized now rather than discovered later.

I raise it because of Kesselring Audiotechnik. Their 2008 application was the X reference in the international search report, and they have twice filed third-party observations in this file under Article 115, once in 2013 and again last April. Both sets were considered and neither changed the Division's mind. In my experience an applicant who takes the trouble to file observations twice is an applicant who opposes.

Validation states: DE, FR, GB, IT, NL, SE. Under the London Agreement no full translation is needed for DE, FR or GB, which is where the money would otherwise have gone.

Beatrix Hollerith
European Patent Attorney
Marsden Europe GmbH, Munich

**Email-65**  
To: Annika Vorderbrück <annika.vorderbrueck@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Coriolis-Programm - Annex A nach der Änderung der EP 1 892 703 - Frist 31. Mai  
Date: April 23, 2015, 09:30  
References: Email-22  
Body:  
Liebe Annika,

bitte übernehmen Sie die diesjährige Prüfung der von uns in Annex A eingebrachten Ansprüche. Annex A des Programmvertrags ist die Liste der als essenziell anerkannten Ansprüche; sie ist anspruchsgenau geführt, nicht patentgenau, und genau daraus entsteht das Problem.

Die EP 1 892 703 B1 ist im Einspruchsverfahren in geändertem Umfang aufrechterhalten worden. Anspruch 1 enthält nun das Merkmal der blockweisen Neuberechnung, Anspruch 7 ist gestrichen. Beide Ansprüche stehen aber noch in der Fassung von 2012 in Annex A. Solange die Liste nicht berichtigt ist, weist sie einen Anspruch aus, den es nicht mehr gibt, und einen Anspruch in einer Fassung, die nicht mehr gilt.

Zu tun ist Folgendes. Erstens: neue Essenzialitätsbegründung für den geänderten Anspruch 1 gegen Abschnitt 7.3.2 der Spezifikation, zwei Seiten, Formblatt des Programmadministrators. Zweitens: Streichungsmitteilung für Anspruch 7. Drittens: Prüfung, ob die B2-Schrift bereits veröffentlicht ist, da der Administrator die Fundstelle verlangt.

Frist ist der 31. Mai, keine Nachfrist. Was am 31. Mai nicht in Annex A steht, nimmt an der Verteilung für das laufende Jahr nicht teil, unabhängig davon, ob der Anspruch essenziell ist. Herr Aduba-Reiss braucht Ihre Fassung bis zum 18. Mai.

Viele Grüße
Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-66**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0392 - Japanese patent registered - 特許第5731049号  
Date: April 27, 2015, 09:25  
References: Email-60  
Body:  
Dear Ingrid,

The decision to grant issued on March 24 and registration was completed on April 24, 2015. The patent number is 特許第5731049号.

The amendment we filed in August was accepted without a second notification and the examiner did not pursue the clarity objection once the word was deleted. Claim 1 now carries the coherence-threshold feature. The granted set is 19 claims, down from the 25 we entered with.

Annuity position, which I set out in full because the placeholder entry I mentioned last June is still on your docket sheet and now needs replacing rather than deleting.

The annuities for the first three years must be paid as a single lump sum within thirty days of the decision to grant. That was done on April 8, before registration, and the receipt is with your accounts section. From the fourth year the annuity falls due annually. The fourth-year payment must be made by April 24, 2018, with a six-month grace period available on payment of a surcharge. I will send the standard reminder at six months and at three months.

To be clear about the pending period: no annuity was payable at any time while the application was pending, and no payment was made or missed.

The registered proprietor is Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. alone. There is no co-proprietor recorded and no license recorded on the register.

Please let me know whether you would like the certificate couriered to San Mateo or held in our safe here, as we do for most clients.

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-67**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: EP 2 187 634 B1 – Patent im Einspruchsverfahren widerrufen (mündliche Verhandlung vom 9. Juni 2015)  
Date: June 10, 2015, 17:05  
References:  
Body:  
Liebe Eleanor,

die mündliche Verhandlung vor der Einspruchsabteilung hat gestern in München stattgefunden. Das Patent wurde in vollem Umfang widerrufen. Ich bedaure das Ergebnis, es kam allerdings nach dem vorläufigen Bescheid vom Februar nicht überraschend.

Zum Verfahrensgang. Der Hinweis auf die Erteilung wurde am 27. Februar 2013 veröffentlicht. Die Lautwerk Systemtechnik GmbH hat am 26. November 2013 Einspruch eingelegt, also innerhalb der Neunmonatsfrist. Geltend gemacht wurden mangelnde erfinderische Tätigkeit nach Artikel 100 a) EPÜ und unzulässige Erweiterung nach Artikel 100 c) EPÜ.

Der Hauptantrag ist an der erfinderischen Tätigkeit gescheitert, ausgehend von D3 in Verbindung mit dem allgemeinen Fachwissen. Die Hilfsanträge 1 und 2 wurden nach Artikel 123 (2) EPÜ beanstandet, weil das Merkmal der gleitenden Fensterlänge in der ursprünglichen Anmeldung nur im Zusammenhang mit der Zweikanalausführung offenbart war. Der in der Verhandlung eingereichte Hilfsantrag 3 wurde nicht zugelassen, da verspätet und nicht prima facie gewährbar.

Zum weiteren Vorgehen. Die Beschwerde ist innerhalb von zwei Monaten nach Zustellung der schriftlichen Entscheidung einzulegen, die Begründung innerhalb von vier Monaten. Die Beschwerde hat aufschiebende Wirkung, das Patent besteht also vorläufig fort. Die Jahresgebühren in DE, FR, GB, IT und NL sind daher weiter zu entrichten; ich habe die Anweisung an den Dienstleister gestern bestätigt.

Meine Einschätzung der Erfolgsaussichten liegt bei etwa dreißig Prozent, die Kosten bis zur Verhandlung bei rund 55.000 EUR. Ich bitte um Entscheidung bis zum 20. Juli.

Viele Grüße
Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-68**  
To: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2012-0688 中国国家阶段进入安排（期限2015年7月15日）  
Date: July 09, 2015, 13:15  
References: Email-54  
Body:  
Nandakumar 女士，您好。

上述案件进入中国国家阶段的期限为2015年7月15日，即自优先权日2013年1月15日起三十个月。我所计划于7月13日（周一）提交，留出两日缓冲。若贵司需要更多时间，可以缴纳宽限费延至三十二个月，但本案无此必要。

已完成事项如下。说明书中文译文共11,400字，权利要求20项，其中独立权利要求1项。超出10项的部分需缴纳附加费，每项150元，合计1,500元。译文已由我所复核，第 [0044] 至 [0051] 段关于分组共享指数的表述与英文本严格对应，未作意译。

实质审查请求可在进入时一并提出，也可在自国际申请日起三年内即2017年1月10日前提出。建议在进入时一并提出，以便尽早排队。

关于申请人。依照贵司2014年1月20日的书面答复，本发明的实质性内容在圣马特奥完成，故不涉及第二十条保密审查问题，我所已将该答复归档。申请人一栏按贵司指示仅列 Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. 一方。委托书上的申请人名称、地址须与国际公布文本完全一致，请贵司再次核对后于7月10日前回签。

另需提醒，本案的国际公布文本 WO 2014/110488 A1 于2014年7月17日公布，自公布日起在中国享有临时保护，如需主张，请留存证据。

顺祝商祺。

傅锦程
专利代理师
恒宇知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-69**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - Shinonome consent obtained for the Ardsley license  
Date: July 09, 2015, 13:22  
References: Email-57  
Body:  
Roswell,

Shinonome Electric's written consent for the Ardsley Broadcast Systems license came through Yodogawa yesterday, dated July 6. Signed by Kaida-san for the IP department and countersigned by their legal department. The scan is in the docket mailbox and the original is on its way to San Mateo.

To recap why we needed it. MAL-2011-0455 is jointly owned with Shinonome Electric in equal shares. Under Article 73(3) of the Japanese Patent Act neither co-owner can grant a license to a third party without the written consent of the other, and a license granted without it simply has no effect. Article 7 of the JD-SHN-09 agreement says the same thing and adds a fifteen business day response window, of which they used eleven.

The consent is drafted narrowly, which is normal for them. It covers Ardsley only, the JP, US, EP and KR members of the family, broadcast encoding products, the life of the license agreement, and it is not transferable to an Ardsley affiliate without a fresh consent. Shinonome takes fifty percent of net royalties, paid semi-annually within forty-five days of our own receipt, per Article 8.

Nothing here is a surprise and nothing was difficult. I flag it only so that Licensing plans around it. Assume six to eight weeks for a consent on this family and do not paper a term sheet that promises a signature date inside that window.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-70**  
To: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: JD-CHW-12 cost reconciliation - the derived Ownership Status values, and two that do not match the filings  
Date: February 26, 2016, 10:30  
References:  
Body:  
Ingrid,

I was reconciling the Chenwei cost bookings against the new matter list this morning, one week after the February 19 cutover, and I fell over something I do not have the authority to fix. I am sending it to you because I do not know who else it belongs to.

Ownership Status in the new system is a controlled field with three permitted values, Sole, Joint and Licensed-In, and it cannot be left empty. The old system had no such field. What it had was a free-text note headed OWNERSHIP BASIS which had no target field in the new schema and was not carried across at all. So the value had to be derived, and the rule in section 4.3 of the cutover scope note is that any matter carrying a JD- program code migrates as Joint, with the counterparty taken from the program record and the share defaulted to fifty percent, and any matter without one migrates as Sole.

Seven matters carry program code JD-CHW-12. All seven now read Joint, Chenwei Microsystems Co., Ltd., 50%.

Five of those seven were in fact filed jointly and the derived value happens to be right. Two were filed in Marsden's name alone and are pending in Marsden's name alone in every office: MAL-2012-0688 and MAL-2013-0402. For those two the record now says one thing and the filing says another.

I have left the derived values as they stand. I do not have authority to change a controlled field on an ownership question and I would not want to exercise it if I did. Please tell me which is wrong, the record or the filing, and who signs off on the answer.

One reason this is not cosmetic: the Ownership Status field is now the source for the quarterly royalty schedule and for the license representation report.

I should be clear about what I have and have not done, because "Yolande checked the ownership fields" is the kind of sentence that gets repeated after it stops being true. I have not checked anything. There is no post-cutover review of Ownership Status, on these seven or on the other fifty-four matters that carry a JD- program code, and none is planned that I know of. I found these two because I happened to be in the Chenwei folder for a cost reason. Nobody has looked at the rest, and on the present plan nobody will.

Yolande Grimbeek
IP Operations Analyst
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-71**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>, Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - the Article 73(3) consent alert is now live in Veritan  
Date: March 09, 2016, 15:44  
References: Email-57  
Body:  
All,

Licensing asked for this before the next consent request goes out, so it is built and this is what it does.

MAL-2011-0455 is the encoder-side loudness family we hold jointly with 東雲電機株式会社 / Shinonome Electric Co., Ltd., fifty percent each, and it is one of only three registered Japanese patents we hold with two proprietors on the face of the register. Anyone who opens a licensing task against this family now gets a blocking alert and cannot save the task until they acknowledge it.

The wording, so that nobody is surprised by it in front of a business client: "Jointly owned. A license to a third party requires the prior written consent of the co-owner under Article 73(3) of the Japanese Patent Act and Article 7 of the JD-SHN-09 agreement. Request through the Japanese associate. Allow six to eight weeks."

Two things this alert is not. It is not a check on the Ownership Status field, and it does not fire on anything except a licensing task. I built it by hand for this one family because Licensing asked for this one family. If you want it on another matter, send me the matter number and I will build that one too; nobody is going through the portfolio looking for candidates.

For the avoidance of doubt on the field itself: Ownership Status in Veritan is a controlled field with exactly three values, Sole, Joint and Licensed-In, and it cannot be left empty. If a matter you work on shows a value that does not match its register position, raise it with Ingrid and me before you edit it, because the field is audited and every change writes a reason code.

Yolande Grimbeek
IP Operations Analyst
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-72**  
To: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Almut Pfannenstiel <pfannenstiel@pfannenstiel-ip.de>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: EP 2 545 719 – Einspruch der Kesselring Audiotechnik GmbH vom 18. März 2016  
Date: March 22, 2016, 08:40  
References: Email-64  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Hollerith,

es ist eingetreten, was Sie im Februar vergangenen Jahres vorhergesagt haben. Die Kesselring Audiotechnik GmbH hat am 18. März 2016 Einspruch gegen das europäische Patent EP 2 545 719 B1 eingelegt. Die Einspruchsfrist wäre am 24. März abgelaufen, also sechs Tage später. Vertreter der Einsprechenden ist Herr Dr. Steinkamp von Steinkamp & Rühl.

Einspruchsgründe: Artikel 100 a) EPÜ, mangelnde Neuheit und mangelnde erfinderische Tätigkeit, sowie Artikel 100 b) EPÜ, unzureichende Offenbarung.

Entgegenhaltungen sind die bereits im internationalen Recherchenbericht genannte eigene Anmeldung der Einsprechenden aus dem Jahr 2008, zwei neue Dokumente, nämlich eine Diplomarbeit der TU Ilmenau aus dem Jahr 2007 und ein Konferenzbeitrag der AES-Convention 2009 in München, sowie eine behauptete offenkundige Vorbenutzung auf der Frankfurter Fachmesse im April 2009.

Eine erste Einschätzung, ohne dass ich die Dokumente vollständig ausgewertet hätte.

Der Einwand nach Artikel 100 b) ist nach meinem Dafürhalten aussichtslos. Die Einsprechende trägt vor, der Kohärenzschwellwert sei nicht ausführbar offenbart. Die Absätze [0038] bis [0044] der Beschreibung sagen jedoch genau, wie er bestimmt wird, einschließlich eines Zahlenbeispiels.

Die Diplomarbeit ist das ernstzunehmende Dokument. Ich habe sie noch nicht vollständig in Händen, die Einsprechende legt lediglich einen Auszug von vier Seiten vor. Wir sollten unverzüglich ein vollständiges Exemplar bei der Universitätsbibliothek anfordern und vor allem klären, ab welchem Datum die Arbeit tatsächlich öffentlich zugänglich war. Bei Hochschulschriften ist das erfahrungsgemäß die schwächste Stelle des Vortrags.

Die behauptete Vorbenutzung ist allein durch eine eidesstattliche Versicherung eines eigenen Mitarbeiters der Einsprechenden belegt. Nach ständiger Rechtsprechung der Beschwerdekammern genügt das in aller Regel nicht.

Die Erwiderungsfrist beträgt vier Monate ab Zustellung der Aufforderung durch die Einspruchsabteilung. Ich schlage vor, wir besprechen kommende Woche die Arbeitsteilung.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen

Dr. Almut Pfannenstiel
Patentanwältin
Pfannenstiel Patentanwaltskanzlei, München

**Email-73**  
To: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Ingrid Solheim-Baptista <ingrid.solheim-baptista@marsdenlabs.com>, Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Renewal Responsibility derived wrong on 94 matters - two Brazilian cases nearly went  
Date: April 13, 2016, 15:20  
References: Email-70  
Body:  
Docket,

Another derived field, and this one nearly cost us a patent.

Renewal Responsibility in the new system was derived from the old system's billing-party code. That code recorded who got invoiced, not who was instructed to pay. Where the annuity provider was invoiced, the migration wrote "Provider - standing instruction." Where the local agent was invoiced, it wrote "Local agent - case by case." Those are not the same distinction and on 94 matters the derived value is wrong.

We found it because two Brazilian cases surfaced on the INPI grace-period list. Both read "Provider - standing instruction." The provider had never been given an instruction on either, because historically we paid Brazil through the local agent and invoiced it back. Nobody was watching them. Both were inside the six-month grace period and I have paid them with the surcharge, BRL 1,140 in total, so there is no loss. Confirmation of payment is on both matter records.

Remediation. I have exported all 94 sorted by next due date. Eleven fall within the next 90 days and I am working those first with the responsible attorneys. The remainder I will clear by the end of Q3 and I will re-verify each against the local agent's own diary rather than against our record, because our record is the thing that is wrong.

This is the third derived field we have had to unwind since the cutover. I would like us to stop treating a derived value as a verified one, and I would like the derivation rules published somewhere the attorneys can read them.

Yolande Grimbeek
IP Operations Analyst
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-74**  
To: 谭建华 <tan.jianhua@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
From: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: JD-CHW-12：项目期间（2014—2015）发明披露的权属认定  
Date: June 08, 2016, 09:55  
References: Email-25  
Body:  
谭部长：

您好。我于本年三月接手上海的知识产权事务。项目实施期限已于2015年3月31日届满，我方现已完成 JD-CHW-12 项目 2014 年至 2015 年期间发明披露的复核，共 12 件，作为结项清点函告如下，供贵司项目档案备查。

一、认定标准。附件A 列有六个工作包。我方按发明的主题是否落入其中任一工作包判断权属：落入的，为双方共有，各占二分之一份额；未落入的，由完成该发明的一方单独所有。这与我司其他同类合作协议的处理方式一致。

二、认定结果。12 件中，3 件的主题落入附件A 第 A-2、A-5 工作包范围，认定为双方共有。其余 9 件的主题不在附件A 任何工作包范围之内，认定为完成方单独所有，其中 7 件由我司圣马特奥及横浜团队完成，2 件由贵司完成。清单另附。

三、后续处理。共有的 3 件拟以双方名义共同申请。依《专利法》第十五条，共有人对权利的行使有约定的，从其约定；没有约定的，共有人可以单独实施或者以普通许可方式许可他人实施该专利，收取的使用费应当在共有人之间分配。共有部分的年费按第八条各承担二分之一。

四、在中国境内完成的 4 件，均已办理保密审查后方向外国申请，请贵司代理机构核对受理通知。

顺颂
商祺

陆嘉宁
知识产权顾问
马斯登（上海）技术有限公司

**Email-75**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Annika Vorderbrück <annika.vorderbrueck@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 / EP 2 389 013 B1 - Lizenzanfrage Rehberg und das Zustimmungserfordernis in Deutschland  
Date: June 21, 2016, 11:05  
References:  
Body:  
Roswell, Tomás,

zur Anfrage der Rehberg Akustiksysteme GmbH, Ingolstadt, die Frau Dr. Waldherr am 14. Juni an Tomás gerichtet hat. Rehberg möchte eine nicht ausschließliche Lizenz am deutschen Teil von EP 2 389 013 B1 für Infotainment-Systeme im Automobilbereich. Ich schreibe auf Deutsch, weil die einschlägigen Fundstellen deutsche sind und ich sie sonst zweimal übersetzen müsste.

Der entscheidende Punkt ist die Inhaberschaft. Die zugrunde liegende Anmeldung PCT/US2010/053421 hat Marsden 2010 allein im eigenen Namen eingereicht, und auch der Hinweis auf die Erteilung vom 17. September 2014 nennt nur uns. Shinonome Electric hat jedoch Ende 2014 einen Anteil am europäischen Teil geltend gemacht, weil ihr damaliger Münchner Entsandter an den außerhalb Japans beanspruchten Merkmalen mitgewirkt hatte. San Mateo hat den Anspruch nach Prüfung der Laborunterlagen anerkannt. Seit der Umschreibung vom 3. Juni 2015 ist EP 2 389 013 B1 daher auf zwei Inhaber eingetragen: Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. und Shinonome Electric Co., Ltd.

Artikel 74 EPÜ verweist für das europäische Patent als Gegenstand des Vermögens auf das nationale Recht des jeweiligen Vertragsstaats. In Deutschland gilt damit die Bruchteilsgemeinschaft nach den §§ 741 ff. BGB. Die Erteilung einer Lizenz ist eine Verfügung über den gemeinschaftlichen Gegenstand und bedarf nach § 747 Satz 2 BGB der Zustimmung sämtlicher Teilhaber. Ohne die Zustimmung von Shinonome können wir Rehberg keine wirksame Lizenz erteilen.

Ich weiß, dass zu dieser Familie eine Bestätigung von Herrn Kaida vom 22. Februar 2011 in der Akte liegt, und ich weiß auch, dass sie hier nicht weiterhilft. Sie ist ausdrücklich auf 「日本国内において」 beschränkt, also auf das Gebiet Japans. Für Deutschland sagt sie nichts. Yokohama hat mir das gestern noch einmal bestätigt.

Wir brauchen also eine gesonderte Zustimmung. Ich schlage vor, dass Frau Minagawa die Anfrage in Osaka stellt, bevor Tomás gegenüber Rehberg irgendeinen Zeitplan nennt.

Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-76**  
To: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
From: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 欧州特許（ドイツ）に係る第三者への実施許諾に関する同意のお願い  
Date: July 14, 2016, 09:40  
References: Email-75  
Body:  
東雲電機株式会社
知的財産部 部長 海田 隆志 様

いつもお世話になっております。淀川国際特許事務所の都倉でございます。

Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. の依頼により、標記の件についてお願いを申し上げます。

対象は、貴社と同社との共有に係る欧州特許 EP 2 389 013 B1（2014年9月17日 特許付与の告示、国際出願 PCT/US2010/053421 は同社単独名義での出願、2015年6月3日付で貴社を共有者として登録済み、発明の名称「ダイアログ強調のためのメタデータ伝送」）のドイツ国における部分でございます。

このたび Rehberg Akustiksysteme GmbH（本社 インゴルシュタット市）より、車載インフォテインメント用途に限定した非独占的実施権の申入れがございました。同社は当該用途の量産をドイツ国内で行っております。

ドイツ法におきましては、欧州特許条約第74条により財産権としての取扱いは各締約国の国内法によるものとされ、ドイツにおける共有はドイツ民法第741条以下の持分共同体となります。実施許諾は共有物の処分に当たり、同法第747条第2文により共有者全員の同意を要すると解されております。したがいまして、貴社の書面によるご同意なくしては有効な実施許諾を行うことができません。

なお、2011年2月22日付で海田部長よりご発出いただきました確認書は、その文言上「日本国内において」と明記されており、ドイツ国における本件には及ばないものと理解しております。この理解に相違がございましたらご指摘賜りたく存じます。

ご検討いただきたい事項は、同意の可否、対価の要否及び額、並びに用途・期間・再実施許諾の可否に関する条件でございます。9月上旬までに一次のご回答を賜れますと幸いでございます。

何卒よろしくお願い申し上げます。

都倉 志乃
弁理士
淀川国際特許事務所

**Email-77**  
To: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 郑慧敏 <zheng.huimin@huachen-ip.cn>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0392 案 第一次审查意见通知书 答复期限2016年12月19日  
Date: August 10, 2016, 11:05  
References: Email-36  
Body:  
陆律师：

您好。国家知识产权局已于2016年8月2日发出本案第一次审查意见通知书，我所于8月8日收到正式文本。答复期限为四个月，自推定收到日起算，即2016年12月17日届满；因该日为星期六，实际顺延至12月19日星期一。

案件信息如下。申请号201280054318.7，国际申请日2012年11月2日，优先权日2011年11月10日，发明名称"用于对象元数据的比特流差错隐藏"，贵司卷号MAL-2011-0392，申请人为马斯登音频实验室公司，唯一申请人，无共同申请人。

审查员共提出三点意见。

第一，权利要求1至4不具备创造性，适用专利法第二十二条第三款。对比文件1为US 2009/0287482 A1，对比文件2为一篇1998年的会议论文。审查员认为，将对比文件2的插值方案应用于对比文件1所述系统属于本领域技术人员的常规选择。

第二，权利要求7中"大致同步"的表述不清楚，适用专利法第二十六条第四款。此点我建议直接删除该修饰语，不必争辩。

第三，说明书第0052段中有一处附图标记引用错误，属形式缺陷，一并修改即可。

关于第一点，我的意见是审查员并未说明将对比文件2的插值方案移植到对象元数据场景的技术启示何在。对比文件2处理的是波形样本，其数据结构和差错特性与元数据字段差异明显，插值在元数据上会产生语义无效的中间值，这一点说明书第0038段已有说明。建议在意见陈述书中重点论述，同时将从属权利要求5的"基于置信度加权"特征并入权利要求1，以提高授权把握。

另请注意本案卷号中的年份。贵司另有一件卷号后四位相同但年份为2010的案件，去年年底我所收到的一份费用清单曾将两者混淆，虽未造成实际损失，但核对花了不少时间。今后往来文件中请务必写明完整卷号。

请于9月30日前给予指示，以便我方准备答复文本。费用估算另函奉上。

顺颂
商祺

郑慧敏
专利代理人
北京华辰知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-78**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 ドイツにおける実施許諾同意の件（一次回答）  
Date: September 08, 2016, 16:25  
References: Email-76  
Body:  
淀川国際特許事務所
弁理士 都倉 志乃 先生

お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部の海田でございます。7月14日付のご照会につき、社内の一次検討が終わりましたのでご回答申し上げます。

まず前提の確認でございますが、2011年2月22日付の当社確認書が日本国内に限られる旨のご理解は、そのとおりでございます。当時、当社は欧州及び米国における共有持分を留保する前提で文言を調整しており、確認書第1項冒頭に「日本国内において」と記載したのはその趣旨によるものでございます。この点に争いはございません。

そのうえで、同意の可否についてでございます。

一、Rehberg 社は当社の直接の競合ではなく、車載インフォテインメント分野において当社は現在ドイツ国内で事業を行っておりません。したがいまして同意すること自体に原則的な支障はございません。

二、ただし無償での同意は困難でございます。本件特許は当社の共有に属し、ドイツ法上、当社の同意がなければ実施許諾が成立しない以上、当社の持分の経済的価値に見合う対価をいただきたく存じます。社内の検討では、一時金として四十万ユーロ前後を想定しております。

三、条件といたしましては、用途を車載インフォテインメントに限定すること、地域をドイツ連邦共和国に限定すること、再実施許諾を認めないこと、及び Rehberg 社の関係会社への拡張には別途の同意を要することを求めます。

四、同意はドイツ部分に限るものとし、フランス、英国、イタリア、オランダの各部分については本件同意の対象外といたします。

正式回答は、知的財産委員会（10月19日開催予定）の議を経たうえで差し上げます。金額につきましては協議の余地がございます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部 部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-79**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Shinonome-Lizenz - Gebietsumfang bei Einfuhren nach Deutschland  
Date: September 22, 2016, 08:20  
References: Email-63  
Body:  
Lieber Tomás,

ich komme auf den Punkt zurück, den Sie 2014 offengelassen haben, weil er jetzt praktisch geworden ist.

Ein Distributor in Ratingen bietet Module mit dem Baustein SDX-5B an. Der Baustein stammt von der 東雲セミコンダクター株式会社 (Shinonome Semiconductor Co., Ltd., Kumamoto), unserer Lizenznehmerin, nicht von einem der anderen Unternehmen dieser Firmengruppe. Die Module gehen an zwei deutsche Fahrzeugzulieferer.

Ziffer 1.14 des Lizenzvertrags definiert das Vertragsgebiet als "Japan and such other countries as the parties may agree". Weitere Länder sind nie vereinbart worden. Damit stellt sich die Frage, ob die Einfuhr nach Deutschland überhaupt lizenziert ist.

Meine Einschätzung: die Lizenznehmerin stellt in Japan her, dort ist die Herstellung lizenziert, und die von ihr in Verkehr gebrachten Chips sind insoweit erschöpft, soweit unsere japanischen Schutzrechte betroffen sind. Für die europäischen Patente derselben Familien gilt das gerade nicht, denn eine Inverkehrbringung in Japan erschöpft die europäischen Rechte nicht. Formal betrachtet verletzt der Distributor also unsere DE-Teile, und die Lizenznehmerin hat ihm etwas verkauft, das sie ihm für dieses Gebiet nicht verkaufen durfte.

Kommerziell will das niemand verfolgen. Vertraglich sollten wir es aber bei der nächsten Änderungsvereinbarung geradeziehen, entweder durch Erweiterung des Gebiets gegen einen Aufschlag oder durch eine ausdrückliche Ausfuhrbeschränkung. Bitte nehmen Sie es in Ihre Liste für die Verhandlung 2017 auf.

Viele Grüße
Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-80**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 - Rehberg consent negotiation, where we are and what it costs  
Date: November 22, 2016, 13:50  
References: Email-78  
Body:  
Roswell, Beatrix,

Status on the Shinonome consent for the German part of EP 2 389 013 B1.

Their opening number in September was EUR 400,000 as a lump sum. After two rounds through Tokura-sensei we are at EUR 310,000, and their IP committee has that figure in front of it. Conditions are field limited to automotive infotainment, territory limited to Germany, no sublicensing, no extension to Rehberg affiliates without a fresh consent, and expressly no effect on the French, British, Italian or Dutch parts.

The commercial arithmetic. Rehberg's forecast unit volume gives us running royalties of roughly EUR 1.15 million over the five year term at the rate on the table. Paying EUR 310,000 out of that to unlock it is not attractive but it is not fatal, and there is no alternative structure. We cannot license around a co-owner in Germany. We cannot wait them out either, because Rehberg have told us plainly that they will design to the competing approach if we cannot give them a signed license by the end of Q1.

Two lessons for the portfolio, and I would like them written down somewhere Licensing will find them.

First, on any jointly owned family the consent is a cost of the deal and belongs in the term sheet economics from day one, not discovered in month four.

Second, a confirmation letter is worth exactly what its own words say and not one jurisdiction more. The 2011 letter on this family is limited to Japan on its face. It has done its job in Japan perfectly well. It was never going to do anything anywhere else, and nobody should have assumed otherwise.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-81**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Subject: 担当変更のご連絡（共同開発関連の窓口について）  
Date: November 22, 2016, 16:50  
References:  
Body:  
都倉先生

いつもお世話になっております。東雲電機の海田でございます。

このたび社内の人事異動に伴い、共同開発関連の当社窓口を変更させていただくこととなりましたので、ご連絡申し上げます。

2017年4月1日付をもちまして、知的財産部の新開 果林が本件の担当となります。新開は本年4月に当部へ着任し、以降、定例レビューの事務局および契約管理を担当しております。すでに何度かお目にかかっているかと存じます。

私自身は2017年3月末をもって知的財産部長の職を離れます。引継ぎのため、本年12月から来年3月までの間は、新開と私の両名を宛先に加えていただけますと幸いです。4月以降は新開のみで結構でございます。

事務的なお願いを一点申し添えます。当社が過去に差し上げた書面につきましては、当社側でも案件ごとに原本と社内決裁書類を保管しております。保管は案件番号を鍵として整理しておりますので、今後、当社が署名した書面の内容や範囲についてご照会をいただく場合には、対象の案件番号と書面の日付をご明示のうえ、書面にてお問い合わせいただけますようお願い申し上げます。口頭でのご照会には正確にお答えできない場合がございます。この点は後任にも申し送っております。

七年間にわたり大変お世話になりました。厚く御礼申し上げます。今後は新開ともども、変わらぬお引き立てを賜りますようお願い申し上げます。

海田 隆志
知的財産部長
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-82**  
To: 都倉 志乃 <tokura@yodogawa-ip.jp>  
From: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 海田 隆志 <t.kaida@shinonome-denki.co.jp>, Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 ドイツにおける実施許諾に対する同意について（正式回答）  
Date: February 09, 2017, 10:33  
References: Email-78  
Body:  
淀川国際特許事務所
弁理士 都倉 志乃 先生

お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部の新開でございます。本件は海田に代わり当職が担当いたします。

標記の件、1月25日開催の知的財産委員会において承認されましたので、正式にご回答申し上げます。

一、当社は、Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. が Rehberg Akustiksysteme GmbH に対し、欧州特許 EP 2 389 013 B1 のドイツ連邦共和国における部分につき非独占的実施権を許諾することに同意いたします。

二、同意の対価は三十一万ユーロとし、同意書の発効日から三十日以内に当社指定口座へお支払いいただきます。

三、条件は下記のとおりでございます。

　（１）用途は車載インフォテインメント機器に限ります。
　（２）地域はドイツ連邦共和国に限ります。
　（３）再実施許諾は認めません。
　（４）Rehberg 社の関係会社への拡張には当社の別途の書面同意を要します。
　（５）期間は実施許諾契約の有効期間に限り、最長で2022年12月31日までといたします。
　（６）本同意はドイツ部分のみを対象とし、フランス、英国、イタリア及びオランダの各部分並びに日本国、米国、韓国及び中国の各対応特許には一切及びません。

四、同意書は日本語及び英語の二か国語で作成し、齟齬がある場合は日本語を優先いたします。

同意書案を今週中にお送りいたします。3月上旬のご調印を目途にお進めいただければ幸いでございます。

新開 果林
知的財産部
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-83**  
To: Annika Vorderbrück <annika.vorderbrueck@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Jahresgebühren auf europäische Anmeldungen – elf Fälle mit falschem Zuständigkeitsvermerk seit der Migration  
Date: March 15, 2017, 08:40  
References: Email-73  
Body:  
Liebe Annika,

bei der Durchsicht der Fälligkeitsliste für das zweite Quartal sind mir elf europäische Anmeldungen aufgefallen, bei denen das Feld Renewal Responsibility seit der Umstellung vom 19. Februar 2016 auf "Provider - standing instruction" steht, obwohl die Jahresgebühren bei diesen Fällen seit jeher von München aus angewiesen werden. Es ist dieselbe Ursache, die Yolande im April letzten Jahres für die brasilianischen Fälle beschrieben hat.

Zur Erinnerung, weil es in San Mateo regelmäßig zu Missverständnissen führt. Das Europäische Patentamt erhebt Jahresgebühren bereits auf die Anmeldung, und zwar ab dem dritten Jahr, gerechnet vom Anmeldetag, Artikel 86 EPÜ. Das Deutsche Patent- und Markenamt erhebt Jahresgebühren ebenfalls sowohl auf Anmeldungen als auch auf erteilte Patente. Das ist der Unterschied zu Japan, Korea und China, wo auf anhängige Anmeldungen nichts anfällt. Die Kollegen dort halten unsere EP-Rechnungen deshalb gelegentlich für fehlerhaft.

Zwei der elf Fälle befinden sich bereits in der Nachfrist. Die Zahlung ist innerhalb von sechs Monaten nach Fälligkeit mit einem Zuschlag von fünfzig Prozent möglich, beide liegen innerhalb dieser Frist, ein Rechtsverlust ist also nicht eingetreten. Ich habe die Zahlungen zum 22. März angewiesen.

Die Liste liegt in Veritan unter der gespeicherten Suche "EP Jahresgebühren Zuständigkeit 2017". Bitte prüfen Sie die vier Fälle aus Ihrem Bestand und bestätigen Sie mir bis Freitag, welcher Vermerk zutrifft. Wir korrigieren dann gesammelt über IP Operations und nicht einzeln, sonst verlieren wir den Überblick.

Viele Grüße
Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-84**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 蓮沼 和成 <hasunuma@shinonome-kogyo.co.jp>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 横浜事業所 4階 内装工事お見積りご送付の件  
Date: March 15, 2017, 13:50  
References:  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
園田 修 様

平素より大変お世話になっております。東雲興業株式会社 建築事業部の蓮沼です。

3月8日にご案内いただきました横浜事業所4階の内装工事につきまして、お見積りを本日発送いたしました。到着は明後日の見込みです。

概要は下記のとおりです。会議室2室の増設、書庫スペースの間仕切り変更、および什器搬入に伴う床補強を含みます。工期は5月8日着工、6月16日完了、休日作業3日を含みます。金額は税別で4,180,000円、什器は別途です。

なお、施錠管理が必要な書庫につきましては、鍵の受渡しを工事完了時に総務ご担当者様へ直接行う形とさせてください。前回の川崎の案件で行き違いがございましたので、今回は書面で受領印をいただきます。

ご不明な点がございましたら私までご連絡ください。ご発注いただける場合は、4月14日までにご一報いただけますと、5月着工に間に合います。

何卒よろしくお願い申し上げます。

蓮沼 和成
建築事業部 営業課
東雲興業株式会社

**Email-85**  
To: Dr. Sigrun Waldherr <s.waldherr@rehberg-akustik.de>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>, Annika Vorderbrück <annika.vorderbrueck@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: EP 2 389 013 B1 - Zustimmung der Mitinhaberin liegt vor, Lizenzvertrag DE  
Date: April 25, 2017, 15:20  
References: Email-82  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Dr. Waldherr,

die Zustimmung der Mitinhaberin liegt vor. Shinonome Electric Co., Ltd. hat die Zustimmungserklärung am 14. März 2017 unterzeichnet; unsere Gegenzeichnung ist am 20. März erfolgt. Damit ist der Weg für den Lizenzvertrag frei.

Zum Hintergrund, den ich Ihnen im Juni geschildert hatte: EP 2 389 013 B1 steht seit der Umschreibung vom 3. Juni 2015 auf zwei Inhaber. Nach § 747 Satz 2 BGB hätten wir Ihnen ohne die Zustimmung der zweiten Inhaberin keine wirksame Lizenz am deutschen Teil erteilen können. Daher die lange Vorlaufzeit, für die ich mich noch einmal entschuldige.

Den Vertragsentwurf habe ich Ihnen heute im Datenraum freigeschaltet. Er bildet die Bedingungen der Zustimmung eins zu eins ab. Drei Punkte hebe ich hervor, weil sie nicht verhandelbar sind.

Erstens ist die Lizenz auf den deutschen Teil des Patents beschränkt. Für Frankreich, Großbritannien, Italien und die Niederlande gilt sie nicht. Wenn Sie diese Länder benötigen, sagen Sie es bitte jetzt, denn dafür ist eine gesonderte Zustimmung erforderlich, die wiederum Zeit und Geld kostet.

Zweitens ist die Lizenz auf Infotainment-Systeme für Kraftfahrzeuge beschränkt und nicht unterlizenzierbar.

Drittens erstreckt sie sich nicht auf verbundene Unternehmen der Rehberg-Gruppe. Für die Rehberg Automotive Electronics s.r.o. in Brünn bräuchten wir eine neue Zustimmung.

Für Rückfragen stehe ich gern zur Verfügung. Herr Renaudin-Whyte meldet sich getrennt zu den kommerziellen Bedingungen.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen

Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-86**  
To: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 郑慧敏 <zheng.huimin@huachen-ip.cn>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2014-0203 案 第二次审查意见通知书 新增对比文件 WO 2011/024531  
Date: May 17, 2017, 09:50  
References:  
Body:  
陆律师：

本案第二次审查意见通知书已于2017年5月11日发出，答复期限为两个月，自推定收到日起算，即2017年7月26日届满。

案件信息：申请号201480031776.2，贵司卷号MAL-2014-0203，发明名称"多通道音频的自适应下混"。

审查员在本次通知书中引入了一篇新的对比文件，即对比文件3：WO 2011/024531 A2，申请人为Halberstam Acoustics Ltd.，公开日为2011年3月3日。

关于该文献的编号，请允许我多写一句。我核对了三遍，确认是2011/024531。贵司同事在上一轮沟通中曾提到一篇编号相近的文献，我调阅后确认并非同一件，两者的申请人、公开年份和技术领域均不相同。此类编号极易混淆，建议贵司在内部传阅时一并写明申请人名称和公开日。

实质意见方面，审查员认为对比文件3的图5公开了本申请权利要求1中"按感知重要性排序的通道合并顺序"这一特征，与第一次通知书已引用的对比文件1结合，权利要求1不具备创造性。

我的分析如下。对比文件3的排序依据是各通道的短时能量，而本申请权利要求1限定的是基于掩蔽阈值的感知重要性度量。二者在数学上并不等价。对于低能量但高感知重要性的语音通道，两种排序会得出相反的结果，说明书第0061段有具体的听音测试数据支持这一点，该数据在本申请优先权日之前未曾公开。

建议在意见陈述书中引用第0061段的对比数据，并将权利要求3中"掩蔽阈值由心理声学模型导出"的特征并入权利要求1。如此修改后，我认为授权前景良好。

第二次答复期限较短，请于6月20日前给予指示。

顺颂
商祺

郑慧敏
专利代理人
北京华辰知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-87**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 富樫 志乃 <s.togashi@kasai-onkyo.co.jp>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 部品供給基本契約 別紙A（部品番号一覧）改定のお願い  
Date: May 25, 2017, 15:40  
References:  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部 園田 修 様

いつもお世話になっております。葛西音響部品株式会社 営業技術部の富樫でございます。

2014年4月1日付 部品供給基本契約の別紙A（部品番号一覧）の改定につきまして、ご相談申し上げます。

現行の別紙Aには18点の部品番号を記載しておりますが、このうち KO-2231-C（ダンパー、外径38mm）につきまして、材料メーカーの生産終了に伴い、2018年3月末をもって供給を終了いたします。後継品は KO-2233-C となります。寸法および取付ピッチは同一、損失係数は現行比で約4%改善しております。評価用サンプルは6月9日に20個お送りいたします。

つきましては、別紙Aから KO-2231-C を削除し KO-2233-C を追加する内容の覚書を締結させていただきたく存じます。単価は現行据置き、最低発注ロットは500個から300個に引き下げます。

なお、別紙Aは契約上、供給対象部品を特定する唯一の書面でございます。別紙Aに記載のない部品のご発注は個別注文書での対応となり、本契約の品質保証条項が適用されませんので、後継品の量産切替までに覚書の締結をお願いできればと存じます。

ご検討のほど、よろしくお願い申し上げます。

富樫 志乃
営業技術部 課長
葛西音響部品株式会社

**Email-88**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>, Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 - Rehberg license executed, consent fee paid, records to update  
Date: May 30, 2017, 08:50  
References: Email-85  
Body:  
Roswell, Bettina,

Closing this out. The Rehberg license for the German part of EP 2 389 013 B1 was executed on May 24. The consent fee of EUR 310,000 to Shinonome Electric was paid on May 26 against their invoice SDN-2017-0088 and has been booked to the JD-SHN-09 cost center as a one-time licensing expense, not as a royalty.

Bettina, four things for Veritan on MAL-2009-0271.

One, the EP member's Ownership Status is Joint and should say so on every national part. It is correct on the EP record itself, but the DE, FR, GB, IT and NL sub-records inherited Sole from the JP member during the migration. That is a jurisdiction-level display problem rather than an ownership dispute, but I do not want anyone reading the German sub-record and concluding we can license it alone.

Two, add the consent as a document of record with its own date, March 14, 2017, and its scope in the summary field: Germany only, automotive infotainment only, no sublicensing, no affiliates, expiring with the license agreement or on December 31, 2022, whichever is earlier.

Three, put a standing alert on the FR, GB, IT and NL parts. Any licensing task there needs a fresh consent.

Four, the JP member of this family is held on a different basis. 特許第5442913号 stands in Marsden's sole name on the strength of a 2011 confirmation that is limited to Japan by its own terms. Leave the JP record as Sole, and leave the territorial limitation visible in the Ownership Basis note, because that limitation is the whole reason the last eleven months happened.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-89**  
To: 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 移行後の年金管理区分の誤りについて（日本案件17件のご確認のお願い）  
Date: June 08, 2017, 13:05  
References:  
Body:  
都築先生

いつもお世話になっております。マースデン・ジャパンの園田でございます。

社内の知的財産管理システムを2016年2月19日に旧システムから現行システムへ移行いたしましたが、その際に自動生成された年金管理区分に誤りのある日本案件が17件見つかりましたので、ご報告申し上げます。

内訳は、本来当社が直接納付すべきところ外部年金業者の一括指示区分となっているものが11件、その逆が6件でございます。実害としては、係属中の出願2件について年金の納付指示が発出され、特許庁で受理されずに返戻された事例がございました。ご承知のとおり、日本では係属中の出願に年金は発生いたしません。第1年から第3年分は特許査定の謄本送達の日から30日以内に一括して納付し、第4年分以降を毎年前納する扱いでございます。

原因は、業者側のマスタが欧州仕様、すなわち出願段階から年金が発生する前提のまま設定されており、移行時にその前提が日本案件にも一律に適用されたことにございます。欧州特許庁は出願に対しても第3年から年金を課しますので、業者としては誤りという認識がなかったようでございます。

つきましては、貴所にご担当いただいている9件につきまして、貴所の期限管理台帳と当社システムの表示に相違がないか、ご確認いただけますでしょうか。相違がある場合は貴所の記録を正といたします。7月14日までにご回答賜れますと幸いでございます。

お手数をおかけいたしますが、何卒よろしくお願い申し上げます。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-90**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - Shinonome consent for Pentavia, signed  
Date: September 21, 2017, 10:16  
References: Email-69  
Body:  
Tomás,

Shinonome Electric's consent for the Pentavia Semiconductor license under MAL-2011-0455 was signed on September 18 and is in the docket mailbox. Shinkai-san handled it; Kaida-san left the department in March and she is now the contact for everything on JD-SHN-09.

Timeline for the file: the request went out through Yodogawa on August 7, their IP committee took it on September 6, signature September 18. Six weeks door to door, which is the fastest we have had on this family.

Scope is the usual shape. Pentavia only, the JP, US, EP, KR and CN members, encoder silicon sold to consumer AV manufacturers, term coextensive with the license agreement, no sublicensing beyond the licensed silicon in the hands of Pentavia's customers, no affiliates without a fresh consent. Royalty split fifty-fifty per Article 8, paid semi-annually.

One drafting point worth keeping. They asked us to add a sentence recording that the consent is given under Article 73(3) of the Patent Act and does not transfer or dilute their share. We agreed, and I would put the same sentence in every future consent under this family. It costs nothing and it removes the only argument anyone could ever make that a course of consents amounted to an assignment of their half.

Second request under this family, second consent. It is working the way joint ownership is supposed to work when both sides are organized about it.

Priyasha Nandakumar
IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-91**  
To: Almut Pfannenstiel <pfannenstiel@pfannenstiel-ip.de>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0932 (Münchener Serie) – Jahresgebühren 2018 und eine Lizenzanfrage  
Date: October 18, 2017, 09:35  
References: Email-46  
Body:  
Sehr geehrte Frau Dr. Pfannenstiel,

zwei Punkte zu MAL-2010-0932, dem Patent zur Lautsprecher-Auslenkungsbegrenzung.

Erstens die Jahresgebühren. Nach der Erteilung im Januar 2014 haben wir in DE, FR, GB und IT validiert. Der Vertrieb teilt mit, dass in Italien seit drei Jahren kein Umsatz mehr mit den betreffenden Verstärkermodulen erzielt wird und auch keiner mehr erwartet wird. Ich schlage vor, die italienische Jahresgebühr 2018 nicht mehr zu zahlen und das Schutzrecht dort verfallen zu lassen. In DE, FR und GB zahlen wir weiter. Bitte bestätigen Sie mir die jeweiligen Fälligkeitstermine. Für Deutschland habe ich den 30. September notiert und möchte das gegengeprüft haben, bevor ich es in unser System eintrage.

Zweitens eine Lizenzanfrage. Die Rotgerber Verstärkertechnik GmbH aus Bielefeld hat über ihren Vertreter angefragt, ob wir eine nicht ausschließliche Lizenz am deutschen Teil erteilen würden. Herr Renaudin-Whyte führt das Gespräch von San Mateo aus und ist deshalb in Kopie.

Für die Akte halte ich fest, was ich Herrn Renaudin-Whyte bereits mündlich gesagt habe. Dieses Patent gehört Marsden allein. Alleiniger Erfinder ist Herr Ahlborn-Vesely, damals hier in München angestellt, die Erfindung ist innerhalb seines Aufgabenbereichs entstanden und unbeschränkt in Anspruch genommen worden, es gibt kein Gemeinschaftsprojekt, keinen Mitanmelder und keinen Mitinhaber. Wir können also ohne Zustimmung Dritter lizenzieren, und zwar weltweit.

Ich sage das ausdrücklich und schriftlich, weil bei uns im Haus derzeit mehrere Vorgänge laufen, bei denen die Antwort auf dieselbe Frage sehr viel länger ausfällt, und weil sich das Aktenzeichen dieses Vorgangs von zwei anderen Vorgängen des Hauses nur durch die Reihenfolge zweier Ziffern unterscheidet. Wer diese Zeilen später einmal zitiert, möge bitte die Serienangabe mitzitieren.

Mit freundlichen Grüßen

Beatrix Hollerith
Europäische Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-92**  
To: Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Marguerite Ashland-Poe <marguerite.ashland-poe@coastalpiedmont.edu>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: CPU-2017-041 - ownership under Article 7 and Annex A Task 2  
Date: November 09, 2017, 12:25  
References: Email-33  
Body:  
Dear Kwabena,

Our invention committee reviewed disclosure CPU-2017-041, "Sparse sampling of room impulse responses using a rotating microphone boom," on November 2 and asked me to open the ownership question with you before we spend money on a filing.

The University's position is that the invention was made in performance of Task 2 of the Annex A research plan under the sponsored research agreement. Task 2 is the room impulse response database collection, the boom was built with agreement funds, and the two contributors on our side are the principal investigator and a graduate student. On that footing Article 7.1 applies and the invention is the sole property of the University, with Marsden holding a first option to an exclusive license exercisable within six months of formal disclosure. Treat this email as starting the six months on the date of formal disclosure, which will be November 20.

I should tell you that the principal investigator does not entirely agree with his own committee. He says one of your acousticians proposed the rotating boom over dinner in April and that without that conversation there is no invention. If that is right, Article 7.2 applies instead, the invention is jointly owned, and both parties may practice without accounting — a very different commercial picture for you and a worse one for us.

I would rather resolve inventorship than argue ownership. Can your acoustician give us a written account of the April conversation, and can we meet the week of December 4? The University will not file until this is settled.

Sincerely,
Marguerite Ashland-Poe
Director, Office of Technology Licensing
Coastal Piedmont University

**Email-93**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: EP 2 545 719 maintained as granted - Kesselring opposition rejected February 7  
Date: February 08, 2018, 13:20  
References: Email-72  
Body:  
All,

The Opposition Division announced its decision at the end of yesterday's oral proceedings in Munich. The opposition is rejected and EP 2 545 719 B1 is maintained as granted. No amendment, no auxiliary request needed, and we were out of the building by four.

This is the beamforming patent, docket MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series. It is not the only matter in the house carrying those four digits, so please quote the year and the series if you forward this.

How it went, briefly.

The sufficiency attack was dropped by the opponent in writing in November, after we pointed at paragraphs [0038] to [0044] and offered to run the worked example in front of the Division.

The Ilmenau thesis was the case. We obtained the full copy from the university library in June 2016 and it does disclose a coherence-weighted array, which was uncomfortable reading. What saved us was the accession record. The library stamped the thesis as catalogued on 14 November 2011, twenty months after our priority date and eight months after our international filing date. Kesselring argued it had been on the department's internal server since 2007. The Division held the evidence of public availability before the priority date insufficient and disregarded the document. Almut Pfannenstiel called that in her very first note on the opposition, in March 2016, which I would like on the record.

The alleged public prior use at Frankfurt in 2009 was supported by a declaration from Kesselring's own head of development and nothing else. No brochure, no photograph, no invoice, no witness who does not draw a salary from the opponent. The Division did not think much of it.

Inventive step over what remained was argued for about ninety minutes and the problem-solution approach came out our way.

Two months to appeal. Almut expects one. I do not. Either way the patent stands and is enforceable in the meantime.

Beatrix Hollerith
European Patent Attorney
Marsden Europe GmbH, Munich

**Email-94**  
To: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: JD-CHW-12 - Annex A bounds title on this program too - confirming before I write it into the file  
Date: March 14, 2018, 07:40  
References: Email-74, Email-25  
Body:  
Jianing,

I am working through the joint development agreements as part of taking over the program portfolio, and I want your view on the Chenwei one before I write my conclusion into the program file, because once it is in there people will rely on it.

The architecture across this family of agreements is the same everywhere I have looked. Annex A defines a field. Inventions inside the field are jointly owned. Inventions outside the field are the sole property of the party whose people made them. The Annex A determination is therefore the whole ballgame and we document it per disclosure.

I have gone through the Chenwei matters on that basis and I cannot see anything that makes this program different. Annex A on JD-CHW-12 is six work packages and every one of them is transport layer or silicon. Eleanor applied the same reading in January 2013 when she decided we would file MAL-2012-0688 alone, and your own close-out letter to Tan of June 2016 applies it as well. That is three of us and five years of practice pointing the same way, with no objection from the counterparty in all that time.

I should be straight about the limits of what I have done. I have read the agreement abstract in Veritan and the Annex A list. I have not pulled the executed agreement and read Article 5 word for word, and I am not going to spend a day doing that to confirm a reading three people have already acted on. If either of you thinks the ownership clause on this program is drafted against something other than Annex A, say so this week, because I am about to write "Annex A determination governs title on JD-CHW-12" into the program file and it will sit there until somebody has a reason to move it.

The one gap I do want to close is that we have never asked Chenwei to agree a boundary in writing on any individual disclosure. That is cheap to fix at the next annual review and I will put it on the agenda.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-95**  
To: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
From: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 迁移遗留问题：12件中国案件的辖区分组设置错误  
Date: May 16, 2018, 11:40  
References:  
Body:  
傅老师，您好。

有一件我方系统的问题需要向您说明，并请您在今后的期限管理上作相应处理。

我司于2016年2月19日更换了知识产权管理系统。迁移过程中，"辖区分组"字段由旧系统的地区代码自动推导生成。推导规则把中国归入了"亚太（不含日本）"分组，而该分组的答复期限默认按两个月计算。中国的实际规则是：第一次审查意见通知书的答复期限为四个月，其后各次为两个月，均自通知书发文日起加十五日推定收到。

受影响的中国案件共12件。后果有两类。一是三件案件的内部提醒早于实际期限约两个月，导致我方多次催促贵所，给您添了麻烦，在此致歉。二是有一件在我方系统中显示为"已逾期"，而实际上尚在期限内，我方内部一度按丧失权利处理，所幸未向贵所发出放弃指示。

另有一点。国家知识产权局的英文名称今年由 SIPO 变更为 CNIPA，我方与外部数据源的著录项目同步接口按英文机构名匹配，自5月起同步失败，中国案件的法律状态自5月8日起未更新。IT 部门预计6月中旬修复。

在上述两项修复完成之前，恳请您在答复期限一事上以贵所的台帐为准，不要以我方系统的提醒为准。若我方发出的提醒与您的记录不符，请直接以您的记录为准并告知我，不必等待我方确认。

顺祝商祺。

陆嘉宁
知识产权顾问
马斯登（上海）技术有限公司

**Email-96**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Shinonome audit - notice served, field work week of August 13  
Date: June 21, 2018, 14:45  
References: Email-63  
Body:  
Roswell,

Audit notice went out this morning under Section 9, sixty days' notice as required. Field work is the week of August 13 in Kumamoto, two auditors from Halvorsen Kestenbaum, scope is calendar years 2016 and 2017.

What triggered it. Their reported volume for 2017 fell 11% while their own investor materials show the relevant segment up 6%, and the SDX-5C line has been reported at a flat 400,000 units for six consecutive quarters, which no real product does.

Scope points I want you aware of before you hear them from their side. We are entitled to unit counts by product designation, the mapping from internal die codes to product designations, and the shipment records that support the mapping. We are not entitled to customer names and I have said so up front, because that objection has stalled two of our audits before. The auditors will work from redacted records with customer identifiers removed.

If the finding exceeds three percent under-reporting, amendment No. 2 gives us the audit cost as well as the shortfall with interest at prime plus two.

One request. Please do not let the business schedule the 2019 renewal conversation until the audit report is final. Last time we negotiated a rate while an audit was open and gave away most of the finding without noticing.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-97**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 特許第5617042号 第5年分特許料の納付期限について（MAL-2011-0288）  
Date: September 19, 2018, 10:55  
References:  
Body:  
園田部長

いつもお世話になっております。淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人の皆川でございます。

標記の件、本年12月に第5年分の納付期限が到来いたしますので、三か月前のご案内として申し上げます。

特許番号：特許第5617042号
発明の名称：低遅延音声符号化におけるフレーム分割制御
貴社整理番号：MAL-2011-0288
設定登録日：2014年12月11日
請求項数：14
特許権者：マースデン・オーディオ・ラボラトリーズ社（単独）

第1年から第3年までの特許料は2014年12月4日に一括納付済みでございます。第4年分は昨年12月6日に納付済みでございますので、次は第5年分となり、納付期限は2018年12月11日でございます。

金額は、年額10,300円に請求項1項あたり800円を加算し、請求項14項でございますので合計21,500円となります。追納期間は6か月ございますが割増特許料が発生いたしますので、従前どおり期限内の納付を前提にご準備をお願いいたします。

一点、確認をお願いしたい事項がございます。貴社の整理番号につきまして、当所の記録では本件は MAL-2011-0288 でございます。しかしながら本年6月にご送付いただいた年金予定表では、特許番号のみが記載され、整理番号の欄が空欄となっておりました。日本の特許番号は桁の並びが似通ったものが多く、当所でも過去に別件の包袋を誤って請求しかけたことがございます。恐れ入りますが、今後は特許番号と整理番号を必ず併記していただけますと助かります。

ご指示をお待ちしております。

皆川 悠子
弁理士
淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人

**Email-98**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
Subject: IPR2018-01462 - petition filed against US 8,391,527 B2  
Date: September 20, 2018, 14:15  
References:  
Body:  
Roswell, Kwabena,

Calderon Acoustics LLC filed an inter partes review petition against US 8,391,527 B2 on September 18. We were served the same day. The patent is MAL-2008-0244, the low-latency crossfade family, and it is in the standard bundle at an allocated 1.8 percent of 2017 bundle revenue.

Scope. The petition challenges claims 1, 4 through 9, and 14. Two grounds, both under 35 U.S.C. 103. Ground 1 is Weatherby in view of Ostrowski. Ground 2 is Weatherby in view of Ostrowski and further in view of a 2006 AES convention paper. Claim 14 is the one that matters commercially; it is the claim we read on their product.

Timing. We should get the notice of filing date accorded within a week. The patent owner preliminary response is due three months from that date, so mid-to-late December, and the institution decision three months after that, so March.

Recommendation. File a preliminary response and keep it narrow. Weatherby was cited in an information disclosure statement during prosecution and the examiner initialed it and discussed it in the first office action. That puts us squarely in 35 U.S.C. 325(d) territory and the Becton Dickinson factors are as favorable as they get: same art, same argument, and the examiner already resolved it. I would lead with that and not spend the page limit on claim construction, which we will lose at this stage in any event because the standard is broadest reasonable interpretation.

I do not recommend a statutory disclaimer of the dependent claims. Adrienne, can you get us a fee estimate through institution by October 5.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-99**  
To: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Annika Vorderbrück <annika.vorderbrueck@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Verrocchio-NDA - Annex A muss vor dem gemeinsamen Einspruch erweitert werden - Frist 14. November  
Date: September 26, 2018, 16:10  
References: Email-5  
Body:  
Liebe Beatrix,

wir haben ein Formproblem, das uns die Frist kosten kann, wenn wir es nicht diese Woche lösen.

Der gemeinsam mit Verrocchio Semiconductors vorbereitete Einspruch gegen die EP 2 371 884 B1 muss bis zum 14. November eingelegt werden. Die Einspruchsfrist beträgt neun Monate ab dem Hinweis auf die Erteilung im Patentblatt, hier der 14. Februar 2018, und sie ist nicht verlängerbar.

Der Entwurf des Einspruchsschriftsatzes stützt sich auf Messdaten, die Verrocchio uns unter der wechselseitigen Geheimhaltungsvereinbarung übergeben hat. Annex A dieser Vereinbarung nennt elf Personen namentlich als zugelassene Empfänger; eine Weitergabe an nicht genannte Personen ist nach Ziffer 4.2 auch dann ein Verstoß, wenn es sich um eigene Mitarbeiter handelt. Unsere Münchner Vertreterin Frau Dr. Lauterbach-Sieveking und die beiden Sachbearbeiter der Kanzlei stehen nicht in Annex A. Der technische Experte, den wir für die Versuchsauswertung brauchen, ebenfalls nicht.

Erforderlich ist ein von beiden Seiten unterzeichneter Nachtrag zu Annex A. Verrocchios Justiziariat nennt zehn Arbeitstage, das führt uns bis Mitte Oktober, was reicht, aber nur ohne weitere Schleife.

Frau Drozdov, bitte prüfen Sie, ob die vier Namen aus Sicht der Informationssteuerung freigegeben werden können. Ohne Nachtrag müssten wir den Schriftsatz ohne die Messdaten einreichen, und dann trägt er nur noch zwei der vier Angriffe.

Viele Grüße
Annika Vorderbrück
Patentanwältin
Marsden Europe GmbH, München

**Email-100**  
To: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
Cc: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2012-0688 家族授权情况汇总，并请核对权属状态  
Date: November 07, 2018, 09:20  
References: Email-68  
Body:  
陆女士，您好。

应贵司要求，现将 MAL-2012-0688 家族各成员的授权情况汇总如下，供年度盘点使用。

中国：ZL 201480004871.2，2018年5月15日授权公告，权利要求20项全部维持。第五年年费已于办理授权登记手续时一并缴纳。

美国：US 9,437,208 B2，2016年9月6日颁证，申请号 14/760,832。

欧洲：EP 2 945 158 B1，授权公告日2018年8月22日，异议期至2019年5月22日届满。已在德国、法国、英国、荷兰四国生效。

日本：特許第6273418号，2018年1月26日设定登录，第1至第3年年费已一并缴纳。

以上四件的申请人／专利权人均登记为 Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc. 一方，与2015年进入国家阶段时贵司的指示一致。

有一处请贵司核对。贵司11月2日发来的案件清单中，本案的"权属状态"一栏为"共有（辰威微电子有限公司，50%）"，与四国登记簿的记载不一致。我所在中国的著录项目登记以国家知识产权局的记载为准，不受贵司内部系统影响，但若贵司确有共有的意思，则应当尽快办理著录项目变更，并注意共有权利的行使规则与单独所有不同。若系贵司系统的记载有误，也请一并更正，以免今后在许可或者转让时产生障碍。

请予确认为盼。

傅锦程
专利代理师
恒宇知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-101**  
To: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2013-0771 特願2013-176482 分割出願提出および設定登録のご報告  
Date: March 01, 2019, 09:20  
References: Email-39  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
黒岩 千尋 様

淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人の皆川でございます。標記案件につきましてご報告申し上げます。

一、本願（特願2013-176482「小型ボイスコイル型振動子の防振支持構造」）は、1月22日付で特許査定の謄本が送達されました。

二、特許査定の謄本送達日から30日以内、かつ設定登録前という要件を満たすため、2月8日に分割出願を提出いたしました。出願番号は特願2019-020318です。同日付で出願審査請求も行っております。設定登録がなされますと原出願は特許庁に係属しなくなり分割ができなくなりますので、分割を先に済ませ、特許料の納付を後回しにする段取りといたしました。

三、分割出願の提出を確認のうえ、2月19日に第1年から第3年分の特許料を納付し、2月26日付で設定登録がなされ、特許第6473296号となりました。登録証は追ってお届けいたします。

分割出願の内容は、明細書の第二実施形態、すなわち板ばねと防振ゴムを併用する二重支持構造に係る部分を独立請求項に格上げしたものです。親出願の審査では第一実施形態の一体成形構造のみが権利化されており、二重支持構造は明細書に記載があるにとどまっておりました。量産機の設計が二重支持構造に寄っているとの黒岩様のご指摘を踏まえ、この形で分割した次第です。

四、新規事項の追加とならないよう、分割出願の請求項の文言はすべて親出願の明細書段落0038から0051の記載に対応させております。対応表を別途お送りいたします。

以上、ご確認のほどよろしくお願い申し上げます。

皆川 悠子
弁理士
淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人

**Email-102**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - Norlander consent granted, and a note on how we ask  
Date: March 12, 2019, 09:05  
References: Email-90  
Body:  
Tomás, Roswell,

Shinonome consented to the Norlander Ljud AB license on March 8. Third consent under MAL-2011-0455, third yes. Scope: Norlander only, the EP and JP members, professional broadcast loudness meters, term of the license agreement, no sublicensing, no affiliates. The Article 73(3) recital Priyasha added in 2017 is in this one too.

Since I am new to this family I will record how the request is actually made, because it is not obvious from the docket.

The request goes through Yodo-Naniwa, not direct. Minagawa-sensei is co-counsel for both proprietors on this family and Shinonome will not act on a request that comes straight from us. The request has to identify the proposed licensee by legal name, the members of the family, the field of use, the term, and whether affiliates or sublicensing are sought. If any of those are left open, they come back for them and you lose three weeks.

Article 7 of the JD-SHN-09 agreement gives them fifteen business days to respond, but in practice the answer waits for their IP committee, which sits monthly. Plan on six to ten weeks.

And they are entitled to say no. Article 73(3) is a veto, not a consultation right, and Article 7 does not oblige them to be reasonable about it.
