We have three yeses on this family, and that is a fact about the relationship rather than a rule of law. Do not promise a licensee a signature date that assumes the fourth.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-103**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
From: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2009-0271 フランス及びイタリアへの同意範囲拡張について  
Date: March 19, 2019, 14:28  
References: Email-88  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部長 園田 修 様
淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人 弁理士 皆川 悠子 先生

お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部の新開でございます。

昨年12月にご照会いただきました、Rehberg 社に対する実施許諾のフランス及びイタリアへの拡張につき、社内手続が終わりましたのでご回答申し上げます。

一、当社は、EP 2 389 013 B1 のフランス共和国及びイタリア共和国における各部分につき、2017年3月14日付同意書と同一の用途・条件で実施許諾を行うことに同意いたします。

二、対価は八万五千ユーロといたします。ドイツ分に比して低額としておりますのは、両国における Rehberg 社の販売規模がドイツの三割程度と伺っているためでございます。

三、フランスにつきましては、貴社ご指摘のとおり、フランス知的財産法典 L.613-29条 c) により、共有者の一方は他方の同意なくして非独占的実施権を許諾し得るものとされております。ただし他の共有者に対する衡平な補償及び先買権の手続を要します。当社といたしましては、この法定の手続をとるよりも、本同意書によって条件を明確にしておくほうが双方にとって簡明であると考えます。したがいまして本同意は、当社が同条に基づく補償請求権及び先買権を行使しないことの確認を兼ねるものといたします。

四、イタリアにつきましては共有者全員の同意が必要でございますので、本同意が要件そのものでございます。

五、英国及びオランダの各部分は、引き続き本同意の対象外でございます。

同意書案は今月中にお送りいたします。

新開 果林
知的財産部
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-104**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>  
Subject: 移行時に生成された代理人区分の誤りと出願審査請求期限の取り違えについて（7件）  
Date: April 17, 2019, 10:55  
References: Email-89  
Body:  
園田部長

黒岩でございます。標記の件、点検が終わりましたのでご報告いたします。

第一に、代理人区分でございます。2016年2月の移行時に、旧システムの「請求書送付先」コードから代理人区分が自動生成されておりましたが、7件について現在の代理人と一致しておりません。うち4件は2014年に代理人を変更した案件で、変更前の事務所名がそのまま残っております。実務上の影響は、庁からの発送書類の到達確認が二重管理になっていた点のみで、期限の徒過はございません。

第二に、こちらが重要でございます。1件、出願審査請求の期限が誤って計算されておりました。当該案件は国際出願を経て日本国内へ移行したものでございますが、システムは国内書面提出日を起算日として3年を計算しておりました。正しくは国際出願日から3年でございますので、システム上の表示は真の期限より約18か月遅い日付を示していたことになります。

幸い、都築先生の期限管理台帳との突合により、真の期限の4か月前に発見できました。出願審査請求は本日付で提出済みでございます。仮に見落としていれば取下げ擬制となり、回復の余地はございませんでした。

同種の起算誤りがないか、国際出願経由の日本案件34件すべてについて都築先生の台帳と突合いたします。7月末までに結果をご報告いたします。あわせて、拒絶理由通知書の応答期限（在外者は3か月、3か月の延長可）の設定についても、同じ経路で誤りが生じていないか確認いたします。

黒岩 千尋
特許エンジニア
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-105**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 문하경 <hk.moon@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, 오세라 <sr.oh@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 / KR 10-1602934 관련 공동 소유 확인 요청의 건  
Date: May 21, 2019, 10:40  
References:  
Body:  
마스든 코리아 주식회사
지식재산 매니저 박세훈 님

한영정밀 주식회사 지식재산실장 문하경입니다. 처음 연락드립니다.

당사는 2018년부터 보유 특허 및 공동개발 이력 전반에 대한 재검토를 진행하고 있습니다. 그 과정에서 2010년 1월 14일자 공동개발계약(JD-HYP-10)과 관련하여 확인이 필요한 사안이 발견되어 서면으로 말씀드립니다.

대상은 귀사 명의의 등록특허 KR 10-1602934(발명의 명칭 "열 모델링을 이용한 스피커 보호", 출원번호 10-2013-7033107, 등록일 2016년 3월 4일) 및 그 대응 외국특허입니다. 귀사 정리번호는 MAL-2011-0304으로 알고 있습니다.

당사의 검토 결과는 다음과 같습니다.

첫째, 본건 발명은 공동개발계약의 유효기간 중에, 당사 안산사업장에서 당사 시료 및 측정 설비를 사용하여 완성되었습니다. 2010년 6월 24일 및 같은 해 9월 16일 검토회 기록에 당사 인력의 기술적 관여가 남아 있습니다.

둘째, 당사 개발본부 김성환 수석이 제공한 보이스코일 열저항 실측 데이터가 등록특허 명세서 표 2 및 도 5에 그대로 반영되어 있습니다.

셋째, 그럼에도 본건은 귀사 단독 명의로 출원, 등록되었습니다.

당사는 본건에 대하여 공동 소유임을 확인하여 주시기를 요청합니다. 특허법 제44조 위반은 같은 법 제133조 제1항 제2호의 무효사유이며, 무효심판 청구에는 기간의 제한이 없다는 점을 아울러 말씀드립니다.

6월 21일까지 회신하여 주시기 바랍니다.

문하경
지식재산실장
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-106**  
To: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 - Hanyoung Precision assertion, and why I think we are fine  
Date: June 05, 2019, 08:55  
References: Email-105  
Body:  
Trevor, Eleanor,

Hanyoung Precision wrote to me on May 21 asserting co-ownership of MAL-2011-0304, our Korean registration KR 10-1602934 and its foreign counterparts. Their deadline for a reply is June 21. I have asked Hanul to acknowledge and take an extension to July 12.

My initial assessment is that we are in a strong position, and the reason is a document rather than an argument.

On May 18, 2011, before we filed anything, Hanyoung gave us a signed and sealed confirmation letter. Kim Do-yun, then General Manager of their IP Department, signed it under the company seal after their IP committee took the matter on March 24, 2011. It says the invention is not within the scope of Annex A of the January 14, 2010 agreement, that it is Marsden's sole property, that Hanyoung asserts no share in it or in the right to obtain patents on it in Korea or elsewhere, and that Hanyoung will not license it to third parties. It names our two inventors and records that no Hanyoung employee is an inventor. It records that the June 24, 2010 demonstration was background technology under Article 9 and not a program deliverable.

That letter answers every point in Ms. Moon's letter, and it answers them in Hanyoung's own words, in writing, before the dispute existed. Their new position contradicts a document their own IP committee approved.

Bettina, please pull the correspondence file. I want the Korean original, the scan, and Mr. Bang's May 24, 2011 covering letter, which is the English account of it.

I will have Hanul read the original against Ms. Moon's letter line by line before we reply. I do not expect surprises.

Se-hoon Park
IP Manager
Marsden Korea

**Email-107**  
To: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
Cc: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 特願2017-108442（MAL-2016-0455）拒絶理由通知書の件 引用文献1は特許第5167402号  
Date: June 12, 2019, 14:15  
References:  
Body:  
黒岩様

お世話になっております。皆川でございます。

標記出願につきまして、2019年6月4日付で拒絶理由通知書が発送され、当所には6月7日に到達いたしました。応答期限は2019年8月5日でございます。指定期間は発送日から60日でございまして、満了日にあたる8月3日が土曜日のため翌週月曜が期限となります。本件は国内出願でございますので、外国出願人の場合の三か月、更に三か月の延長という扱いは適用されません。この点、社内でご共有の際にご注意いただければと存じます。

拒絶理由は特許法第29条第2項のみでございます。

引用文献1：特許第5167402号（特許権者：協和音響機器株式会社、登録日2013年1月18日）
引用文献2：特開2011-227502号

引用文献1の番号につきまして、念のため申し添えます。桁の並びが酷似した別番号の特許が複数存在いたします。社内でご共有される際には、番号のみではなく特許権者名と登録日を併記していただけますようお願い申し上げます。当所でも今回、包袋を取り寄せる際に一度誤った番号で請求してしまい、二日を無駄にいたしました。

内容についてでございます。引用文献1の請求項1は、帯域分割後のゲイン平滑化を時定数二段切替で行う構成を開示しております。本願請求項1との相違は、本願が平滑化の時定数を入力信号の過渡性指標に応じて連続的に変化させる点にございます。審査官はこれを設計事項と認定しておりますが、引用文献1の明細書段落0033には、二段切替を採用した理由として演算量の削減が明記されております。連続変化はこの目的に真っ向から反しますので、阻害要因の主張が十分に可能であると考えます。

意見書のみで応答し、補正は行わない方針をご提案申し上げます。ご検討のうえ、7月中旬までにご指示をいただけますでしょうか。補正を行う場合の案も併せてご用意いたします。

皆川 悠子
弁理士
淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人

**Email-108**  
To: 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2014-0233 の発明者認定に関する申立てへの対応方針について  
Date: June 12, 2019, 15:35  
References:  
Body:  
都築先生

園田でございます。標記の件でご相談がございます。

2018年に退職いたしました元技術者より、当社の米国継続出願 15/482,617（社内整理番号 MAL-2014-0233、位相整合再構成を伴うクロスオーバーフィルタバンク）につきまして、自身が発明者として記載されるべきであるとの申立てを、6月3日付の書面で受領いたしました。代理人を通じたものではなく、本人からの書面でございます。

申立ての根拠は、2013年後半に当該方式の初期検討に関与したというものでございます。当社の認識では、着想は2013年11月に横浜の発明者二名によってなされており、申立人の関与は評価用テストベンチの実装に限られると理解しております。ただし、当時の設計ノートは2016年9月の事業所集約時に処分されており、現行システムには2014年1月1日以降の記録しか移行されておりません。社内に一次資料がないというのが実情でございます。

そこで、本社の情報ガバナンス部門に対し、メールジャーナルの検索を依頼することといたしました。同部門は2008年以降の全社メールを保管しており、当時の横浜のやりとりも残存している見込みでございます。検索対象期間は2013年1月1日から2014年6月30日まで、対象者は発明者二名及び申立人本人の三名といたします。費用は発生いたしません。

結果が出次第、先生に共有いたします。米国側の対応、すなわち 37 CFR 1.48 による訂正の要否につきましては、結果を踏まえてご相談させていただきたく存じます。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-109**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Journal archive results - MAL-2014-0233 - the conception record exists and it is unambiguous  
Date: July 02, 2019, 09:45  
References: Email-108  
Body:  
Osamu, Kwabena,

Search complete. Scope was three custodians, January 1, 2013 to June 30, 2014, being 3,400 journaled messages. Elapsed search time nine minutes; review of the 61 responsive documents took me most of a morning.

What is there.

A design note dated November 6, 2013, circulated internally as a message body rather than an attachment, setting out the phase-alignment derivation in full including the two-band worked example. It is authored jointly by the two named inventors and the reasoning is complete — there is nothing in the issued claims that is not in that note.

The complainant appears on the distribution list of that note as a recipient. His reply, November 8, asks what sample rate the evaluation bench should run at. There is a further message from him dated February 19, 2014 offering to build the bench and a thread in March about instrumenting it. There is nothing anywhere in the set in which he proposes the reconstruction step, and nothing in which either named inventor attributes it to him.

My conclusion, offered as a fact-finder and not as a lawyer: his documented contribution is to reduction to practice, not to conception. Kwabena will know whether that ends it, but my understanding is that under US law it does, and that no correction under 37 CFR 1.48 is called for.

One limit I want on the record, because people are starting to treat this archive as an oracle. It establishes what was written. It does not establish what was said in a room in Yokohama in October 2013, and if the complainant's case is that he said it out loud, this search does not touch that.

Fenella Drozdov
Director, Information Governance
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-110**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 문하경 <hk.moon@hanyoung-p.co.kr>  
Cc: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, 정한슬 <hs.jeong@serim-law.kr>  
Subject: RE: MAL-2011-0304 / KR 10-1602934 관련 공동 소유 확인 요청의 건  
Date: July 09, 2019, 15:18  
References: Email-105  
Body:  
마스든 코리아 주식회사
지식재산 매니저 박세훈 님

한영정밀 문하경입니다. 6월 27일자 회신 및 방지훈 변리사님을 통한 자료 송부 잘 받았습니다.

귀사께서 원용하시는 2011년 5월 18일자 확인서는 당사가 발행한 것이 맞습니다. 당사도 원본 사본을 보관하고 있으며, 그 성립과 내용에 대하여 다투지 아니합니다.

다만 귀사 회신에는 확인서 제1항의 마지막 문장이 인용되어 있지 아니합니다. 확인서 제1항 전문은 아래와 같습니다.

「본건 발명은 2010년 1월 14일자 공동개발계약 별지 A에 정하는 대상 범위에 포함되지 아니하며, 귀사의 단독 소유임을 확인합니다. 당사는 본건 발명 및 이에 기초한 국내외 특허를 받을 권리에 대하여 어떠한 지분도 주장하지 아니하며, 제3자에 대한 실시허락도 행하지 아니합니다. 본 확인은 공동개발계약의 유효기간 동안에 한한다.」

마지막 문장은 당시 당사 법무팀의 요청으로 삽입된 것입니다. 2011년 2월 17일자로 당사 오세라가 방지훈 변리사님께 보낸 메일에서도 "당사는 계약 기간 중 발생하는 사안에 대하여만 사전 확인을 부여하는 것을 원칙으로 한다"는 취지를 미리 말씀드린 바 있습니다.

해당 공동개발계약은 2014년 3월 31일자로 유효기간이 만료되었고 연장되지 아니하였습니다. 종료 사무는 2014년 3월 28일자 당사 통지로 정리되었습니다. 따라서 확인서의 효력은 2014년 4월 1일 이후에는 미치지 아니합니다.

당사는 확인서의 효력이 있었던 기간 중의 귀사 행위를 문제 삼지 아니합니다. 당사가 요청드리는 것은 현재의 권리 귀속입니다.

7월 31일까지 재회신하여 주시기 바랍니다.

문하경
지식재산실장
한영정밀 주식회사

**Email-111**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 정한슬 <hs.jeong@serim-law.kr>  
Cc: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, 서미래 <mrseo@hanul-ip.co.kr>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 건 법률의견 (2011년 확인서의 효력 및 대응 방향)  
Date: August 14, 2019, 11:47  
References: Email-110  
Body:  
마스든 코리아 주식회사
지식재산 매니저 박세훈 님

법무법인 세림 변호사 정한슬입니다. 7월 22일 자문 의뢰하신 건에 대한 의견을 아래와 같이 드립니다.

일. 확인서의 효력 기간

2011년 5월 18일자 확인서 제1항 말미의 「본 확인은 공동개발계약의 유효기간 동안에 한한다」는 문언은 명확하며 해석의 여지가 크지 아니합니다. 공동개발계약이 2014년 3월 31일자로 만료된 이상, 확인서는 2014년 4월 1일부터 그 효력을 상실하였다고 보아야 합니다. 유효기간 중에 발생한 법률효과가 소급하여 소멸하는 것은 아니나, 본건에서 다투어지는 것은 현재의 권리 귀속이므로 실익이 없습니다.

이. 금반언 및 신의칙 주장의 가능성

귀사가 확인서를 신뢰하여 단독 출원 및 등록 비용을 지출하였다는 점에서 신의성실의 원칙 위반을 주장할 여지는 있습니다. 다만 확인서 자체에 기간 제한이 명시되어 있었고, 2011년 2월 17일자 상대방 메일에도 그 방침이 예고되어 있었던 이상, 귀사의 신뢰가 보호가치 있는 것이었다고 인정받기는 어렵다고 봅니다.

삼. 무효심판 위험

상대방의 주장이 사실이라면 특허법 제44조 위반에 해당하고, 이는 같은 법 제133조 제1항 제2호의 무효사유입니다. 무효심판 청구에는 제척기간이 없으며 등록 후 언제라도 청구가 가능합니다. 특허가 무효로 되면 귀사는 단독 소유는 물론 어떠한 권리도 보유하지 못하게 됩니다.

사. 실질적 기여 여부

김성환 수석 제공 데이터가 명세서 표 2 및 도 5에 반영되어 있다는 상대방 주장은 사실관계상 다투기 어려워 보입니다. 다만 데이터 제공만으로 공동발명자성이 곧바로 인정되는 것은 아니므로, 이 점은 협상에서 감액 요소로 활용할 수 있습니다.

오. 결론

무효심판으로 전부를 잃는 위험을 감수하기보다, 공동 소유를 전제로 한 별도 합의로 정리하실 것을 권고드립니다. 실시료 배분과 상호 실시 조건을 함께 정하시는 것이 바람직합니다.

정한슬
변호사
법무법인 세림

**Email-112**  
To: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
From: Dermot Achinstein <dermot.achinstein@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0329 - letting the last members go  
Date: September 11, 2019, 10:05  
References: Email-56  
Body:  
Adrienne,

Closing this one out, about five years later than I would have liked.

We took the RCE in 2014 and got the narrow claim allowed in 2015. The product group shipped fractional delay compensation in exactly one SKU, which was discontinued in 2017. The EP member was abandoned at the examination fee stage in 2016. The JP member granted and we have been paying annuities on it since, for no reason I can now articulate.

Bettina's portfolio review puts it in the bottom decile on every measure we use, and Eleanor's standing instruction is that anything in the bottom decile with no product and no licensing interest goes unless somebody makes a case for it. Nobody has made a case for it. Kirkbride-Nwosu left in 2016 and I am not going to invent an argument on his behalf.

So: do not pay the 7.5-year US maintenance fee due in November 2022, and I am instructing Minagawa-sensei separately to stop the Japanese annuities at the next due date.

One request. When you close your file, please make sure the closing letter carries MAL-2010-0329 in full, with the year and the title. I have spent a good part of this decade correcting people who read those four digits in a different order than the one they were written in, and I would rather not spend any of the next decade on a matter that is closed.

Dermot Achinstein
Patent Agent
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-113**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
From: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 桐原音響株式会社への実施許諾に対する同意について（不同意のご回答）  
Date: September 18, 2019, 16:35  
References: Email-102  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部長 園田 修 様
淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人 弁理士 皆川 悠子 先生

お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部の新開でございます。

8月2日付でご照会いただきました、桐原音響株式会社に対する特許第5498271号及び対応外国特許の実施許諾につき、9月11日開催の知的財産委員会において審議いたしました。まことに残念でございますが、本件については同意いたしかねます。以下、理由を申し述べます。

第一に、桐原音響株式会社は、日本国内の家庭用AVレシーバ市場において当社の直接の競合でございます。2018年度の国内出荷台数では当社に次ぐ第二位であり、当社との差は縮小しております。本特許発明は当社製品の主要な差別化要素の一つであり、これを競合他社に開放することは当社の事業に直接の不利益をもたらします。

第二に、ご提示のライセンス条件では対象製品が家庭用AVレシーバ全般とされており、用途による切り分けができておりません。仮に用途を業務用に限定していただけるのであれば、あらためて検討の余地がございます。

第三に、実施料の五割配分をもって当社の逸失利益が填補されるとは考えにくく、この点は金額の多寡の問題ではないと考えております。

念のため申し添えますが、本回答は特許法第73条第3項及び共同開発契約第7条に基づく当社の権利の行使でございます。同項は同意を留保する理由の相当性を要件としておらず、また共同開発契約第7条にも合理的理由なく同意を拒んではならない旨の定めはございません。過去三件について同意を差し上げてまいりましたのは、いずれも当社の事業と競合しない案件であったためでございます。

本件が貴社の事業計画に影響を及ぼすことは承知しております。用途限定その他の条件変更のご提案がございましたら、あらためてご相談を承ります。

新開 果林
知的財産部
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-114**  
To: Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Halvard Ostenfeld <halvard.ostenfeld@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 - recommendation to concede co-ownership and settle with Hanyoung  
Date: October 02, 2019, 17:30  
References: Email-111  
Body:  
Eleanor, Roswell,

I am recommending that we concede co-ownership of MAL-2011-0304 and settle. Serim's written opinion of August 14 and Hanul's separate read land in the same place, and I agree with them.

The short version is that we relied on the wrong half of a sentence for eight years.

The May 18, 2011 confirmation letter is genuine, was properly authorized, and says everything we have always said it says, except that its first paragraph ends with a sentence limiting the confirmation to the term of the joint development agreement. That agreement expired on March 31, 2014 and was not renewed. Hanyoung told us in writing on March 28, 2014 that it was expiring, and nobody joined the two facts together. Our English-language record of the letter, which is what San Mateo has read since 2011, is Mr. Bang's covering note of May 24, 2011, and the final sentence is not in it.

The exposure if we fight. If Hanyoung is right on the facts, the sole-name filing breaches Article 44 of the Korean Patent Act, which is an invalidity ground under Article 133(1)(ii) with no limitation period. We would be risking the whole registration in order to keep a share we probably do not have. Estoppel is weak, because the limitation was on the face of the very document we relied on.

The counterweight is that providing data does not by itself make a co-inventor, and their case on inventive contribution is thinner than their case on the letter. That is worth money in a negotiation and close to nothing in litigation.

Se-hoon has an indicative number from them of KRW 1.2 billion. I think we settle between 900 million and 1 billion, with cross-working rights so that neither side needs the other's consent to practice the invention itself.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-115**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 오현규 <h.oh@seocho-ipsvc.kr>  
Subject: 연차료 대행계약 Annex A(관할 목록) 개정안 - 대한민국 부분 회신 요청  
Date: October 24, 2019, 08:35  
References: Email-45  
Body:  
마스든 코리아
박세훈 매니저님께

서초IP서비스 오현규입니다. 지난주 통화에서 말씀하신 건에 대하여 개정안을 정리하여 보내드립니다.

귀사와 Pellworm & Sarno 사이의 연차료 대행계약 Annex A는 관할별로 서비스 등급을 정하고 있으며, 대한민국은 현재 제2류(귀사의 서면 지시가 있는 경우에만 납부)로 되어 있습니다. 당사는 해당 계약의 한국 하도급 대리인으로서 실제 납부를 수행합니다.

개정 요청 사항은 두 가지입니다.

첫째, 대한민국을 제1류(지시 없이도 기한 내 납부)로 상향하는 안입니다. 2018년에 지시 회신이 늦어져 추납기간에 납부한 건이 두 건 있었고, 추납 시 가산료가 발생하였습니다.

둘째, Annex A의 각주에 한국의 납부 구조를 명시하는 안입니다. 한국은 설정등록 시 제1년분부터 제3년분까지의 등록료를 일괄 납부하고 그 이후 매년 납부합니다. 심사 계속 중인 출원에는 연차료가 발생하지 않으므로, 현재 Annex A에 포함된 귀사의 계속 중인 출원 11건에 대하여는 당사가 청구할 항목이 없습니다. 이 점이 매년 예산 검토 때 문의 대상이 되고 있어 각주로 정리하는 편이 좋겠습니다.

회신은 11월 29일까지 부탁드립니다. 개정 발효는 2020년 1월 1일자를 예정하고 있습니다.

감사합니다.

오현규
대리
서초IP서비스

**Email-116**  
To: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Eleanor Bricknell <eleanor.bricknell@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 방지훈 <jhbang@hanul-ip.co.kr>, 정한슬 <hs.jeong@serim-law.kr>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0304 공동 소유 및 실시료 배분 합의서 체결 완료 (KRW 940,000,000)  
Date: November 08, 2019, 14:05  
References: Email-114  
Body:  
아시몰로우 님, 브릭넬 님

마스든 코리아 박세훈입니다. 금일 오전 안산에서 합의서 체결을 완료하였기에 보고드립니다. 합의서 원문이 한국어이므로 조항 인용의 정확성을 위하여 한국어로 작성합니다.

일. 명칭 및 일자: 「MAL-2011-0304 특허 공동 소유 및 실시료 배분에 관한 합의서」, 2019년 11월 8일자. 서명자는 당사 측 박세훈, 한영정밀 측 문하경 실장입니다.

이. 소유: KR 10-1602934 및 대응 외국특허(US, EP, JP, CN)에 대하여 양사 각 2분의 1의 공동 소유임을 확인합니다. 한국 특허등록원부의 공유자 추가 등록은 11월 하순 신청 예정입니다.

삼. 정산금: 당사가 한영정밀에 금 구억 사천만원(KRW 940,000,000)을 지급합니다. 2019년 11월 29일까지 일시 지급하며, 2011년부터 2019년까지의 과거 실시분에 대한 정산을 포함합니다.

사. 향후 실시료: 제3자 실시허락에 따른 순실시료는 각 2분의 1로 배분합니다.

오. 상호 실시: 양사는 상대방의 동의 없이 자기 실시할 수 있으며, 각자의 계열회사에 대한 실시허락에 대하여는 상호 사전에 포괄적으로 동의합니다. 제3자에 대한 실시허락은 특허법 제99조 제4항에 따라 종전대로 상호 서면 동의를 요합니다.

육. 부제소: 양사는 본건과 관련하여 무효심판 및 소송을 제기하지 아니합니다.

칠. 확인서: 2011년 5월 18일자 확인서가 2014년 3월 31일자로 그 효력을 다하였음을 양사가 확인합니다.

베리탄 등록은 크로지어 님께 요청드렸으며, 소유 상태는 Joint, 공유자는 한영정밀 주식회사로 기재됩니다.

박세훈
지식재산 매니저
마스든 코리아

**Email-117**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Larchmont Conferencing - inbound licensing enquiry on the beamforming family - MAL-2010-0392 (San Mateo series)  
Date: November 13, 2019, 15:30  
References:  
Body:  
Tomás,

Douglas Frearson at Larchmont Conferencing Systems called me on Monday. They are launching a ceiling-array conference product in the third quarter next year, their outside counsel has cleared everything except our beamforming family, and Frearson would rather talk about a license now than commit to tooling and talk about it later. That is a good sign and you should treat it as one.

The family is MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series. US 8,824,693, the divisional MAL-2010-0392D, EP 2 545 719 which survived the Kesselring opposition last year, Japanese patent 特許第5731049号, and a Chinese member that granted in 2016.

Three points before you take the call.

We own this outright. Sole ownership, single assignee, no joint development program behind it, no outside co-inventor, no program code on the matter, no third party whose consent we need in order to grant a license anywhere in the world. Trevor has walked the chain of title back to the two inventor assignments from 2010 and 2011 and it is clean. That is not true of everything in this portfolio and it is worth knowing which of your conversations are the easy ones. This is an easy one.

The EP member is the strong card. An opposition taken to oral proceedings and rejected without amendment is worth more across a negotiating table than three unexamined applications, and Frearson's counsel will know that.

Do not quote a rate on the first call. Frearson is good and he will anchor you if you let him.

Roswell Vantassel
Senior Vice President, Licensing
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-118**  
To: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 傅锦程 <fu.jincheng@hengyu-ip.cn>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: ZL 201310288455.7 无效宣告请求案：第42186号审查决定，专利权全部维持有效  
Date: November 19, 2019, 16:10  
References:  
Body:  
陆女士，您好。

上述无效宣告请求案的审查决定已下发，结论对我方完全有利，特此报告。

案情回顾。请求人江南声学科技有限公司于2019年3月14日提出无效宣告请求，彼时受理单位仍为专利复审委员会；自2019年4月1日机构调整后，本案由复审和无效审理部继续审理，合议组成员未变更。请求人主张的无效理由有三项：权利要求1至3不具备新颖性，权利要求1至12不具备创造性，以及说明书公开不充分。

证据方面，请求人提交了对比文件1（日本公开特许公报特开2009-192587号及其中文译文）、对比文件2（一篇2010年的会议论文）以及一份公证购买的产品说明书。我方在答复中对公证书的公开时间提出质疑，并指出对比文件2未记载权利要求1中的自适应分段步骤。

口头审理于2019年9月26日在北京进行，历时约四小时。合议组当庭重点调查了对比文件2的公开日与产品说明书的真实性。

第42186号审查决定于2019年11月11日发出，维持专利权全部有效。决定认为：公证购买的产品说明书未能证明其在本专利申请日前公开；对比文件1与2的结合不能得出权利要求1的技术方案，且本领域技术人员没有结合动机；说明书公开充分。

请求人可以自收到决定之日起三个月内向北京知识产权法院提起行政诉讼。我所将监控立案信息，若有起诉，第一时间告知贵司。

顺祝商祺。

傅锦程
专利代理师
恒宇知识产权代理有限公司

**Email-119**  
To: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>, Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Lessons learned from MAL-2011-0304 - four changes to how we handle confirmation letters  
Date: February 20, 2020, 09:12  
References: Email-116  
Body:  
All,

The Hanyoung Precision matter closed in November. It cost us KRW 940,000,000 and a half share of a registered Korean patent we thought was ours outright. Marisol asked for a short account of what we are changing. Here it is, and I would like everyone who touches an ownership record to read it.

What went wrong is narrow and it is boring. We held a confirmation letter that was limited in time, and we did not record the limitation anywhere a person would see it.

Four changes, effective now.

One. Every ownership confirmation from a counterparty gets read for scope before it is filed, and the scope goes into the Veritan Ownership Basis note in three parts: what subject matter it covers, what territory it covers, and how long it lasts. If any of the three is not stated in the document, the note says "not stated."

Two. If a confirmation is limited to the term of an agreement, that agreement's expiry date goes onto the matter as a diary event with a ninety day warning, and the warning goes to the responsible attorney, not to a shared mailbox.

Three. No ownership decision rests on a summary of a foreign-language document. Where the operative document is not in English we obtain a certified translation of the operative paragraphs and file it with the original. Yolande is retro-fitting this on the fifty-one matters whose Ownership Basis note cites a non-English document.

Four. A program code is not an ownership fact and an ownership fact is not a program code. Both directions of that confusion have cost us money in the last three years.

The point I want to leave you with is the first one. Ask whether the confirmation is time-limited. On this matter, one sentence at the end of one paragraph was the entire case, and for eight years the only English record of that paragraph did not contain it.

Bettina Crozier
Director, IP Operations
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-120**  
To: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0117 (Munich series) - EP granted, and a Veritan cleanup request on WO 2012/025431  
Date: March 04, 2020, 09:50  
References: Email-51  
Body:  
Bettina,

Two things, one good and one administrative.

The European patent on MAL-2011-0117, adaptive port compensation for vented enclosures, was mentioned as granted in the Bulletin on February 26. Validated in DE, FR, GB and ES. The opposition period runs to November 26, 2020 and I have diarized it. The US case, where we elected the enclosure claims back in 2013, granted in 2016, and the divisional on the tuning procedure is still pending.

The administrative item. Veritan is carrying the international publication number for this family as free text, and it appears in three different forms across the various members: "WO 2012/025431", "WO2012/025431 A1", and, on the Spanish validation record, "WO 2012/02543". Somebody has truncated it, probably in a column width, probably years ago.

I would like the publication number made a controlled field. Failing that, I would like all three normalized to WO 2012/025431 A1 and a validation rule that rejects a WO number of the wrong length.

I am asking because this is the second time in seven years that a search result or a report has come back to me keyed on a publication number in this range and I have had to work out by hand whether the document was ours. There are at least four published applications in the 2012/024xxx and 2012/025xxx ranges that are relevant to this technology and that differ from one another only in the order of two digits. Two of them are not ours at all. One belongs to a direct competitor, and circulating a competitor's publication internally under our own docket number is a mistake I would like to make structurally impossible rather than merely unlikely.

Beatrix Hollerith
European Patent Attorney
Marsden Europe GmbH, Munich

**Email-121**  
To: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Corinne Vasquez-Tanabe <c.vasquez-tanabe@shinonome-america.com>  
Cc: Adrienne Whitcomb <awhitcomb@whitcombreyes.com>  
Subject: Apologies - our Section 8 and 15 reminder reached your inbox by mistake  
Date: March 11, 2020, 17:25  
References: Email-61  
Body:  
Ms. Nandakumar,

My apologies for the docket reminder that arrived at your general IP address on Monday. It concerns US Registration No. 4,712,655 for SHINONOME in class 9, which is ours, and the combined Section 8 declaration of use and Section 15 declaration of incontestability due this year. Our outside counsel appears to have picked up a Marsden contact address from a 2011 trade show file when they set up the record, and it has been sitting there ever since. It is corrected now.

For completeness, since the reminder mentions our filings generally: Shinonome America, Inc. is the US sales and marketing subsidiary of the Osaka group and we hold the group's US trademark registrations. We do not hold or administer any patents and we are not a party to your patent agreements. If anything patent related ever arrives here it goes back to Japan unopened.

If it is useful to you, I can send our current US mark list so your watch service does not flag them again as unattributed. Please tell me who should receive it.

Kind regards,
Corinne Vasquez-Tanabe
General Counsel
Shinonome America, Inc.

**Email-122**  
To: 정하윤 <hy.jung@nasung-elec.co.kr>  
From: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 2020년 2분기 로열티 명세서 오발송에 대한 정정 안내  
Date: September 15, 2020, 09:05  
References:  
Body:  
나성전자 주식회사
라이선스 담당 정하윤 님

안녕하십니까. Marsden Audio Laboratories 한국 지식재산 담당 박세훈입니다. 8월 31일자로 귀사에 발송된 2020년 2분기 로열티 명세서에 오류가 있어 정정하여 안내드립니다. 먼저 혼란을 드린 점 사과드립니다.

원인은 다음과 같습니다. 당사는 2016년 2월 19일 사내 지식재산 관리시스템을 교체하였으며, 그 과정에서 실시권자 목록이 구 시스템의 자유기재 항목으로부터 자동 변환되었습니다. 구 시스템에는 실시권자를 특허 패밀리에 연결하는 항목이 따로 없었고, 계약 담당자가 메모란에 적어 둔 내용을 기계적으로 해석한 결과, 한국 실시권자 세 곳이 실제 계약 대상이 아닌 패밀리에 연결되었습니다. 그 상태가 4년 넘게 유지되어 왔습니다.

그 결과 2분기 명세서에는 귀사가 실시권을 보유하고 있지 않은 두 개의 패밀리(MAL-2015-0119, MAL-2016-0287)가 포함되었습니다. 해당 두 건을 제외한 정정 명세서를 오늘 별도로 송부드립니다. 청구 금액은 118,400,000원에서 94,700,000원으로 변경되며, 차액 23,700,000원은 3분기 청구서에서 공제하거나, 요청하시면 환급하여 드리겠습니다.

기존 명세서는 폐기하여 주시기 바랍니다. 아울러 2016년 1분기 이후의 모든 명세서를 재검토하고 있으며, 과오납이 확인될 경우 계약 제9조에 따라 소급하여 정산하겠습니다. 검토 결과는 11월 중 회신드리겠습니다.

박세훈
지식재산 매니저
마스든 코리아

**Email-123**  
To: Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marguerite Ashland-Poe <marguerite.ashland-poe@coastalpiedmont.edu>  
Subject: Coastal Piedmont - Amendment 3 signed - Annex A restated and the CPU-2017-041 ownership settled  
Date: September 17, 2020, 10:40  
References: Email-92  
Body:  
Kwabena,

Amendment 3 to the sponsored research agreement was signed yesterday and it closes both open items. Thank you for the eighteen months of patience; the answer we got is the one you proposed in the first meeting.

On CPU-2017-041, the parties have recorded the invention as jointly owned under Article 7.2. Our acoustician's April 2017 contribution to the rotating boom is accepted, and the University has added him as a co-inventor on the US application. Under 7.2 each party may practice without accounting to the other, so we do not need a license, and neither of us can grant an exclusive license alone. Marsden pays USD 145,000 toward the filing and prosecution costs already incurred and the parties share future costs equally. There is no running royalty. The first-option machinery in 7.1 falls away for this invention because 7.1 never applied to it.

On the go-forward structure, Annex A has been restated. It now has four tasks, and the new Task 4 — the reference implementation hardening work we are funding separately at USD 210,000 — is expressly carved out of Article 7 by a new 7.5, so inventions arising in Task 4 are the sole property of Marsden. That is what we wanted and I am told it is the first time this university has agreed to it.

Please update the agreement abstract, and make sure the Ownership Status on the CPU-2017-041 matter reads Joint with the co-owner named.

Marisol Etxeberria
Vice President and Chief IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-124**  
To: 富樫 志乃 <s.togashi@kasai-onkyo.co.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 部品供給基本契約 第12条 別紙A記載部品に係る改良発明の帰属について  
Date: November 12, 2020, 13:15  
References: Email-87  
Body:  
葛西音響部品株式会社
営業技術部 富樫 志乃 様

いつもお世話になっております。マースデン・ジャパン知的財産部の園田です。

先月の技術会議で話題となりました KO-2233-C のダンパー形状変更につきまして、権利の帰属を整理させていただきたく存じます。書面で確認しておきたいという趣旨であり、御社のご提案に異を唱えるものではございません。

部品供給基本契約 第12条は、別紙Aに掲げる部品に関して生じた改良発明について、次のとおり定めております。両社の従業員が共同でなした改良発明は両社の共有とし、持分は各2分の1とする。いずれか一方の従業員のみがなした改良発明は、当該一方の単独所有とする。共有に係る発明の出願は共同で行い、第三者への実施許諾には他方の同意を要する。

今回のダンパー形状（外周リブの二段化）は、御社加西工場のご担当者が単独で考案されたものと理解しております。したがって第12条により御社の単独所有であり、当社は権利を主張いたしません。その前提で、当社としては次の二点をお願いしたく存じます。

一、当社製品への使用について、非独占の実施許諾を無償で頂戴したく存じます。第12条後段の趣旨に沿うものと考えております。

二、出願をご予定の場合は、当社の量産切替（2021年6月）前の出願をご検討ください。切替後は当社側の製品資料が公知となる可能性がございます。

なお本件は別紙Aの改定とは別件として扱い、覚書は分けて締結させていただければ幸いです。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-125**  
To: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Journal archive request - MAL-2010-0518 - we need the date the design left the building  
Date: March 10, 2021, 11:15  
References:  
Body:  
Fenella,

A narrow request, and I have tried to scope it the way you asked people to.

MAL-2010-0518, parametric stereo upmix with decorrelation control. The EP member is under opposition and the opponent has pleaded public prior use. Their evidence is thin — a declaration from a former employee of a third party who says he saw the design described at an industry meeting in 2010 and cannot produce a document. Beatrix's view is that the declaration on its own will not carry the day.

Before we say that in writing to the Opposition Division, I want to know what our own record says about when this design was first described outside Marsden. If the opponent is wrong about the meeting but right about the year, I would rather find that out now than at oral proceedings.

Scope I am asking for: four custodians, being Wendell Aduba-Reiss and the three codec group members named on the disclosure, January 1 to December 31, 2010. Terms should include the working name of the algorithm, "decorrelation control", the meeting name, and the file names of the two contribution documents. Please also flag anything sent to an address outside marsdenlabs.com that carries either contribution document, regardless of the terms.

The priority date is October 12, 2010. Anything that left the building before that date is the answer I am afraid of, and it is the answer I need.

Kwabena Ofori-Lund
Senior Patent Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-126**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>, Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Q4 2020 royalty report - page 6 attributes the beamforming receipts to the wrong family  
Date: March 17, 2021, 11:40  
References: Email-62  
Body:  
Roswell, Bettina,

I signed the Q4 royalty report on Friday and then read it properly over the weekend, which is the wrong order to do those two things in. Page 6 is wrong and it needs to be reissued before it goes to Finance.

The report attributes USD 418,300 of Q4 receipts under the Larchmont Conferencing license to a Yokohama-series family. Those receipts arise under the beamforming license and they belong to MAL-2010-0392, San Mateo series: US 8,824,693 and its foreign members. The Larchmont license does not run to anything in the Yokohama series and never has. I have the executed license in front of me and its Schedule 1 lists four patents, all four from the beamforming family.

I can see how it happened. The Veritan royalty extract keys on the docket string with the series stripped. Both matters answer to "MAL-2010-0392" once you do that, and the Yokohama matter sorts first, so the extract took the first hit and stopped looking.

Three things.

Bettina, please reissue page 6 and tell me how many prior quarters are affected. I am assuming this is not the first one and I would rather know the number than guess it.

Second, the fix belongs in the extract and not in the report. If a report can be wrong in this way once, it will be wrong in this way again, and the next one may be a report that leaves the building.

Third, and this is the part I actually care about. A royalty report is a document that a counterparty may one day read as an admission about what we own and what we have licensed. Anything that attributes revenue to a family is, in unfriendly hands, a statement about that family. I do not want an accounting extract inventing positions on our behalf because a sort order was convenient.

Fenella, is this something your quarterly controls should be catching, or is it outside the scope you have been given? An honest answer helps me more than a reassuring one.

Marisol Etxeberria
Vice President and Chief Intellectual Property Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-127**  
To: Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>, Beatrix Hollerith <beatrix.hollerith@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Journal archive results - MAL-2010-0518 - the archive answered, and it answered against us  
Date: March 25, 2021, 16:50  
References: Email-125  
Body:  
Kwabena, Beatrix,

Search complete. Four custodians, January 1 to December 31, 2010, 3,100 journaled messages, eleven minutes of search time and two days of review. You will not like the result and I am giving it to you plainly.

The second contribution document left the building on August 26, 2010 at 09:14, sent by Wendell Aduba-Reiss to an external mailing list. The list is the working group reflector. Its subscriber list at that date, which is itself in the journal because the list manager mailed it round in July, had 214 addresses across 41 organizations. There is no confidentiality legend on the message, no NDA covering the reflector, and the reflector maintains a web archive that has been publicly readable since 2007.

The document describes the decorrelation control loop including the adaptation rule. I am not competent to say whether it discloses the claims, but it uses the same figure numbering as the application drawings and the equations look the same to me.

The priority date is October 12, 2010. That is six weeks and five days after the send.

There is a second item you should see. On August 30, 2010, one of the codec group members asked Wendell in writing whether the contribution should have gone out before the filing. There is no reply to that message anywhere in the journal.

I record for the file that this is the second search this team has run where the archive answered the question cleanly and the answer was not the one that was wanted. The archive is not a friendly witness. It is only a complete one.

Fenella Drozdov
Director, Information Governance
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-128**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 - Cape Lyford consent signed, plus annuity and family status  
Date: June 15, 2021, 14:12  
References: Email-113  
Body:  
Roswell, Trevor,

Two routine items on MAL-2011-0455.

Consent. Shinonome signed the consent for the Cape Lyford Media Pty Ltd license on June 10. Requested through Yodo-Naniwa on April 27, their committee took it on June 3, signature a week later. Scope: Cape Lyford only, the EP, JP and AU members, streaming service loudness compliance tooling, term of the license agreement, no sublicensing, no affiliates. The Article 73(3) recital is in it. Fourth consent, one refusal.

The refusal was Kirihara Onkyo in September 2019 and it still stands. Kirihara came back in February with the field cut down to professional installed audio, and Shinkai-san has told us informally that a narrowed request would be looked at properly. We have not filed it yet, because Kirihara have not decided whether the narrowed field is worth the royalty. That one is on Licensing, not on Shinonome.

Annuities. The ninth year fee on 特許第5498271号 falls due March 14, 2022. Yodo-Naniwa pays and invoices both proprietors fifty-fifty as they have since registration. The German and French national renewal fees on the EP member fall due at the end of April 2022; Munich handles those and recharges Shinonome through the same account.

Nothing here needs a decision. I am filing it for the record so that the consent history on this family stays in one thread.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-129**  
To: Se-hoon Park <s.park@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 윤태석 <ts.yoon@daehanip.co.kr>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 10-2019-0134782 의견제출통지서 접수 (대응기한 2021년 8월 9일)  
Date: June 16, 2021, 08:30  
References:  
Body:  
Marsden Korea
박세훈 매니저님

대한특허법인 윤태석입니다. 표제 출원에 대하여 2021년 6월 9일자 의견제출통지서가 발송되었으며 어제 수령하였습니다. 지정기간은 2개월이므로 대응기한은 2021년 8월 9일입니다. 기간연장은 1개월 단위로 신청할 수 있습니다.

거절이유는 두 가지입니다.

첫째, 특허법 제29조제2항 진보성 결여입니다. 인용발명 1은 공개특허공보 10-2015-0093471호, 인용발명 2는 US 2013/0170672 A1입니다. 심사관은 인용발명 1에 개시된 그룹 단위 지수 부호화에 인용발명 2의 오차 확산 기법을 결합하는 것이 통상의 기술자에게 용이하다고 판단하였습니다.

둘째, 특허법 제42조제4항제2호 기재불비입니다. 청구항 7의 "실질적으로 균일한"이라는 표현이 불명확하다는 지적입니다.

대응 방안을 말씀드립니다. 청구항 1에 그룹 크기 적응 한정사항을 도입하는 보정서와, 두 인용발명의 결합 곤란성 및 명세서 표 3의 현저한 효과를 근거로 하는 의견서를 함께 제출할 것을 권해 드립니다. 해당 한정사항은 명세서 문단 [0061]에 근거가 있으므로 신규사항 추가 문제는 발생하지 않습니다. 청구항 7은 "편차가 3퍼센트 이내인"으로 한정하겠습니다.

참고로 본 출원은 2019년 출원이므로 심사청구기간은 출원일부터 3년이며, 심사청구는 출원 시에 이미 완료되어 있습니다.

예상 비용은 관납료를 포함하여 1,650,000원입니다. 7월 16일까지 지시하여 주시면 기한 내 제출에 무리가 없습니다.

윤태석
변리사
대한특허법인

**Email-130**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Shinonome - SDX-7 family, rate, and where the renewal clock stands  
Date: June 16, 2021, 15:05  
References: Email-96  
Body:  
Roswell,

Three things on the Shinonome Semiconductor license, in order of how soon they bite.

SDX-7. They have told us informally that SDX-7A samples in Q4 and that it carries the licensed block. Under the amended schedule it is a licensed product the moment it contains the block, so there is nothing to negotiate on coverage; the only question is rate. They will ask for a step below USD 0.037 on the argument that SDX-7 is a lower-ASP part. My recommendation is to hold the 0.037 and give volume relief only above 20 million units.

Audit. Closed. The 2018 field work found under-reporting of 4.1% concentrated in the SDX-5C line, they paid USD 214,000 in settlement in March 2019 including audit costs, and the reconciliation process has held up since. No further action.

Renewal. Term runs to December 31, 2022 with automatic three-year renewal unless either party gives notice six months out, so the notice date is June 30, 2022, twelve months from now. If we want a restructure — and I think we do, because the territory definition is still "Japan and such other countries as the parties may agree" — we have to give notice and negotiate under time pressure, or open the conversation early and keep the automatic renewal as our fallback. I prefer the second.

I will put a paper to you in September with the rate model.

Tomás Renaudin-Whyte
Licensing Manager
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-131**  
To: 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 移行由来の設定誤りに関する日本案件の点検完了報告（41件中35件是正済み）  
Date: October 05, 2021, 14:25  
References: Email-104  
Body:  
都築先生

園田でございます。2017年より継続しておりました、システム移行に由来する設定誤りの点検につきまして、日本案件分の作業が概ね完了いたしましたのでご報告申し上げます。長期にわたりご協力を賜り、誠にありがとうございました。

対象は日本案件41件、是正済みが35件、残り6件は貴所以外の代理人にご担当いただいている案件で、年内に完了する見込みでございます。

誤りが生じていた項目は三つでございました。年金管理区分が17件、代理人区分が7件、期限計算グループが17件でございます（重複あり）。いずれも2016年2月19日の移行時に、旧システムの別目的の項目から機械的に導出されたものでございます。導出規則そのものは文書化されておりましたが、導出結果が正しいかどうかを案件ごとに検証する工程が計画に含まれておりませんでした。

実害としては、係属中の出願に対する年金納付指示の返戻が2件、出願審査請求期限の誤表示が1件でございました。いずれも期限の徒過には至っておりません。

今後の運用について、社内で次のとおり定めました。特許庁の記録及び代理人事務所の期限管理台帳を正本とし、当社システムの表示は参考値として扱います。両者に相違がある場合は、確認を待たず代理人の記録に従います。また、年に一度、貴所の台帳と当社の一覧を突合させていただきたく存じます。第一回は来年1月にお願いできれば幸いでございます。

引き続きよろしくお願い申し上げます。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-132**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0596 の2012年の補正指示書について（アーカイブ検索を依頼すべきか）  
Date: February 09, 2022, 10:15  
References:  
Body:  
園田部長

黒岩でございます。ライセンシーとの係争案件につきまして、ご判断を仰ぎたく存じます。

MAL-2011-0596（日本国特許第5462813号）について、ライセンシーである甲社は、当社が2012年に行った手続補正により請求項1の範囲が減縮され、同社製品は現在の請求項の技術的範囲に属しないと主張しております。同社は、当時当社が代理人に対して発出した補正指示書に減縮の意図が明記されているはずであり、その開示を求めるという立場でございます。

当社側にこの指示書が見当たりません。現行システムには2014年1月1日以降の記録しか移行されておらず、当時の代理人でありました北浜特許事務所は2015年に閉鎖、所属の弁理士の方々は複数の事務所に分散しておられます。都築先生に照会いただきましたところ、承継された包袋は特許庁提出書類の写しのみで、依頼者との往復書簡は含まれていないとのことでございました。

本社の情報ガバナンス部門にメールジャーナルの検索を依頼することは可能でございます。ただ、懸念が一点ございます。当時、北浜事務所への指示は横浜事業所の複合機からファクスで送付する運用でございました。複合機のファクス送受信がメール基盤に取り込まれるようになったのは2014年以降でございますので、そもそもジャーナルに残っていない可能性が高いと考えております。

費用は発生しないとのことでございます。空振りの可能性は高いものの、依頼してみる価値はあると考えますが、いかがいたしましょうか。

黒岩 千尋
特許エンジニア
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-133**  
To: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>, 都築 和久 <tsuzuki@sanjo-tokkyo.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: RE: MAL-2011-0596 の2012年の補正指示書について（検索結果：該当なし、和解の方針）  
Date: March 03, 2022, 15:40  
References: Email-132  
Body:  
黒岩さん
都築先生

園田でございます。情報ガバナンス部門による検索結果が出ましたので共有いたします。結論として、該当する記録はございませんでした。

検索範囲は2011年1月1日から2013年12月31日まで、横浜及びサンマテオの全メールボックス、対象は約10万通、所要時間は14分でございました。ヒットはゼロでございます。ドロズドフ部長からは、当時の複合機からのファクス送信はメール基盤を経由しておらず、ジャーナルには原理的に残り得ない旨、書面でご確認をいただいております。黒岩さんのご懸念のとおりでございました。

したがいまして、2012年の補正の意図を示す当社側の一次資料は存在いたしません。特許庁提出の手続補正書と意見書は当然残っておりますが、意見書には減縮の趣旨を積極的に述べた記載がなく、甲社の主張を否定する材料にはなりません。

この点を前提に、事業部門と協議のうえ、和解の方針といたしました。内容は、解決金の支払いはなく、その代わりに2022年4月1日から3年間、実施料率を0.42パーセントから0.35パーセントへ引き下げるものでございます。逸失額は約4,600万円と試算しております。訴訟に移行した場合の費用と、請求項の解釈が公の場で確定することの不利益を勘案した判断でございます。事業部門の承認は3月1日に得ております。

一点、社内の教訓として記録に残しておきたいことがございます。記録が存在しないということは、記録が作成されなかったことを意味するのであって、事実が存在しなかったことを意味するものではございません。今回はその区別ができなかったために譲歩しております。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-134**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 新開 果林 <k.shinkai@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 会社分割による事業承継のご通知（東雲デバイスソリューションズ株式会社の設立について）  
Date: April 05, 2022, 08:45  
References:  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部 園田 修 様

平素より格別のお引き立てを賜り厚く御礼申し上げます。東雲電機株式会社 知的財産部の新開でございます。

このたび弊社は、2022年4月1日をもちまして、デバイスソリューション事業を新設分割により承継させ、東雲デバイスソリューションズ株式会社を設立いたしましたので、ご通知申し上げます。

新会社の概要は下記のとおりです。

商号：東雲デバイスソリューションズ株式会社
本店所在地：大阪市中央区（弊社と同一ビル内、フロアは異なります）
事業内容：車載および業務用音響デバイスの開発・製造・販売
連絡先ドメイン：shinonome-ds.co.jp

東雲電機株式会社は引き続き存続し、素材事業および産業機器事業を継続いたします。知的財産部は当面、両社の業務を兼務体制で担当いたしますが、2023年度中に分離する予定です。

契約関係につきましては、個別の契約ごとに承継の有無および承継先が異なりますので、対象となる契約については別途、書面にてご案内申し上げます。本通知をもって既存契約の当事者が変更されるものではございませんので、その旨ご了知くださいますようお願い申し上げます。

なお、ご請求書の宛先および技術窓口につきましては、5月以降順次ご連絡いたします。

今後とも変わらぬご厚誼を賜りますようお願い申し上げます。

新開 果林
知的財産部
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-135**  
To: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Veritan party records - six entities in our files read "Shinonome" - stop merging them  
Date: May 18, 2022, 12:10  
References: Email-134  
Body:  
Trevor, Yolande,

The carve-out notice has produced the third request this month to "merge the duplicate Shinonome parties." There are no duplicates. There are six distinct legal entities in our files whose name reads Shinonome, and merging any two of them would be a data loss we could not reconstruct. I have locked the party records against merge and I am writing the list down so that it exists somewhere other than in my head.

PTY-004118 東雲電機株式会社 / Shinonome Electric Co., Ltd., Osaka, shinonome-denki.co.jp. Counterparty on a joint development program.

PTY-071902 東雲デバイスソリューションズ株式会社 / Shinonome Device Solutions K.K., Osaka, shinonome-ds.co.jp. New. Carved out April 1, 2022.

PTY-004631 東雲セミコンダクター株式会社 / Shinonome Semiconductor Co., Ltd., Kumamoto, shinonome-semi.co.jp. A licensee of ours. Same group, different company, no development relationship.

PTY-005507 Shinonome America, Inc., Irvine CA, shinonome-america.com. US sales subsidiary. Trademarks only. No patent matters, ever.

PTY-062240 東雲精密工業株式会社 / Shinonome Seimitsu Kogyo Co., Ltd., Nagano, shinonome-seimitsu.co.jp. Precision machining supplier. No relation to the Osaka group — different owners, different shareholders, the characters are simply the same.

PTY-088014 東雲興業株式会社 / Shinonome Kogyo Co., Ltd. Construction. Facilities only, no IP relationship at all.

Two rules from today. Search by party ID, not by name. And "Shinonome" on its own in a subject line is not an identification of anybody — if you write it, put the entity in the first sentence of the body.

Bettina Crozier
Director, IP Operations
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-136**  
To: 別府 直哉 <n.beppu@shinonome-semi.co.jp>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 東雲様 ライセンス契約更新のご相談（2023年1月1日以降の取扱い）  
Date: September 15, 2022, 16:35  
References: Email-130  
Body:  
東雲セミコンダクター株式会社
ライセンス管理部 別府 直哉 様

お世話になっております。マースデン・ジャパンの園田でございます。熊本の御社宛のご連絡です。同名の他社様に届くことのないよう、宛先をご確認いただけますと幸いです。

2011年7月1日発効の特許ライセンス契約につきまして、契約期間が2022年12月31日をもって満了いたします。第14条により、いずれの当事者からも6か月前までに不更新の通知がない限り3年間自動更新されるところ、本年6月30日までに通知はなく、既に自動更新の要件を満たしております。したがいまして、契約は2025年12月31日まで自動的に継続いたします。

その上で、当社としては下記2点について、更新期間中の覚書による手当てをご相談させていただきたく存じます。

一、対象地域。第1.14条は「日本国および両当事者が合意するその他の国」と定めておりますが、実態として御社製品は欧州および北米に相当量が流入しております。地域の拡大と料率の見直しをあわせてご検討いただきたく存じます。

二、報告単位。SDX-7系列につきまして、製品呼称ごとの数量報告をお願いいたします。ダイ単位でのご報告では突合ができません。

11月中旬に大阪または熊本でお打合せの機会を頂戴できませんでしょうか。候補日を3つほどご提示いただければ調整いたします。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-137**  
To: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 邵文卿 <shao.wenqing@chenwei-micro.com.cn>  
Cc: Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>, 陈慕青 <chen.muqing@chenwei-micro.com.cn>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 关于 JD-CHW-12 项目成果专利权属的函（辰威法函〔2022〕第074号）  
Date: October 18, 2022, 11:50  
References:  
Body:  
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.
首席知识产权法务官 Etxeberria 女士

辰威微电子有限公司法务部谨致此函。

一、事实。贵我双方于2012年3月15日签署《联合开发协议》（项目编号 JD-CHW-12），实施期限自2012年4月1日至2015年3月31日。协议第五条约定：在本合作项目实施过程中产生的一切发明创造、技术秘密及其他知识产权，无论是否属于附件A所列工作包的范围，均由双方共同所有，各占百分之五十的份额；任何一方不得单独以自己名义就前述发明创造申请专利。协议第十八条约定中文本为准。

二、我司经检索发现，贵司以自己名义单独申请并已获授权的下列专利，其技术方案系在项目实施期间形成：中国 ZL 201480004871.2、美国 US 9,437,208 B2、欧洲 EP 2 945 158 B1、日本 特許第6273418号，优先权日为2013年1月15日。上述专利权利要求1所记载的"对一组对象位置矢量计算共享指数并按对象编码尾数差值"的方案，系2012年10月上海集成周期间由我司周立群、邵旻等工程师提出，首个定点实现由我司完成（代码库提交号 r2871），相关记录及白板照片均在我司项目档案中留存。

三、我司要求：
（一）自收到本函之日起三十日内，书面确认上述四件专利由双方共同所有，并配合办理各国著录项目变更或者权利人变更登记；
（二）提供自2016年1月1日起该专利族全部许可收入的账目及分配方案；
（三）就中国专利，在权属确认前，我司保留依据《中华人民共和国专利法》第十四条单独以普通许可方式许可他人实施并按法定规则分配许可费的权利。

四、逾期未获妥善答复的，我司将向上海知识产权法院提起专利权权属纠纷之诉，并不再受本函所载和解意向的约束。

顺致
商祺

邵文卿
法务总监
辰威微电子有限公司

**Email-138**  
To: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: RE: Chenwei - I have now read Article 5 and it does not say what we have all been assuming  
Date: November 09, 2022, 09:40  
References: Email-137, Email-70  
Body:  
Marisol, Roswell,

I have spent three weeks building the Annex A argument and I am going to stop, because Annex A is irrelevant. Here is Article 5 of JD-CHW-12 in the English courtesy translation that Veritan holds:

"Article 5 (Ownership of Intellectual Property). All inventions, technical know-how and other intellectual property arising in the course of the implementation of this program, whether or not falling within the scope of the work packages listed in Annex A, shall be jointly owned by the two Parties in equal shares of fifty percent (50%) each. Neither Party shall file a patent application in its own name alone in respect of the foregoing."

Whether or not falling within the scope of Annex A. Every hour anyone has spent on the work package list has been spent on a question the agreement expressly removes. I have gone back through the 2012 and 2013 traffic we hold and I cannot find anyone who quotes this clause, on our side, ever.

Before anyone else has to say it: the note in the program file recording the opposite reading is mine, from March 2018, and I wrote in it that I had not pulled the executed agreement. Jianing's close-out letter to Chenwei of June 2016 applies the same wrong test and states it to them in writing. Eleanor's January 2013 decision rests on it as well. Three of us reached the same answer by reading the work package list instead of the ownership clause, and I am the one who had least excuse.

Three further problems.

Article 18 provides that the Chinese text governs. Veritan holds only the English translation. Jianing has been to the contracts vault and the Chinese execution copy is not there either; the vault record shows a scanned English signature page and nothing else.

Second, Veritan has recorded MAL-2012-0688 as Joint, Chenwei Microsystems Co., Ltd., fifty percent, continuously since February 19, 2016. Yolande Grimbeek flagged that the record contradicted the register a week after cutover and asked for an instruction. There is no answer to her question anywhere. Our own system has said for six and a half years that we owe them half of this family, and we have never paid them anything.

Third, our license representations to four China licensees state that Marsden is sole proprietor of the CN member. Roswell, I need the exposure number on that by Friday.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-139**  
To: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 楼砚秋 <lou.yanqiu@jinglin-law.cn>  
Cc: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 辰威微电子权属主张的初步法律意见  
Date: December 07, 2022, 14:45  
References: Email-138  
Body:  
Etxeberria 女士、陆女士：

京琳律师事务所楼砚秋。就辰威法函〔2022〕第074号，谨提出初步意见如下。

一、关于第五条的解释。该条文义清楚，"无论是否属于附件A所列工作包的范围"是明示的排除性表述，不存在按《民法典》第一百四十二条进行目的解释以缩小其范围的空间。贵司拟主张的"附件A范围外故不属项目成果"，在该条之下没有意义。此外第十八条约定以中文本为准，贵司内部使用的英文译本即使有出入，也不影响结论。

二、关于"项目实施过程中产生"。这是唯一有争点的要件。贵司的优先权日为2013年1月15日，落在实施期限（2012年4月1日至2015年3月31日）之内；技术交底书上载有项目编号；发明的定点实现在对方代码库中。以上事实对贵司不利。可争之处仅在于构思地点与构思时间，需要贵司提供2012年10月之前的独立构思证据。若无此类证据，本项抗辩难以成立。

三、关于中国专利的许可。请贵司注意，即使确认共有，依《中华人民共和国专利法》第十四条，共有人可以单独实施，也可以以普通许可方式许可他人实施该专利，所收取的使用费应当在共有人之间分配。这意味着：辰威可以在不经贵司同意的情况下，向贵司在中国的竞争对手发放普通许可，贵司只能取得一半使用费；贵司亦然。共有的实际后果，在中国远比在日本或者德国宽松，对权利人而言则意味着无法控制被许可人的范围。

四、另有一项风险请特别注意。2014年1月14日恒宇知识产权曾就当时《专利法》第二十条（现行第十九条）保密审查事宜致函贵司，贵司于同月20日书面答复称发明的实质性内容在圣马特奥完成。若在本次争议中查明该技术方案的实质性内容形成于中国境内，则中国专利存在因违反该条而不被授予专利权、进而被宣告无效的风险，且该风险由贵司单方承担，辰威并无损失。对方目前未提出此点。

五、建议。在对方起诉前以协商方式解决，并将谈判重心放在许可安排与对价，而非权属本身。

楼砚秋
合伙人 律师
京琳律师事务所

**Email-140**  
To: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Journal archive request - JD-CHW-12 - looking for a confirmation letter, or for the fact that we never asked for one  
Date: January 09, 2023, 08:15  
References: Email-138  
Body:  
Fenella,

Trevor's note has left us with one factual question that nobody in this company can currently answer: did we, at any point between 2012 and 2015, obtain from Chenwei anything in writing saying that the 0688 subject matter sat outside the program? Veritan cannot answer it. Nothing on this matter predates January 1, 2014, because the 2016 cutover carried correspondence only from that date forward and the old system was decommissioned in June 2018.

The journal is the one place left. It was never in scope for the migration, and it was never in scope because it is not a docketing system — it is a copy of the mail. We have journaled every mailbox on the corporate tenant since 2008 and nobody has ever applied a retention schedule to it, because the schedule was written for systems of record and the journal is not one. So the 2012 to 2015 Chenwei traffic should still be sitting there exactly as sent.

Two things about method, learned the expensive way in March 2021.

Search by custodian and date range, January 1, 2012 to December 31, 2015, not by matter number. The docket number is San Mateo's internal string and roughly half of this correspondence is in Chinese and will not contain it.

Do not filter on the English terms. If Chen Muqing and Han Liheng were writing to us, they were writing in Chinese, and nothing they wrote will contain the words "ownership" or "Annex A." Please have someone add 确认书, 确认函, 权属, 共有, 附件A and 第五条 to the term set.

One scoping note that helps you: Marsden had no Shanghai mailboxes on the tenant before June 2016, so everything Chenwei sent us landed in San Mateo.

What I want, in order: any confirmation in either language; failing that, any request from us for one; and the Chinese execution copy of the agreement with its Annex A, which we do not hold anywhere.

Bettina Crozier
Director, IP Operations
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-141**  
To: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 楼砚秋 <lou.yanqiu@jinglin-law.cn>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Journal archive search results - JD-CHW-12 - there is no letter, and there is something worse  
Date: January 18, 2023, 12:52  
References: Email-140  
Body:  
It is not there.

Scope of the search: every Marsden mailbox journaled between January 1, 2012 and December 31, 2015, being 176,300 messages across San Mateo, Munich and Yokohama. The hit list, the term set in both languages, the deduplication method and the chain of custody are in search record IG-2023-041. The search took nineteen minutes.

What we have.

One. No confirmation letter. Not in Chinese, not in English, not in either direction. There are 412 messages between Marsden mailboxes and chenwei-micro.com.cn across the four years. None contains 确认书, 确认函, confirmation, 权属, ownership, 附件A or Annex A in a sense that touches this question. Nobody ever asked Chenwei for a confirmation. Nobody ever refused to give one. The question was never put to them at all.

Two. Eleanor Bricknell's message of January 22, 2013, intact, in which the question of asking for a confirmation is raised by Dermot Achinstein and answered in the negative, with three numbered reasons. The second reason is that asking would put a live ownership question in front of Chenwei while a commercial negotiation was open. It is the most quotable document in the set and every word of it is quotable against us.

Three. A message of May 14, 2013 from Wendell Aduba-Reiss to Dermot Achinstein, subject line about claim scope on this matter. It says the shared exponent came off a whiteboard at the Shanghai integration week of October 15, 2012 with four Chenwei engineers in the room, names Shao Min as the author of the first fixed-point implementation and cites their commit number, and says there is a photograph of the whiteboard in the integration week minutes which Chenwei circulated to both sides. Claim 1 of all four granted members is the shared exponent. I am not a patent lawyer and I am not going to characterize it, but I have read the assertion letter and it describes the same whiteboard.

Four. Chen Muqing's message of September 11, 2013 to Halvard Ostenfeld, in Chinese, quoting Article 5 and asking Marsden for a list of applications filed on program results. There is no reply to it in the journal. I searched twice and then had a second analyst search independently.

Five. The Chinese execution copy of the agreement, Annex A included, attached to Chen Muqing's message of March 19, 2012. So we do have it, and have had it for eleven years.

Six. Four Chinese-language messages into San Mateo mailboxes between 2012 and 2014 with no reply and no evidence that anyone had them translated.

A word about the method, because people will draw the wrong lesson from March 2021 and from this. The search took nineteen minutes. It would have taken nineteen minutes in October when the letter arrived, in 2016 when Yolande asked her question and got no answer, or in 2013. The archive is not on our side. It is not on anyone's side. It kept what was sent, and what was sent here is a decision not to ask a question and an engineer being honest in writing about where an idea came from.

Fenella Drozdov
Director, Information Governance
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-142**  
To: Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Device Solutions asks whether the Semiconductor license covers its products - it does not  
Date: March 09, 2023, 10:20  
References: Email-135  
Body:  
Tomás,

Shinonome Device Solutions K.K. (PTY-071902, the entity carved out of Shinonome Electric on April 1, 2022) has written to the licensing inbox asking us to confirm that its audio device products are "covered by the existing group license." The short answer is no, and I would like us to say so cleanly and quickly rather than let it sit.

The licensee under the 2011 patent license agreement is 東雲セミコンダクター株式会社 (PTY-004631, Kumamoto). Section 1.9 defines Licensee as that company and its majority-owned subsidiaries. Device Solutions is not a subsidiary of Shinonome Semiconductor; it is a sister company under the same ultimate parent, and the agreement has no affiliate-of-affiliate language and no change-of-control extension. Nothing that happened in the 2022 reorganization moved the license.

I want two things in the reply. First, the entity statement: name both companies with their party IDs so there is no ambiguity six months from now about who was told what. Second, a positive offer — if they want coverage, we will quote a separate license, and I would rather have that conversation than an infringement conversation with a company that has just been created.

Bettina, please make sure the inbound letter is filed against PTY-071902 and not against the license file. It arrived on the licensing thread and the natural thing is to drop it there, which would be exactly the error your May note warned about.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-143**  
To: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2010-0392D - data quality item - the trailing D is being read as four different things  
Date: May 16, 2023, 14:25  
References: Email-48  
Body:  
Docket,

Raising this as a data quality item rather than a docketing error, because nothing in Veritan is actually wrong. The problem is what people do with what Veritan gives them.

MAL-2010-0392D is the 2013 divisional of the San Mateo beamforming family, adaptive microphone-array beamforming for conference endpoints. Granted 2016. Maintenance fee at 7.5 years due next year. Ownership Status is Sole, same as the parent, and correctly so.

In the last eighteen months I have found the trailing D interpreted as four different things by four different people: a document-type marker meaning "divisional paperwork," a draft indicator, a duplicate flag, and, twice, a letter appended by hand to a docket number belonging to a different series entirely, on the theory that somebody at some point had been distinguishing two matters that shared a number.

That last one is the one that worries me, and it is not a hypothetical. Anyone searching a shared drive or an email archive for a bare docket number will get this matter back in their results whether or not the number they typed was ours, because a substring match does not know what a series is and does not know what a suffix is.

Three fixes, in increasing order of effort.

Add "Divisional of MAL-2010-0392 (San Mateo series), adaptive microphone-array beamforming for conference endpoints" to the matter's short description, so it shows in every list view and every export.

Stop using bare letter suffixes on new divisionals and adopt a hyphenated form instead.

Retire the letter suffix across the 41 existing matters that carry one.

I will do the first this week regardless. The third needs a decision from Bettina and a change window.

Yolande Grimbeek
IP Operations Analyst
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-144**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 志水 直哉 <n.shimizu@shinonome-denki.co.jp>  
Cc: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2011-0455 Ottersen Digital A/S への実施許諾に対する同意書送付の件  
Date: May 23, 2023, 10:50  
References: Email-128  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部長 園田 修 様
特許エンジニア 黒岩 千尋 様

お世話になっております。東雲電機 知的財産部 ライセンスグループの志水でございます。新開の休職に伴い、本年4月より共有特許に関する同意手続を担当しております。

4月11日付でご照会いただきました Ottersen Digital A/S（デンマーク王国オーフス市）への実施許諾につき、5月16日開催の知的財産委員会において承認されましたので、同意書に押印のうえ本日発送いたしました。写しは淀川・浪速国際特許業務法人経由でお送りしております。

同意の範囲は下記のとおりでございます。

一、対象特許は特許第5498271号並びに欧州、米国、韓国及び中国の各対応特許でございます。

二、実施権者は Ottersen Digital A/S に限り、同社の関係会社には及びません。

三、対象製品は放送事業者向けラウドネス管理ソフトウェアに限ります。同社が家庭用機器分野に参入される場合は、あらためて当社の書面同意を要します。

四、期間は実施許諾契約の有効期間に限ります。

五、再実施許諾は認めません。

六、本同意は特許法第73条第3項に基づくものであり、当社の持分の譲渡又は希釈を伴うものではございません。

七、実施料は共同開発契約第8条に従い、純実施料額の二分の一を半期ごとに当社へお支払いいただきます。

本件は本特許に係る五件目の同意でございます。従前どおり、対象製品分野が当社の事業と競合しない案件につきましては、迅速に対応してまいる所存でございます。

志水 直哉
知的財産部 ライセンスグループ
東雲電機株式会社

**Email-145**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Jianing Lu <j.lu@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>, Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>, 楼砚秋 <lou.yanqiu@jinglin-law.cn>  
Subject: Chenwei - settled. Co-ownership recorded, USD 1,240,000, and we have given up China  
Date: June 14, 2023, 17:10  
References: Email-141  
Body:  
The Co-Ownership and Royalty Sharing Agreement with Chenwei Microsystems Co., Ltd. was executed in Shanghai on June 12 and is effective. Terms, and then why.

One. Co-ownership of MAL-2012-0688 in equal fifty percent shares across all four granted members, effective from the original filing dates. The CNIPA 著录项目变更 was filed June 13, the USPTO assignment was recorded the same day at reel/frame 063918/0442, the EPO Rule 22 request has gone in, and the JPO 移転登録 is with Tsuzuki-sensei. MAL-2013-0402, the other JD-CHW-12 matter that was filed in our name alone, went abandoned in 2017 for non-payment of the US issue fee and Chenwei did not include it.

Two. Marsden pays USD 1,240,000. USD 905,000 is Chenwei's fifty percent of royalties allocated to this family from the 2016 through 2022 bundles, USD 190,000 is interest at the contractual rate, USD 145,000 is their costs. Two tranches, July 14 and October 13.

Three. The part that will cost more than the money. Marsden covenants not to grant any license under ZL 201480004871.2 without Chenwei's prior written consent. Chenwei gives no reciprocal covenant. Under Article 14 of the PRC Patent Law a co-owner may grant a non-exclusive license on its own and simply share the royalties, so Chenwei keeps that freedom in full and we have contracted ours away. Read plainly: Chenwei may license our Chinese competitors tomorrow without asking us, and remit us half of whatever it charges, and we may not license anyone at all without asking them.

Four. What that is worth. This family carried an allocated USD 410,000 of the 2022 China bundle across four licensees. Three of those agreements renew in 2024 and 2025 and each renewal now needs a consent we cannot compel. Roswell's revised model takes forward China revenue on this family down sixty percent — half the royalty gone as of right, the rest discounted for consent risk. That is the real number, not the 1.24 million.

Five. Why we settled rather than fought. We had nothing. There is no confirmation, there is no request for one, and there is a January 2013 memorandum from our own Chief IP Counsel setting out why we chose not to ask. On top of that our own Director of Standards described Chenwei's contribution to the claimed feature in writing in May 2013, and Lou Yanqiu's view is that a Shanghai court reads that as an admission and stops reading. The Article 19 secrecy examination exposure was not raised by Chenwei and we did not raise it either.

Six. Veritan. The Ownership Status field on this matter reads Joint and has read Joint since February 19, 2016. As of Monday it is correct. Nobody should take any comfort from that sentence; the field was right by accident for seven years and nobody ever checked whether it was right for a reason.

Marisol Etxeberria
VP & Chief IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

To: Priyasha Nandakumar <priyasha.nandakumar@marsdenlabs.com>
