Adrienne Whitcomb
Whitcomb Reyes LLP

**Email-147**  
To: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Tomás Renaudin-Whyte <tomas.renaudin-whyte@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: I need everything we ever said to Verrazzano Broadcast about the 2011 rate  
Date: February 21, 2024, 10:05  
References:  
Body:  
Fenella,

Verrazzano Broadcast Systems has stopped paying and their counsel sent us a letter on Monday. Their position is that at a meeting in New York in the autumn of 2011, someone from Marsden gave an oral assurance that they would never be charged more than the most favorable rate given to any broadcast licensee. They say that assurance was repeated in correspondence. Our agreement has no most-favored-licensee clause and an integration clause that should dispose of the whole argument, but they are asserting promissory estoppel and their number is USD 6.4 million in claimed overpayment.

I want to know what we actually said. Not what the contract says — what our people wrote to them and about them.

Please search everything. Every mailbox, 2008 to today, anything mentioning Verrazzano or their two subsidiaries, anything mentioning most favored nation or MFN or best rate in a broadcast context, and the calendar entries for whoever was in New York that autumn. I would rather have too much than miss the one message that decides this.

How fast can you have it, and do you need anything from me to start? Tomás can give you the licensee aliases and the old entity names — they were Verrazzano Media Holdings until 2014 and there are at least two other trading names in the file.

The litigation hold went out this morning to Licensing and to Finance.

Roswell Vantassel
SVP Licensing
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-148**  
To: Roswell Vantassel <roswell.vantassel@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Fenella Drozdov <fenella.drozdov@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: RE: Verrazzano - not at that scope, and here is what it would cost  
Date: March 06, 2024, 13:50  
References: Email-147, Email-141  
Body:  
Roswell,

I have scoped what you asked for and I am declining to run it as specified. Here is the arithmetic and then the reason that matters more than the arithmetic.

What you asked for is sixteen years across 611 custodians, being 4.7 million journaled messages. Most of that is recent — tenant volume has roughly doubled every four years, so a single year now is worth six or seven of the years you actually care about. Your term set, first pass, returns 61,400 documents after deduplication and family grouping. That is not a number anyone at Marsden reviews internally. External review at USD 1.35 per document is USD 82,900, hosting at USD 14.50 per gigabyte per month across the eleven weeks the review takes is another USD 13,500, so USD 96,400 before a single privileged document is looked at twice, and privilege review is the expensive half of any review. My annual budget for search support is USD 60,000 and it is committed through Q3.

The reason that matters more. A sixteen-year unbounded collection across 611 custodians on a live dispute creates a corpus that exists, that is preservable, and that the other side can ask for in its entirety once they learn it exists. Right now their discovery burden is theirs. If I build them a searchable set of 4.7 million of our messages, some of it becomes their evidence and none of it stops being discoverable because we did not like what it said.

What I will run this week, at no cost and in about half a day: the four people who could plausibly have been in that room, plus the licensing shared mailbox, January 1, 2011 through December 31, 2012. That is roughly 52,000 messages. Give me a fifth name if you have one.

For calibration: the Chenwei search in January 2023 covered four years and three sites and took nineteen minutes, because it was scoped to a question that had a yes or no answer. It also came back with an answer nobody wanted, which is the other thing you buy when you go looking.

Fenella Drozdov
Director, Information Governance
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-149**  
To: Wendell Aduba-Reiss <wendell.aduba-reiss@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Kwabena Ofori-Lund <kwabena.ofori-lund@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Marsden IP Team <ip-team@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: Ceiling-array landscape - the three publications that keep getting confused in that room  
Date: April 09, 2024, 10:15  
References: Email-34  
Body:  
Wendell, Trevor,

Following Monday's meeting, here is the short version of who owns what in the ceiling-array space, written specifically so that nobody has to squint at a publication number again.

Three documents came up on Monday and at least two of them were mixed up in the room, one of them by me.

WO 2012/024513 A1, Norrköping Akustik AB, published February 2012. Room response equalization from sparse measurement points. This is the document that was the X reference against our own MAL-2012-0257 in 2012 and it is still cited against us periodically. Norrköping was acquired in 2019 and the US member now sits with a licensing entity, which is why it turns up in third-party demand letters rather than in competitor products.

WO 2011/024531 A2, Halberstam Acoustics Ltd., published March 2011. Perceptual downmix ordering. Cited against our Chinese application in 2017. Halberstam is a real operating company with real products and has, as far as I can find, never asserted anything against anybody.

WO 2012/025431 A1. This one is ours. Munich series, MAL-2011-0117, adaptive port compensation for vented enclosures. It is not a competitor document, and it should not have been on a competitive landscape slide, which on Monday it was.

Two of those three differ only in the order of two digits. I have stopped trusting myself to read them by eye and now paste every one into a lookup sheet. I recommend the same to everybody, including and especially the people who are certain they do not need to.

On substance: nothing in the landscape reads on the ceiling-array product as currently specified. The nearest is the Norrköping equalization document, and our design does not do sparse measurement at all, so the nearest is not near.

Kwabena Ofori-Lund
Senior Patent Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-150**  
To: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: 中垣内 昌孝 <nakagaito@shinonome-seimitsu.co.jp>  
Cc: 榛葉 千秋 <shinba@hokuto-tokkyo.jp>  
Subject: 特願2013-176482 及びその分割出願に係る共有持分の確認等について（ご通知）  
Date: November 19, 2024, 09:35  
References:  
Body:  
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
知的財産部長 園田 修 様

拝啓 時下ますますご清栄のこととお慶び申し上げます。東雲精密工業株式会社 知的財産室の中垣内でございます。

このたびは、標記出願に係る権利の帰属につきまして、書面にてご通知申し上げます。

一、貴社と弊社は、2012年9月3日付「開発委託基本契約書」を締結しております。同契約第6条第2項は、両社の従業員が共同でなした発明は両社の共有とし、持分は寄与度に応じて協議により定める旨を規定しております。

二、貴社が2013年8月26日に出願された特願2013-176482「小型ボイスコイル型振動子の防振支持構造」および同出願に係る各分割出願につきまして、弊社技術部 山辺 直人 が共同発明者であると考えております。根拠は、2012年10月30日および同年12月4日開催の設計検討会議事録（弊社控え、貴社ご担当者の押印あり）ならびに弊社図面 SK-12-4471 Rev.C（2012年11月19日作成）でございます。特に、特許第6473296号の分割に係る二重支持構造、すなわち板ばねと防振ゴムを併用し外周リブで拘束する構成は、上記図面において弊社が初めて提示したものでございます。

三、共有に係る特許を受ける権利について共有者全員によらない出願は特許法第38条に違反し、特許法第123条第1項第2号の無効理由に該当いたします。同無効理由には期間の制限がございません。

四、つきましては、（１）共有持分のご確認、（２）係属中の分割出願（特願2024-072905）への出願人の追加および発明者の追加、（３）登録済の特許第6473296号および特許第7491235号に係る持分の移転登録につき、ご協議をお願い申し上げます。

2024年12月20日までに書面にてご回答賜りますようお願い申し上げます。訴訟等を前提とするものではございません。

敬具

中垣内 昌孝
知的財産室長
東雲精密工業株式会社

**Email-151**  
To: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>  
Subject: 【要調査・12月13日まで】東雲精密工業様からの共有持分確認要求について（MAL-2013-0771）  
Date: November 21, 2024, 16:15  
References: Email-150, Email-101  
Body:  
黒岩様

標記の件、19日付で東雲精密工業株式会社（長野・精密加工、Veritan の当事者ID は PTY-062240）より書面での通知を受領しました。回答期限は12月20日です。実質的な回答は当方の調査が終わるまで行いませんので、先方への連絡は私を通してください。

はじめに一点、社内で必ず確認していただきたいことがあります。本件の相手方は大阪の東雲電機グループの各社とは資本関係のない別法人です。表記が同一であるため、過去に取引先マスタで取り違えが発生しています。案件登録、書面の綴じ込み、外部照会のいずれも PTY-062240 で行ってください。

調査していただきたいのは次の四点です。

一、2012年9月3日付 開発委託基本契約書の原本と、第6条第2項の文言。当時の担当は既に退職しているため、契約管理台帳から現物を出してください。

二、2012年10月30日および12月4日の設計検討会議事録。先方は当社担当者の押印があると主張しています。当社控えの有無と押印の有無を確認願います。

三、当社側発明者2名の実験ノートのうち、2012年7月から12月まで。二重支持構造、特に外周リブによる拘束の着想がいつ、誰から出たものかを特定してください。2013年の出願時、当社は7月に社内で着想済みという整理で単独出願しております。その前提が崩れるかどうかが本件の分かれ目です。

四、係属中の分割出願（特願2024-072905）の審査状況と、手続補正が可能な期間。

皆川先生には第38条および第123条第1項第2号の論点整理をお願いしております。12月13日に打合せを設定しますので、それまでに一、二、三をお願いします。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-152**  
To: Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>, Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2013-0771 - the Seimitsu claim is good - recommend we concede and put the pending divisional into joint names  
Date: December 11, 2024, 11:50  
References: Email-151  
Body:  
Marisol,

Yokohama has finished the file review and I have read the underlying documents. My recommendation is that we concede. I would rather write that sentence now than defend a position I do not believe in for two years.

The facts. The 2012 development consignment agreement with Shinonome Seimitsu Kogyo, Article 6.2, makes an invention made by personnel of both companies jointly owned with shares fixed by discussion according to contribution. The minutes of October 30 and December 4, 2012 exist in our own file, they carry our engineer's seal, and drawing SK-12-4471 Rev. C predates anything of ours showing the outer-rib constraint. Our two inventors' notebooks show the single-piece geometry in July 2012, which is what the 2013 filing decision relied on, but the double-support arrangement with the rib constraint first appears in our notebooks on December 11, 2012, a week after their drawing. Our own inventors say, without being pushed, that Yamabe proposed it.

Why this cannot be left. The double-support arrangement is exactly what the 2019 divisional claimed and what registered as 特許第7491235号 in May 2024. A joint application filed without a co-owner offends Article 38 and is an invalidity ground under Article 123(1)(ii), with no time limit. That is not a risk that ages out.

What I propose. Put the pending divisional 特願2024-072905 into joint names by a change of applicant with a partial assignment, and add Yamabe as an inventor by amendment while it is still pending. Record a partial transfer on the two registered patents. Offer shares of three quarters to us, one quarter to them, which is defensible on contribution and which their counsel has hinted is acceptable. Get blanket consent under Article 73(3) for our existing licensees in the same instrument, because otherwise every future license needs their signature.

One housekeeping point. This company is party PTY-062240 and is unrelated to the Osaka group. Please make sure nobody files this correspondence anywhere near the group's records.

Trevor Ashimolowo
Senior IP Counsel
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-153**  
To: 中垣内 昌孝 <nakagaito@shinonome-seimitsu.co.jp>  
From: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: 皆川 悠子 <minagawa@yodo-naniwa-ip.jp>, Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: 共有持分の確認および出願人名義変更等に関する当社回答（特願2024-072905 ほか）  
Date: January 23, 2025, 09:15  
References: Email-152  
Body:  
東雲精密工業株式会社
知的財産室長 中垣内 昌孝 様

平素より大変お世話になっております。マースデン・ジャパン知的財産部の黒岩でございます。2024年11月19日付ご通知に対する当社の回答を申し上げます。

一、当社は社内調査の結果、貴社 山辺 直人 氏が、二重支持構造（板ばねと防振ゴムを併用し外周リブで拘束する構成）に係る発明の共同発明者であることを確認いたしました。貴社のご指摘を受け入れ、2012年9月3日付 開発委託基本契約書 第6条第2項に基づく共有であることを認めます。

二、持分につきましては、寄与度に鑑み、当社4分の3、貴社4分の1とすることをご提案いたします。同項の「協議により定める」に基づくものでございます。

三、手続につきましては、下記のとおり進めさせていただきたく存じます。
（１）係属中の特願2024-072905について、持分一部譲渡による出願人名義変更届を2月中に提出。あわせて願書の発明者に山辺氏を追加する手続補正書を提出いたします。
（２）登録済の特許第6473296号および特許第7491235号について、持分の移転登録申請を同時期に行います。譲渡証書の案文は本日別送いたします。
（３）年金は持分にかかわらず当社が全額負担いたします。

四、実施許諾につきましては、特許法第73条第3項により共有者の同意を要します。当社の既存ライセンシー3社に対する実施許諾について包括的なご同意を賜りたく、その対価として、当該特許に係る受領実施料の4分の1を貴社にお支払いする内容の覚書案を2月7日までにお送りいたします。将来の新規許諾は都度ご協議とさせてください。

ご確認のうえ、ご不明点をご指摘いただけますと幸いです。

黒岩 千尋
特許エンジニア
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社

**Email-154**  
To: Marsden IP Docket <ip-docket@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Yolande Grimbeek <yolande.grimbeek@marsdenlabs.com>, Marisol Etxeberria <marisol.etxeberria@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: BR PI 1005472-3 lapsed - restoration petition filed, and the cause is eight years old  
Date: February 11, 2025, 15:25  
References: Email-73  
Body:  
The Brazilian patent on MAL-2009-0388 lapsed for non-payment of the fourteenth annuity. The restoration petition went in on February 7 and I expect it to be granted, but the sequence is worth reading because the cause is not what anyone would guess.

Sequence. The annuity fell due on the anniversary in June 2024. INPI allows payment within six months of the due date with a surcharge; that window closed in December. Extinguishment was published in RPI 2812 on December 17, 2024. Under Article 87 of the Industrial Property Law a patent extinguished for non-payment may be restored on request within three months of that publication, so our outer date was March 17, 2025. The petition was filed February 7 with the annuity, the surcharge and the restoration fee, BRL 4,918 in total. Our agent in São Paulo confirms it is docketed.

Cause. The Renewal Responsibility field on this matter reads "Provider - standing instruction." It has read that since February 19, 2016, when it was derived from the old system's billing-party code. This matter was one of the ninety-four I wrote about in April 2016. It was not in the eleven we cleared inside ninety days, because its next due date at the time was more than five years out and we prioritized by proximity. It then sat. The provider had no instruction and never had one. Our agent assumed the provider held it because our own record said so.

Remediation. All ninety-four have now been re-verified against the local agent's diary rather than against Veritan. Three others were in the same posture; all three are current and I have moved them to agent-instructed with written confirmation on each file. From this year, IP Operations reconciles the whole renewal book against the agents' records every January, and the Veritan value is treated as a prompt rather than as an instruction.

No other loss. This one was recoverable. The next one might not be.

Bettina Crozier
Director, IP Operations
Marsden Audio Laboratories, Inc.

**Email-155**  
To: Chihiro Kuroiwa <c.kuroiwa@marsdenlabs.com>  
From: Osamu Sonoda <o.sonoda@marsdenlabs.com>  
Cc: Bettina Crozier <bettina.crozier@marsdenlabs.com>, Trevor Ashimolowo <trevor.ashimolowo@marsdenlabs.com>  
Subject: MAL-2013-0771 手続完了のご報告と社内への申し送り（東雲精密工業様との共有）  
Date: March 19, 2025, 15:30  
References: Email-153  
Body:  
黒岩様、関係各位

東雲精密工業株式会社（PTY-062240）との共有持分の件、一連の手続が完了しましたのでご報告します。

一、覚書「共有持分の確認および実施許諾に関する覚書」を3月7日に締結しました。持分は当社4分の3、同社4分の1。既存ライセンシー3社への実施許諾について包括同意を得ており、受領実施料の4分の1を四半期ごとに支払います。新規許諾は都度協議です。

二、特願2024-072905の出願人名義変更届は2月21日に受理されました。発明者追加の手続補正書も同日受理。特許第6473296号および特許第7491235号の持分移転登録は3月12日付で完了しています。

三、Veritan の Ownership Status を Joint に変更し、共有者、持分、および第73条第3項の同意要件を備考欄に記載しました。年金は引き続き当社が全額負担しますので、更新指示の宛先は変更ありません。

四、申し送りとして二点、記録に残します。

第一に、開発委託契約に発明の帰属条項がある場合は、出願前に相手方の関与の有無を書面で確認すること。今回、当社は2013年に社内の着想時期のみを根拠として単独出願を決定しており、相手方の設計検討会資料を確認しておりませんでした。手元の資料だけで帰属を判断したことが原因です。

第二に、社名の表記が同一の別法人が複数存在する場合は、必ず当事者IDで特定すること。本件でも当初、案件登録の照会が大阪の同名各社に向けられかけました。クロージャー様の2022年の通知のとおりです。

以上、ご確認ください。

園田 修
知的財産部長
マースデン・ジャパン株式会社
